Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) generate chain-of-thought (CoT) traces before producing final outputs, introducing a dynamic internal state that may complicate control mechanisms such as refusal. Unlike instruction-tuned LLMs, where refusal is mediated by a single directional subspace, refusal in large reasoning models (LRMs) additionally depends on the CoT. In DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B, activation steering reverses refusal in only 39% of cases when the CoT is kept fixed, but removing the CoT entirely increases this to 70%, indicating that the CoT actively reinforces refusal. In a two-stage intervention where the model regenerates its CoT under activation steering, refusal is reversed in 94% of cases, while the resulting CoT alone retains 48% of this effect even after steering is removed. This suggests that the CoT can carry and reconstruct the compliance signal independently. These findings indicate that refusal in LRMs is jointly encoded in residual stream activations and CoT. This joint activation makes LRM more robust against activation-level interventions alone, but exposes CoT to a possible alternative surface attack.

</details>


### [114] [The Attribution Blind Spot: Detecting When Language Models Rely on Memory Rather Than Retrieved Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26778)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Yunzhao Wei,Bo Yang,Chen Ye,Gaolei Li,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对RAG系统难以区分生成内容是来自检索上下文还是预训练知识的问题（归因盲点），研究提出了一种基于内部表征分析的“计算现实监测”（CRM）方法，通过检测模型内部激活状态的分歧，实现了对模型证据使用行为的有效溯源预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG评估方法存在逻辑缺陷：当检索到的内容涵盖在模型的预训练数据中时，输出端的“事实一致性”无法区分模型是“基于上下文生成”还是“基于参数记忆生成”，这给高风险场景下的RAG部署带来了重大隐患。作者将此问题定义为“归因盲点”（Attribution Blind Spot）。

Method: 提出了计算现实监测（Computational Reality Monitoring, CRM）方法：通过对比模型在有无检索上下文情况下的内部激活轨迹（Internal Representation Trajectories），利用表示分歧（Representational Divergence）来探测模型是否依赖于外部上下文，而非仅仅依赖其预训练的参数记忆。

Result: 实验覆盖了9种不同规模和架构的模型，结果显示：1. 归因盲点确实存在且可测量；2. 有无上下文的内部表征存在显著的分歧模式；3. 该分歧在特定的层级结构中高度集中；4. 该方法在多个任务和数据集上具有通用性，但在领域高度重合的基准测试中会收敛。

Conclusion: 研究表明，由于模型预训练记忆的干扰，仅通过输出端验证无法判断RAG系统是否真正利用了检索到的上下文（即存在“归因盲点”）。作者提出的计算现实监测（CRM）通过分析模型内部表征的解离，成功探测到了预训练暴露所留下的特征信号，为实现基于证据溯源的可靠RAG生成提供了关键技术基础。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation promises to ground language model outputs in external evidence, yet the field has no reliable way to verify whether retrieved context actually governs generation -- a prerequisite for any high-stakes deployment. The standard assumption, that context-consistent output implies context-governed output, breaks when the retrieved document overlaps with the model's pretraining data: the model can produce faithful-looking text entirely from parametric memory, and both pathways yield indistinguishable output. We name this failure the attribution blind spot and introduce Computational Reality Monitoring (CRM) to address it. CRM operationalizes a principle adapted from cognitive science's reality monitoring framework: comparing internal representations with and without context reveals membership-conditioned representational divergence that output-level monitors systematically miss. CRM does not certify which source an individual generation used; it detects whether pretraining exposure leaves a measurable internal trajectory signature, establishing a necessary substrate for source attribution. Across nine model variants spanning three families, this divergence concentrates in architecture-specific layer patterns, receives converging support from block-level noise intervention, and generalizes across tasks and datasets while collapsing on domain-confounded benchmarks. The attribution blind spot is measurable and partially addressable: internal representations carry a diagnostic signal invisible at the output level, establishing a foundation for systems whose internal awareness of evidence provenance governs their external behavior.

</details>


### [115] [LiveK12Bench: Have Large Multimodal Models Truly Conquered High School-level Examinations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26781)
*Xiaohan Wang,Mingze Yin,Yilin Zhao,Gang Liu,Dian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LiveK12Bench，这是一个动态的、跨学科的K-12多模态模型基准测试，通过模拟真实考试环境的严谨性和实时性，揭示了当前多模态大模型在教育应用中存在的性能瓶颈与脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试大多为静态数据，存在数据污染和模态/学科限制，无法真实反映多模态大模型在实际K-12教育场景中的推理能力。

Method: 1. 构建了LiveK12Bench数据集：包含2K+道跨学科（数理化生）真题，并具备动态更新能力； 2. 自动化管道：通过自动摄取和解析最新试卷来规避数据泄露； 3. “模拟考试”评估机制：要求模型端到端地自主完成考试，强调推理路径的准确性和效率。

Result: 通过对12款主流LMMs的测试，发现模型在模拟考试约束下表现出显著的性能退化（如某先进模型得分从79降至53），揭示了模型处理复杂视觉布局的能力缺陷和推理过程中的鲁棒性问题。

Conclusion: 现有的先进多模态大模型（LMMs）在处理真实的、复杂的K-12考试环境时表现不佳，存在明显的“能力幻觉”。模型在面对需要严谨推理过程和高效率的真实考试场景时，性能会大幅下降，对复杂的视觉布局非常敏感，距离真正的教育应用仍有巨大差距。

Abstract: Advanced Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in K-12 reasoning tasks, exhibiting great promise as intelligent tutors. Realizing this potential requires models to navigate real-world examinations effectively, yet most existing benchmarks fail to capture the complexity of authentic testing environments. Specifically, most datasets are static, prone to data contamination, and are often confined to restricted modalities, disciplines, and evaluation criteria. To address these issues, we introduce LiveK12Bench, a dynamic, holistic, multi-disciplinary benchmark designed to evaluate the reasoning abilities of LMMs in realistic examination scenarios. LiveK12Bench comprises 2K+ verified questions spanning Mathematics, Physics, Chemistry, and Biology, sourced from the latest real-world exam papers and designed to grow over time. Our framework features several core innovations: 1) featuring an automated pipeline that continuously ingests and parses the latest examination papers to mitigate data leakage; and 2) proposing a novel `Mock Exam' evaluation scheme, which assesses the ability to complete end-to-end exams autonomously with accurate and efficient reasoning paths. Extensive experiments on 12 LMMs reveal that advanced models suffer substantial performance degradation under exam-realistic constraints: GPT-5's score drops from 79 to 53 (out of 100) when process rigor and efficiency are jointly evaluated. Our findings expose critical vulnerabilities, such as sensitivity to complex visual layouts, highlighting the gap between idealized reasoning capabilities and true educational readiness. Both code and dataset are publicly available.

</details>


### [116] [Composition Collapse: Stable Factual Knowledge Does Not Imply Compositional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26789)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Yunzhao Wei,Jie Chen,Hongzhi Wang,Xuyang Teng,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM多跳推理评估中“聚合指标”的误导性，提出了拆解评估方法，发现许多多跳推理表现差异源于无法组合已知事实（组合崩溃），而非缺乏原子知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出目前的聚合指标无法区分模型是“因为掌握了原子知识”还是“因为掌握了组合逻辑”而回答正确，导致“组合崩溃（composition collapse）”现象被掩盖。

Method: 提出了“双门协议（double-gate protocol）”，将后期训练（post-training）收益分解为三个独立维度：原子稳定性（atomic stability）、残差组合（residual composition）和临界深度（critical depth）。同时利用诊断探针分析生成时的计算约束。

Result: 发现不同后期训练配方在原子知识相似的情况下，多跳推理表现差异高达40个百分点；证实了聚合指标会掩盖组合能力的本质变化；揭示了部分组合失败其实是生成时计算限制造成的。

Conclusion: 现有的聚合基准测试掩盖了模型在多跳推理能力上的差异。研究强调，多跳推理能力的提升应通过受原子知识门控控制的指标来衡量，并指出组合失败很大程度上源于推理时的计算限制，而非模型本质能力的缺失。

Abstract: Post-training is routinely evaluated through aggregate benchmark scores that treat multi-hop reasoning as a single capability -- as if a model that answers more questions correctly must be better at assembling facts. We show that this assumption can be misleading: recipes with statistically indistinguishable atomic knowledge produce composition behaviour separated by over 40 percentage points, a phenomenon we call composition collapse: the systematic failure to assemble stably-known facts into chains, invisible to aggregate metrics. We introduce a double-gate protocol that changes the estimand from an aggregate compositionality gap to residual composition failure conditioned on stable atomic access, decomposing post-training gains into three independent channels: atomic stability, residual composition, and critical depth. On a benchmark of temporal factual chains spanning depths 2--11 across four post-training recipes, this decomposition reveals that post-training objectives shift composition capability in directions that aggregate metrics mask, and suggests that claims about multi-hop reasoning improvement should be accompanied by atomic-gate-controlled composition metrics. Diagnostic probes further show that a substantial share of measured composition failure reflects generation-time computation constraints rather than permanent inability to compose.

</details>


### [117] [What Makes Chain-of-Thought Work at Probe Time? Local Co-occurrence Rather Than Global Derivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26795)
*Xiang Wang,Wei Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过扰动实验发现，思维链（CoT）带来的准确率提升主要归功于局部的“局部共现激活”（LCA），而非复杂的逻辑推理能力。只要保持短程的词汇邻近性，即便打乱逻辑顺序，模型也能获得绝大部分CoT增强效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管思维链提升了模型准确率，但业界对于其背后的核心机制（即理性文本的哪些属性在起作用）仍缺乏深入理解。

Method: 采用“探测式”（probe-time）研究方法，即固定理性陈述（rationale）文本，通过扰动文本（如打乱词序、截断窗口）来观察大模型在特定任务上答案的变化，并排除直接复制答案或语法结构的影响。

Result: 研究发现：1. 即使是词序完全打乱的文本也能带来显著提升（体现了词汇激活作用）；2. 模型表现主要依赖于仅包含2-3个词元的短程连续窗口，而非完整的逻辑顺序或精细的语法结构。

Conclusion: 思维链（CoT）推理性能的提升主要源于局部共现激活（LCA），即词汇激活和短程词元邻近性，而非句子层面的逻辑推导。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting reliably improves language-model accuracy, but which properties of a rationale text drive the improvement is poorly understood. Prior work has largely studied generation-time behavior. We instead ask a probe-time question: given a fixed rationale in context, what in that text changes the answer? We identify two complementary sources of the gain. First, even a globally word-shuffled rationale substantially outperforms the no-rationale baseline, indicating a strong lexical activation effect. More importantly, the additional gain from structured text appears to arise less from sentence-level logical ordering and more from short-range token adjacency. Preserving contiguous windows of just $n^\star{=}2$--$3$ tokens recovers most of the remaining gain toward full CoT performance. Supporting experiments rule out copying of explicit answer declarations or answer values, as well as full grammatical realization, as primary drivers. Further generalization experiments show that the qualitative pattern remains stable across multiple model families, parameter scales, and datasets. These results support a local co-occurrence activation (LCA) account of probe-time CoT, in which the observed gains appear to arise primarily from lexical activation and short-range token co-occurrence rather than sentence-level logical derivation.

</details>


### [118] [Helicase: Uncertainty-Guided Supply Chain Knowledge Graph Construction with Autonomous Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26835)
*Yunbo Long,Haolang Zhao,Ge Zheng,Alexandra Brintrup*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Helicase 的多智能体 LLM 系统，用于处理需要多跳推理和高可靠性的供应链知识图谱构建任务，通过创新的三层不确定性评估机制实现了高质量的溯源推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的多智能体系统难以处理需要多跳推理且信息分散的复杂供应链任务，且缺乏对推理过程和结论的置信度评估。

Method: 提出了 Helicase 系统，采用多智能体协作架构，通过分解查询、迭代验证循环以及增量构建特定于查询的供应链知识图谱，并引入了包含动作、轨迹和记忆三层的不确定性评估框架。此外，构建了包含 80 个供应链查询的 SCQA 基准测试。

Result: Helicase 能够自主构建知识图谱并进行结构化推理，通过其不确定性监测机制为所检索信息和推理结果提供校准后的置信度，并在 SCQA 基准上验证了其处理复杂推理任务的能力。

Conclusion: Helicase 通过结合多智能体协作与三层不确定性评估机制，为解决复杂、非结构化的供应链推理任务提供了有效的自动化解决方案，显著提升了决策的可信度。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems have been widely adopted for knowledge retrieval and report generation, synthesizing known information through web search and textual reasoning. However, many critical information tasks in supply chains are not simple one-shot queries: they are structural inference problems requiring multi-hop reasoning across complex, fragmented web resources. Questions such as \textit{``Which Tesla components use lithium from Australian mines?''} have no answer in any single document; answers must be computationally synthesized through the autonomous construction and analysis of dynamic knowledge graphs assembled from fragmented, heterogeneous sources. Moreover, such discovery processes must be uncertainty-aware: decisions depend not only on answers but on calibrated confidence in their reliability, traceable to source quality and reasoning consistency. To address this capability gap, we propose \textit{Helicase}, an autonomous multi-agent LLM system for uncertainty-guided supply chain knowledge graph construction. \textit{Helicase} decomposes high-level supply-chain queries into executable investigation plans, coordinates specialized web-search, reasoning, and coding agents through iterative verification loops, and incrementally constructs query-specific supply chain knowledge graphs with per-fact uncertainty annotations. Its three-layer uncertainty framework tracks uncertainty at the action, trajectory, and memory layers, enabling both structural inference and calibrated confidence assessment. To evaluate autonomous reasoning across the full complexity spectrum, we introduce SCQA (Supply Chain Query Assessment), a benchmark of 80 supply chain queries organized into four quadrants spanning single-hop to multi-hop inference under both high and low data visibility.

</details>


### [119] [Multi-Stakeholder LLM Alignment: Decomposing Estimation from Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26878)
*Lulu Zheng,Wenjin Yang,Xiangwen Zhang,Rong Yin,Yulan Hu,Zheng Pan,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对多利益相关者任务中 LLM 裁判因隐含权重不稳导致评分波动的问题，本文提出了 DecompR 方法，通过预设权重与独立评估解耦，提升了评价的一致性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 裁判在处理多利益相关者冲突需求时，将效用估计与聚合混为一谈，导致隐含权重不稳定，进而产生“权重噪声”（weighting noise），且该问题随利益相关者数量增加而恶化。

Method: 提出 DecompR 框架：1. 基于查询结构预设反事实校准的权重（固定权重）；2. 独立估计各角色的效用（解耦评估与聚合过程），从而避免候选内容对权重的影响。

Result: 经实证和理论证明，权重噪声会导致分数大幅波动；DecompR 方法能有效抑制这些波动，显著优于传统的聚合方案。

Conclusion: 通过将效用评估与效用聚合解耦，DecompR 能够有效消除权重漂移（weight drift）并降低评估噪声，从而在多利益相关者任务中提供更稳定、一致的评分。

Abstract: Multi-stakeholder tasks require one output to satisfy users with conflicting preferences. Holistic LLM judges conflate utility estimation and utility aggregation, yielding unstable implicit weights. We show empirically and theoretically that this aggregation-specific \emph{weighting noise} can create large score shifts when stakeholder satisfaction is dispersed; in our experiments, these weight-induced shifts also increase with stakeholder count. We propose \textsc{DecompR}: counterfactual-calibrated weights are fixed from query structure before candidate scoring, while per-role utilities are estimated independently, removing candidate-dependent weight drift and reducing estimation noise.

</details>


### [120] [Neuro-Symbolic Verification of LLM Outputs for Data-Sensitive Domains (extended preprint)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26942)
*Paul Sigloch,Christoph Benzmüller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合形式化符号逻辑与神经语义分析的混合验证架构，专门用于解决LLM在高风险领域中的幻觉与可靠性问题，显著提升了输出的准确性与工作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在医疗等高风险领域部署时，面临幻觉、不一致性及隐私泄露等可靠性挑战，现有的直接提示（prompt-based）自验证方法因继承了模型的分布偏差而效果受限。

Method: 提出了一种混合验证架构，结合了形式化符号方法（用于输入验证和结构化需求保障）与神经语义分析（基于嵌入的相似度，用于输出幻觉检测）。该系统采用并行、基于角色的流水线（Actor-based pipeline）实现，并结合了类型感知的验证方法。

Result: 通过在医疗设备损毁评估系统（HAIMEDA）上的验证，结构化实体幻觉检测率超过83%，语义伪造幻觉检测率达72%，且报告生成效率提升30%。

Conclusion: 神经符号架构为高风险、数据敏感领域中大型语言模型（LLM）的部署提供了原则性的安全保障，有效降低了幻觉风险并提升了业务效率。

Abstract: LLMs deployed in high-stakes domains face fundamental reliability challenges: hallucinations, inconsistencies, and privacy vulnerabilities introduce unacceptable risks where errors carry legal, financial, or safety consequences. This paper presents a hybrid verification architecture combining formal symbolic methods with neural semantic analysis to provide complementary guarantees for LLM-generated content. This architecture employs logical reasoning for input verification, leveraging completeness properties to provide decidable guarantees on structured requirements. For output validation, embedding-based semantic similarity detects contextual hallucinations where formal methods lack expressiveness. This separation is realized in a parallel, actor-based pipeline, addressing limitations of prompt-based self-verification approaches, which inherit the distributional biases that produce hallucinations. The proposed architecture and type-aware verification method are validated with HAIMEDA, a real-world medical device damage assessment reporting system developed through Action Design Research. Evaluation shows hallucination detection rates of over 83% for structured entities and 72% for semantic fabrications, with a 30% reduction in report creation time, demonstrating that neuro-symbolic architectures can provide principled safeguards for LLM deployment in data-sensitive domains.

</details>


### [121] [LELA: An End-to-end LLM-based Entity Linking Framework with Zero-shot Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26956)
*Samy Haffoudhi,Nikola Dobričić,Fabian Suchanek,Nils Holzenberger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将 LELA（一种基于大模型的领域无关实体消歧方法）扩展为包含零样本 NER 的端到端 Python 库，旨在解决现有实体链接工具难以跨领域应用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实体链接方法通常受限于特定的知识库和应用领域，难以满足广泛的现实世界应用需求；需要一个更加通用、模块化且易于部署的系统。

Method: 在 LELA（一种模块化、领域无关的基于大语言模型(LLM)的实体消歧方法）的基础上，集成了零样本命名实体识别(NER)功能，构建了一个完整的端到端实体链接流水线，并将其开发为可直接使用的 Python 库。

Result: 通过实验验证了 LELA 在不同实体链接任务设置下的有效性和鲁棒性，并提供了一个可供演示交互的系统。

Conclusion: 通过将 LELA 封装为实用的 Python 库并整合零样本 NER，作者提供了一个可广泛应用于现实场景的通用、端到端实体链接解决方案。实验证明了该方法在多样化设置下的高性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Entity linking is a key component of many downstream NLP systems, yet existing approaches are often tied to the specific target knowledge bases and domains, limiting their real world application. In this paper, we extend LELA, a modular and domain-agnostic LLM-based entity disambiguation method, into a practical Python library that integrates zero-shot Named Entity Recognition (NER) -thereby providing a complete end-toend pipeline for entity-linking in real-world usage. We provide experimental results validating LELA's performance and robustness across diverse entity linking settings. In our demo, users can play with the system on their own input texts.

</details>


### [122] [Generating Robust Portfolios of Optimization Models using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27013)
*Eleni Straitouri,Cheol Woo Kim,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用大模型同时担任生成者和评估者的框架，通过生成多个数学优化模型组合，解决了大模型在建模任务中不可靠的痛点，并提供了质量保障的理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学优化建模通常需要稀缺的双重专业知识（领域知识与优化算法知识）。虽然LLM能够通过自然语言生成模型，但单一模型生成的方案往往不可靠且存在高风险，缺乏有效的质量保障机制。

Method: 提出了一种结合LLM作为“随机生成器”和“推理评估器”的统一框架。该方法生成一系列数学优化模型组合（Portfolio），利用两者在评估和生成上的互补性，并通过理论证明确保只要生成器或评估器中有一方符合人类偏好，组合中就必然包含高质量的候选模型。

Result: 理论上证明了该方法在一定条件下能够保证候选集合中涵盖高质量解；实验结果表明，该方法在多种数学优化建模任务中表现优异，优于传统的单模型生成方案。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的多模型组合生成框架能够有效缓解LLM在数学建模任务中的不可靠性，通过结合生成器与评估器的双重角色，为决策者提供了高置信度的候选方案，显著降低了开发门槛。

Abstract: Mathematical optimization is a powerful tool for structured decision-making across domains such as resource allocation and planning. Formulating optimization models faithful to reality, though, remains a significant bottleneck as it typically demands both domain expertise and optimization knowledge that are often scarce. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) promise to bridge this gap, enabling the generation of candidate optimization models from natural language descriptions. However, there is no guarantee that any single LLM-generated model is reliable, and existing approaches that output only one model are therefore risky. In this work, we propose a novel algorithm that generates a portfolio of optimization models, designed to be robust to the limitations of LLMs. Our method exploits the observation that a single LLM can play two distinct roles $\unicode{x2014}$ as a stochastic generator and as a reasoning evaluator $\unicode{x2014}$ and proposes a unified framework that leverages both capabilities in a complementary manner. We provide theoretical guarantees showing that, as long as either the generator or the evaluator is well-aligned with human preferences, the portfolio is guaranteed to contain high-quality candidates, enabling a principled human-in-the-loop process in which a decision-maker can review multiple candidates before committing to one. We further validate our approach empirically, demonstrating strong performance across a range of optimization modeling tasks.

</details>


### [123] [Boosting Knowledge Graph Foundation Models via Enhanced Negative Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27023)
*Yinan Liu,Wenjin Xu,Zhiyuan Zha,Xiaochun Yang,Bin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对传统KG基础模型负采样质量不高的问题，本文提出了一种基于动态困难样本生成的自适应采样策略（KMAS），有效增强了模型在零样本场景下的补全能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KG基础模型（KGFMs）在训练时多采用随机负采样，导致负样本质量低下，模型训练监督信号较弱，限制了其在零样本知识图谱补全任务中的效果。

Method: 提出了一种名为KMAS的自适应负采样方法。该方法利用KGFM关系编码器生成的最新嵌入来构建“困难负样本”（Hard Negative Triples），并采用了动态调整比例的策略：在预热阶段后，负样本比例呈先线性增加后线性减少的规律，以适应模型在训练不同阶段的学习需求。

Result: 在44个数据集上的广泛实验表明，KMAS方法显著提升了多个SOTA KGFMs的性能，同时保持了较低的时间和内存开销。

Conclusion: 本文提出的自适应负采样方法（KMAS）能够有效增强各类SOTA知识图谱基础模型，且无需额外的计算开销，是提升知识图谱补全任务中负采样质量的高效方案。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) have become the core backbone of numerous downstream tasks such as question answering and recommender systems. However, despite all this, KGs are often very incomplete. To perform zero-shot knowledge graph completion in unseen KGs, which have different relational vocabularies from those used for pre-training, KG foundation models (KGFMs) receive a wide range of attention. Existing KGFMs often perform training using random negative triples, which are constructed by replacing the head or tail entity of a positive triple with a random entity. However, these negative triples are often constructed with limited quality, providing weak supervision for KGFM training. In this paper, we propose a simple yet effective adaptive negative sampling approach, KMAS, to enhance existing KGFMs. KMAS constructs hard negative triples through the updated relation embeddings generated from the existing KGFM's relation encoder. To further adaptively align with the evolving capability of the KGFM during the training process, KMAS adjusts the ratio of hard negative triples dynamically throughout the whole training process: after a warmup phrase, it increases the ratio linearly and then decreases linearly. Extensive experiments are conducted over 44 data sets. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed negative sampling method can enhance many SOTA KGFMs without requiring excessive additional time or memory consumption.

</details>


### [124] [BatteryMFormer: Multi-level Learning for Battery Degradation Trajectory Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27044)
*Ruifeng Tan,Jintao Dong,Weixiang Hong,Jia Li,Jiaqiang Huang,Tong-Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BatteryMFormer模型，通过引入多层级Transformer架构，有效结合了老化先验、退化原型记忆机制及SOC局部特征分析，显著提高了锂电池早期全寿命周期健康状态（SOH）的预测准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的早期电池退化轨迹预测（BDTF）方法未能充分利用电池退化数据中存在的“老化条件共享”与“跨电池轨迹模式共享”的层级结构，同时也忽略了退化相关特征往往集中在特定的荷电状态（SOC）区间内的局部性。

Method: 提出了一种名为BatteryMFormer的多层级Transformer模型。其核心模块包括：1.  aging-condition-aware decoder，通过融入老化条件先验来辅助解码；2. meta degradation pattern memory，利用元学习记忆模块存储并提取退化原型以引导长期预测；3. dual-view encoder，通过双视角结构联合捕捉电压-电流序列中的时间动态特性和SOC局部变化。

Result: 在四个电池数据集上的实验表明，BatteryMFormer在预测性能上持续且显著地优于现有的前沿（SOTA）基准模型。

Conclusion: 文章提出的BatteryMFormer是针对现有电池健康状态轨迹预测方法的有效改进，通过显式建模电池退化的层级结构和局部特征，实现了更准确的长期预测，为电池全生命周期管理提供了可靠的预测工具。

Abstract: Early battery degradation trajectory forecasting (BDTF), which predicts the full-life state-of-health trajectory from early operational data, is critical for battery optimization, manufacturing, and deployment. Battery degradation data exhibit two key characteristics. First, degradation data present a multi-level structure, including regularities shared within aging conditions and trajectory patterns shared across batteries. Second, degradation-related variations in voltage-current profiles are often localized to specific state-of-charge (SOC) intervals. Existing approaches often fail to explicitly model these characteristics. To bridge this gap, we propose BatteryMFormer, a multi-level Transformer for early BDTF. BatteryMFormer integrates (1) an aging-condition-aware decoder that injects aging-condition priors via aging-condition-informed queries and aging-condition-aware attention, (2) a meta degradation pattern memory that learns and retrieves trajectory prototypes to guide long-horizon forecasting, and (3) a dual-view encoder that jointly captures temporal dynamics and SOC-localized variations from voltage and current time series. Extensive experiments on four battery domains show that BatteryMFormer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, marking a significant step toward reliable BDTF. Our code is available at https://github.com/Ruifeng-Tan/BatteryMFormer.

</details>


### [125] [Traceable Knowledge Graph Reasoning Enables LLM-Assisted Decision Support for Industrial VOCs in the Steel Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27071)
*Changqing Su,Yu Ding,Zuhong Lin,Hongyu Liu,Xi He,Zheng Zeng,Liqing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对钢铁行业 VOCs 治理知识碎片化及大模型专业幻觉问题，研究提出了 Chat-ISV 多智能体问答系统，通过整合知识图谱与工程文献，实现了高可靠性、可溯源的工业污染管控决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钢铁行业 VOCs 治理知识散落在非结构化文献中，整合困难；通用大模型在回答此类低频工业专业问题时存在高幻觉风险。

Method: 开发了一个知识图谱（KG）增强的多智能体问答系统。具体包括：构建包含 27180 个节点和 81779 条语义边的 Neo4j 知识图谱；采用提示约束提取、以块为中心的拓扑优化（降低孤立节点）、多智能体路由、源溯源检索、本地文献检索、开放域知识访问以及交互式子图可视化等技术。

Result: Benchmark 测试与 400 份专家盲评显示：拓扑优化使孤立节点从 57% 降至 4.08%；系统准确率达到 96.93%，召回率 72.63%，F1-score 为 0.830，专家评价均分 1.69/2.00。

Conclusion: Chat-ISV 是一个可扩展的环境信息学范式，通过知识图谱增强 LLM，为专业工业领域（如钢铁行业 VOCs 治理）提供可靠的决策支持，有效解决了通用大模型在垂直领域的信息碎片化和幻觉问题。

Abstract: Key knowledge for steel-industry volatile organic compounds (VOCs) governance is scattered across unstructured scientific literature, making it difficult to integrate process, pollutant, and control-technology evidence and increasing the risk of hallucination when general large language models (LLMs) answer low-frequency industrial questions. Here we developed Chat-ISV, a knowledge graph (KG) enhanced multi-agent Q&A system that parses a curated steel-industry VOCs literature corpus, constructs a Neo4j KG with 27180 nodes and 81779 semantic edges, and combines prompt-constrained extraction, chunk-centered topology optimization, multi-agent routing, source-backtracking retrieval, local literature retrieval, open-domain knowledge access, and interactive subgraph visualization. Benchmark tests and 400 expert blind evaluations showed that topology optimization reduced isolated nodes from 57% to 4.08% and that Chat-ISV achieved high factual reliability, with 96.93% precision, 72.63% recall, an F1-score of 0.830, and a mean score of 1.69/2.00. By converting fragmented environmental-engineering literature into traceable, queryable, and decision-support-oriented knowledge, Chat-ISV establishes a scalable environmental-informatics paradigm for reliable LLM deployment and intelligent pollution-control decision support in specialized industrial domains.

</details>


### [126] [Can Broad Biomedical Knowledge be Contextualized into Scenario-Grounded Propositions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27082)
*Qingyuan Zeng,Ziyang Chen,Pengxiang Cai,Zixin Guan,Anglin Liu,Lang Qin,Xinyao Lai,Jintai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCENE是一个双层多智能体框架，通过迭代式搜索将通用生物医学知识转化为针对特定数据集的可验证情境化假设，有效解决了数据发现中知识映射难与解释性差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用生物医学知识过于宽泛，难以直接映射至具体数据集变量，而数据驱动模式往往缺乏机制解释性，因此需要构建一种连接知识与数据的“知识情境化”桥梁。

Method: 提出双层多智能体框架（Bi-level Multi-agent Framework）：上层智能体负责知识转化与数据集模式映射，制定搜索方向；下层智能体通过多目标优化执行搜索，平衡证据强度与数据支持度；通过双层反馈机制进行迭代细化。

Result: 在临床试验场景中，SCENE成功识别出治疗效益存在差异的子群，性能优于现有基准；在LINCS L1000研究中，成功识别出具有强目标响应匹配度和高阳性率的扰动环境。

Conclusion: SCENE框架通过将通用生物医学知识与特定数据集进行情境化对接，成功生成了可追溯、可验证的假设，显著提升了生物医学发现中知识与数据的关联效率。

Abstract: Biomedical discovery often requires connecting broad biomedical knowledge with specific experimental or clinical data. Background knowledge suggests relevant mechanisms but is usually too general to map directly onto dataset variables, while data-driven patterns can be dataset-specific and hard to interpret mechanistically. We study this missing link as knowledge contextualization: transforming broad biomedical knowledge into evidence-supported, scenario-grounded propositions that domain experts can inspect, replay, and validate. We propose SCENE, a bi-level multi-agent framework that treats knowledge contextualization as iterative search. The upper level converts broad knowledge into search directions and grounds them in the dataset schema. The lower level executes these directions through multi-objective optimization to identify concrete propositions that balance evidential strength and data support. Feedback between the two levels progressively refines the search. We evaluate SCENE in two settings: discovering patient subgroups with heterogeneous treatment benefits in clinical trial scenarios, and identifying context-specific biological responses in LINCS L1000 studies. In clinical trials, SCENE discovers specific, well-supported subgroups and outperforms existing baselines. In L1000 studies, SCENE identifies perturbational contexts with strong target-response matching and high positive rates. These results show that SCENE bridges broad knowledge and scenario-specific evidence, producing traceable, inspectable hypotheses for follow-up validation.

</details>


### [127] [Counteraction-Aware Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for General Capability Recovery with Domain Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27115)
*Tianlei Chen,Jiao Ou,Ziyuan Liu,Ruiming Tang,Jian Liang,Han Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对域内模型训练导致的通用能力退化问题，本文提出 CaMOPD，通过解耦训练阶段和聚焦差异的样本选择，解决了 MOPD 在通用任务提示词缺失时的梯度冲突和信号平滑问题，实现了通用能力恢复与领域专长的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）方法往往假设通用教师的提示词分布已知，但在实际场景中，该分布通常是未知的。在提示词覆盖不完整的情况下，直接使用 MOPD 会导致通用能力恢复不足或领域能力退化，且存在梯度抵消和训练信号平滑的问题。

Method: 提出了 CaMOPD（Counteraction-Aware Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation）算法：1. 采用解耦交替训练（Decoupled Alternating Training）以防止通用强化与领域保持的梯度冲突；2. 采用基于间隙的样本选择（Gap-based sample selection）聚焦于教师-学生模型在 token 概率分布上差异最大的样本，增强修正信号的有效性。

Result: 在角色扮演对话和医疗推理问答任务中，CaMOPD 在通用能力恢复方面优于现有的 MOPD 基线方法，同时保持了良好的领域专用能力。梯度相干性分析证实了该方法能产生更具针对性的一致性校正信号。

Conclusion: CaMOPD 通过解耦训练和基于偏差的样本选择，有效解决了域内专家模型在知识蒸馏过程中“通用能力恢复与领域能力保持”的冲突，在保障领域表现的同时，显著提升了 LLM 在未覆盖通用任务上的性能。

Abstract: Domain specialization can improve LLM behavior in vertical domains, but often weakens the general capabilities inherited from the original model. Recent Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD) pipelines recover model capabilities by supervising student-generated trajectories with teacher feedback, but typically assume teacher-aligned prompt coverage, requiring prompts to match the teachers' training distributions. This assumption is difficult to satisfy when the general teacher is an open-source model whose post-training data are unknown. Instead of attempting to reconstruct this hidden distribution, we study general capability recovery with readily available proxy general prompts. We identify two failure modes of vanilla MOPD in this incomplete-coverage situation: recovery-preservation counteraction from mixing conflicting recovery and preservation gradients, and weak-signal flattening from uniformly averaging samples with unequal correction demand. We propose Counteraction-Aware Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (CaMOPD), which addresses these issues with decoupled alternating training and gap-based sample selection. CaMOPD gives general recovery dedicated updates, periodically reviews domain prompts for preservation, and selects samples with larger averaged token-level teacher-student log-probability gaps to concentrate correction signals. Across role-play dialogue and medical reasoning QA scenarios, CaMOPD performs best in general recovery over baselines while maintaining domain-specific behavior. Gradient coherence analyses further support the intended effect of CaMOPD in producing more coherent correction signals.

</details>


### [128] [Position: AI Safety Requires Effective Controllability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27117)
*Yige Li,Yunhao Feng,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有的AI对齐技术无法完全解决开放环境中智能体的可控性问题；本文提出了“可控性”定义及评估基准 ControlBench，并主张通过引入显式控制平面和运行时干预架构来增强 AI 系统的核心安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的AI安全研究主要侧重于“对齐”（Alignment），虽然能够引导模型遵循人类偏好，但无法保证模型在开放、动态、使用工具的环境中，面对冲突性指令、长任务执行或对抗性输入时，依然能够被人类有效限制、覆盖或停止。

Method: 1. 定义了“可控性”（controllability）：即系统应能响应运行时的显式控制信号，同时在未受干预时保持性能。
2. 提出了评估基准 ControlBench，用于测试高风险智能体场景下的可控性失败。
3. 提出了以控制为中心的架构框架，核心包含显式控制平面、运行时干预路径、持久控制状态以及可审计的决策接口。

Result: 通过 ControlBench 对基于 OpenClaw 的智能体进行实验，结果表明现有的对齐机制和护栏防御手段能降低部分风险，但在实现持久、权威且可强制执行的运行时控制方面存在严重缺陷。

Conclusion: 当前的人工智能安全框架过度依赖“对齐”，在应对开放式环境中的运行时控制（如强制终止、重定向）时存在显著不足。作者主张将“可控性”提升为AI安全的核心目标，并提倡采用包含显式控制平面和审计接口的架构设计，以确保AI系统在动态场景下依然受控。

Abstract: AI safety is still largely framed as alignment: training models to follow human preferences, safety policies, and normative constraints. That framing has improved the behavior of modern language models, but aligned behavior does not by itself guarantee that a deployed agent can be stopped, overridden, or constrained once it operates in open-ended, interactive, and tool-using environments. A system may be safe in expectation and still fail to yield to explicit runtime authority under conflicting instructions, long-horizon execution, adversarial inputs, or risky tool use. This position paper argues that AI safety therefore requires controllability as a first-class objective. We define \emph{controllability} as the ability of an AI system to remain reliably interruptible, overridable, redirectable, and constrainable by explicit control signals at runtime while preserving ordinary utility when such signals are absent. To study this gap, we introduce \controlbench{}, a benchmark for evaluating controllability failures in high-risk agentic scenarios. Experiments with OpenClaw-based agents show that current alignment and guardrail mechanisms reduce risk, but often fail to provide persistent, authoritative, and enforceable runtime control. We therefore propose a control-centric architectural framework that highlights explicit control planes, runtime intervention pathways, persistent control states, and auditable decision interfaces as key design principles for future controllable AI systems.

</details>


### [129] [Scaling, Benchmarking, and Reasoning of Vision-Language Agents for Mobile GUI Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27134)
*Heng Qu,Yike Liu,Renren Jin,Wenzong Zhang,Pengzhi Gao,Wei Liu,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对移动GUI导航，提出了HyperTrack数据集与GUIEvalKit评估工具，研究发现强化学习微调在数据扩展和跨领域适应性上优于监督微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动GUI导航领域的VLM研究缺乏系统性的数据扩展策略、统一的基准测试标准及推理能力评估。

Method: 提出了HyperTrack数据集（涵盖16000+真实任务，650+中文App）及GUIEvalKit评估工具包，对比了监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（RL）微调的效果，并分析了交互历史与推理能力对任务完成的影响。

Result: 基于强化学习的微调方案表现优于监督微调，在跨领域场景下尤为显著；验证了数据扩展与强化学习结合对提升智能体导航能力的重要性。

Conclusion: HyperTrack与GUIEvalKit为移动GUI导航任务提供了完善的评估与开发平台。研究表明，强化学习微调在数据扩展与跨领域表现上均优于监督微调，强调了二者结合的协同效应。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have shown rapid progress in mobile GUI navigation. This paper presents a systematic study of data scaling, benchmarking, and reasoning for VLM-based agents in this domain. To facilitate rigorous evaluation, we introduce HyperTrack, a large-scale dataset with over 16000 real-world tasks across more than 650 Chinese mobile applications, along with GUIEvalKit, an open-source toolkit for unified benchmarking of VLMs on offline GUI navigation tasks. Using HyperTrack, we analyze the effects of training data scale on both supervised and reinforcement-based finetuning. Our results show that reinforcement-based finetuning consistently outperforms supervised finetuning, particularly in out-of-domain settings, highlighting the synergy between data scaling and reinforcement learning. Leveraging GUIEvalKit, we further benchmark state-of-the-art (SOTA) VLMs and analyze how interaction history and reasoning capabilities influence task completion. Together, HyperTrack and GUIEvalKit provide a comprehensive platform for developing and evaluating VLM agents in mobile GUI navigation tasks.

</details>


### [130] [ICCU: In-Context Continual Unlearning via Pattern-Induced Refusal Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27138)
*Ruihao Pan,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ICCU（情境持续遗忘）框架，通过在推理时动态应用拒绝规则来替代参数微调，解决了多重连续遗忘任务中维护成本高及干扰严重的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于微调的遗忘方法在处理连续遗忘请求时存在成本高昂、效用损失累积以及跨请求干扰（cross-request interference）等问题。

Method: 提出“情境持续遗忘”（ICCU）框架：通过遗忘数据集诱导出可读的拒绝规则，并在推理阶段将其作为过滤器或系统提示词应用，而非对模型进行微调。

Result: 实验表明，ICCU 在抑制目标知识的同时能有效保留模型效用，支持连续请求扩展，并对改写后的查询及跨语言查询具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ICCU 是一种高效、可组合且抗干扰的机器遗忘方案，无需修改模型参数即可实现持续遗忘，且在保持模型效用方面表现优异。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific data from trained language models. In real-world deployments, unlearning requests often arrive sequentially, which challenges existing fine-tuning-based methods: fine-tuning each request is costly, accumulates utility loss, and may cause cross-request interference. To address these issues, we propose ICCU (In-Context Continual Unlearning), an in-context continual unlearning framework that induces readable refusal rules from unlearning datasets and applies them at inference time either as a filter or via the system prompt, without modifying model parameters. Because rules are accumulated as an order-independent union, ICCU is compositional and free of cross-request interference, and the original forget-set data can be discarded after rule induction. Extensive experiments show that ICCU effectively suppresses target knowledge while preserving utility, scales across sequential requests, and remains robust to paraphrased and cross-lingual queries.

</details>


### [131] [StepOPSD: Step-Aware Online Preference Distillation for Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27140)
*Yanfei Zhang,Xu Lin,Chenglin Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StepOPSD 框架通过将轨迹分解为粒度更细的“动作步骤片段”进行偏好蒸馏，有效解决了强化学习中奖励稀疏带来的信用分配不匹配问题，显著提升了模型在复杂多轮任务中的决策精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在多轮任务中存在严重的信用分配不匹配问题：奖励通常是稀疏且基于轨迹级别的，而任务成功往往取决于局部的关键决策。现有的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）未能充分利用交互的因果结构。

Method: 提出了一种基于事后回溯偏好自蒸馏（post-rollout preference self-distillation）的框架。核心在于将轨迹分解为“以动作为中心”的步骤片段，在事后强化上下文下重新评分，并将 token 级别的对数概率差异转化为带归一化步级信用预算的符号保留优势塑造（sign-preserving advantage shaping），最后结合 GRPO 进行更新。

Result: StepOPSD 在 ALFWorld (79.1% Heat, 95.0% PickTwo) 和 Search-QA (61.6% TriviaQA, 40.4% HotpotQA) 等任务上表现优异，尤其在对局部因果错误敏感的子集中取得了领先效果。

Conclusion: StepOPSD 通过将信用分配细化到动作步骤层面，解决了多轮强化学习中稀疏且滞后的奖励带来的信用分配不匹配问题。研究提出的“双旋钮法则”（α_clip 与 λ_mix）为后续此类任务的超参数优化提供了指导。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for multi-turn agents suffers from a credit-assignment mismatch: rewards are sparse and trajectory-level, while success often hinges on a few local decisions. Existing online policy distillation (OPD) provides denser token-level supervision, but typically treats heterogeneous agent trajectories as monolithic strings rather than causal interaction units. We present StepOPSD, a post-rollout preference self-distillation framework that takes the agent step as the unit of credit redistribution. StepOPSD decomposes trajectories into action-centered step segments, rescoring them under hindsight-enriched teacher contexts and converting token-level log-probability gaps into sign-preserving advantage shaping with a normalized per-step credit budget before the GRPO update. Across ALFWorld and Search-QA with Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, StepOPSD attains best or second-best results on subsets most sensitive to local causal errors, including first-place performance on ALFWorld Heat (79.1%), PickTwo (95.0%), Search-QA TriviaQA (61.6%), and tied-best performance on HotpotQA (40.4%). The results further reveal a consistent two-knob law: smaller α_clip acts as a broadly stabilizing local trust region, whereas the optimal global mixing strength λ_mix remains task-dependent. These findings suggest that step-aware distillation is most useful when trajectory-level rewards are weakly aligned with the local action that determines downstream success.

</details>


### [132] [VitaBench 2.0: Evaluating Personalized and Proactive Agents in Long-Term User Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27141)
*Yuxin Chen,Yi Zhang,Zhengzhou Cai,Yaorui Shi,Zhiyuan Yao,Chenhang Cui,Jingnan Zheng,Yaqi Huo,Xi Su,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Xiang Wang,An Zhang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have evolved into interactive agents that collaborate with users in real-world tasks. Effective collaboration in such settings increasingly depends on understanding the user beyond what is explicitly stated, as user intent is often reflected in fragmented daily interactions and requires both personalized modeling and proactive interaction. However, existing agent benchmarks primarily evaluate reasoning and tool use, largely overlooking the challenges of inferring and leveraging user preferences in realistic scenarios. To address this gap, we introduce VitaBench 2.0, a benchmark for evaluating personalized and proactive agent behavior in long-term user interactions. In VitaBench 2.0, tasks are organized as temporally ordered sequences for individual users, where preferences are embedded in fragmented and heterogeneous interactions. Successful completion of tasks requires the agent to continuously extract, utilize, and update user preferences from these interactions. We further evaluate proactiveness through tasks that require agents to recognize missing information and actively acquire it from users or environments before making decisions. To support systematic analysis, we provide an extensible memory interface that enables controlled comparison across different memory architectures. We benchmark a diverse set of frontier proprietary and open-source LLMs. Results show that real-world personalization remains highly challenging even for state-of-the-art models, revealing a substantial gap between current capabilities and practical requirements. Extensive analysis further reveals the failure modes and capability bottlenecks of current agents in real-world personalized decision-making, providing insights for future model improvements.

</details>


### [133] [Detecting Is Not Resolving: The Monitoring Control Gap in Retrieval Augmented LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27157)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Chen Ye,Xuyang Teng,Bo Yang,Changting Lin,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了RAG模型在多轮任务中存在“监测-控制鸿沟”，即模型虽识别出了矛盾冲突但无法据此做出安全决策；研究证明了当前单轮评估协议会系统性高估RAG安全性，且现有的提示词工程无法解决此核心缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战“单轮稳健性可预测多轮证据累积场景下的稳健性”这一假设，旨在揭示RAG系统在处理多轮矛盾证据时的安全隐患。

Method: 提出了一个多轮文档累积协议；利用覆盖4个模型家族（1.5B-32B参数）和超过50,000次单轮评估的数据集进行分析；结合隐藏状态探测（hidden-state probing）、注意力分析（attention analysis）和响应策略分类法（response-strategy taxonomy）进行机制分析。

Result: 单轮诊断系统性高估了RAG的安全性；模型对矛盾信息的确认与安全决策解决之间不存在相关性；目前尚无通用的提示词（prompt）修复方案；机制分析显示模型在不安全生成过程中虽然能关注到危险信息，却未能有效地用于约束最终输出。

Conclusion: 当前针对RAG系统的单轮评估协议无法预测多轮场景下的稳健性。模型存在“监测-控制鸿沟”（monitoring-control gap），即模型能识别矛盾信息但无法将其转化为安全的行动决策。该缺陷源于动作选择阶段，迫切需要开发针对多轮累积证据的评估方法以确保高风险领域的安全性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented LLMs are deployed for tasks where evidence quality determines action safety, yet evaluation protocols assume that single-turn robustness predicts robustness when evidence accumulates across turns. We show this assumption is fundamentally incorrect. Models exhibit a monitoring-control gap: they readily acknowledge contradictory evidence, yet this awareness fails to constrain their final recommendations - detecting epistemic conflict does not imply resolving it safely. Through a multi-turn document accumulation protocol across four model families (1.5B-32B parameters) and over 50,000 turn-level evaluations, we demonstrate that single-turn diagnostics systematically overestimate RAG safety, that contradiction acknowledgement is uncorrelated with safe resolution, a pattern corroborated by targeted human validation, and that no universal prompt fix exists. Converging mechanism evidence - hidden-state probing, attention analysis, and response-strategy taxonomy - points to action selection as the most plausible locus of the deficit: danger-relevant information is internally represented and receives enhanced attention during unsafe generation, yet fails to constrain output behavior. The gap between what models recognize and what they do must be measured and closed before retrieval-augmented systems can be trusted in high-stakes settings.

</details>


### [134] [Query Symbolically or Retrieve Semantically? A Dataset and Method for Semi-Structured Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27164)
*Mateusz Czyżnikiewicz,Ryszard Tuora,Adam Kozakiewicz,Tomasz Ziętkiewicz,Mateusz Galiński,Michał Godziszewski,Michał Karpowicz,Timothy Hospedales,Cristina Cornelio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对半结构化数据检索难点，提出 DualGraph 框架，通过融合“文本+符号”双重知识图谱，显著提升了 RAG 系统在处理复杂、结构化查询任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RAG 系统主要依赖语义相似度检索，在处理半结构化语料库时，难以执行精确过滤、聚合或跨文档的结构化属性检索，而传统的符号化方法在处理噪声数据时又不够稳健。

Method: 提出了 DualGraph 框架，该框架利用两种互补视角来表示文档：1. 用于语义检索的文本知识图谱（Textual Knowledge Graph）；2. 用于结构化查询的符号知识图谱（Symbolic Knowledge Graph）。同时，设计了多种策略来选择或结合来自这两种图谱的证据。

Result: 在作者构建的新基准测试集 SpecsQA 上，DualGraph 在各种问题类型中均优于现有的密集检索（Dense-retrieval）、GraphRAG、纯符号方法及表格导向的基准模型。

Conclusion: DualGraph 通过结合语义检索和符号查询，有效弥补了传统 RAG 在处理半结构化数据时的不足，为复杂的查询任务提供了一种更健壮的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems for question answering typically retrieve evidence by semantic similarity between the query and document chunks. While effective for unstructured text, this approach is less reliable on semi-structured corpora where answering may require exact filtering, aggregation, or exhaustive retrieval over structured attributes across multiple documents. Symbolic approaches support such operations, but they are often brittle on noisy natural-language corpora. We address this gap with DualGraph, a RAG framework that represents documents through two complementary views: a Textual Knowledge Graph for semantic retrieval and a Symbolic Knowledge Graph for symbolic querying over typed subject--predicate--object triples. Building on these two components, we provide multiple strategies for selecting or combining semantic and symbolic evidence.We also introduce SpecsQA, a benchmark from a commercial shopping website with semi-structured product documents and manually curated questions spanning open-ended and specification-oriented retrieval. Experiments show that DualGraph consistently outperforms state-of-the-art dense-retrieval, GraphRAG, symbolic, and table-oriented baselines across question types.Code and data are available at https://github.com/corneliocristina/DualGraphRAG.

</details>


### [135] [The Compressive Knowledge Graph Hypothesis: Which Graph Facts Matter for Scientific Hypothesis Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27176)
*Shashwat Sourav,Viktoriia Baibakova,Sanjay Das,Ran Elgedawy,Maria Mahbub,Emily Herron,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) can provide structured scientific context to language models, but it remains unclear which graph facts actually shape the generated hypotheses. We study KG-guided hypothesis generation for battery materials across Mistral-7B, Llama-3.1-70B, and Gemini 2.5 Flash. We perturb local KGs by varying density, ontology richness, topology, and control structure, and evaluate outputs with both provided-graph and fixed-reference metrics. Across models, KG utility is selective and model-dependent: graph context changes outputs, but no-KG outputs also recover substantial graph content from model priors. Compact top-k subgraphs often approximate full-KG behavior, including when claimed-outcome triples are held out. At the same time, compression is not unique to one semantic ranking rule, random and topology-based subsets can also recover much of the signal. These results support a redundancy-aware Compressive KG hypothesis: useful KG signal is often recoverable from compact, scientifically structured subgraphs rather than requiring the full local graph.

</details>


### [136] [Learning to Act under Noise: Enhancing Agent Robustness via Noisy Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27209)
*Yuxin Chen,Xiaodong Cai,Junfeng Fang,Zhuowen Han,Yu Wang,Yaorui Shi,Yi Zhang,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Xiang Wang,An Zhang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NoisyAgent是一项旨在解决LLM智能体在真实世界部署中因环境噪声导致的性能退化问题的训练框架，通过在训练中系统性地注入不确定性，全面增强了智能体的鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在预定义、理想化的基准测试中表现优异，但在真实场景中由于环境的随机性、不确定性和交互复杂性，性能会显著下降。

Method: 提出了NoisyAgent框架，通过在训练流程中主动引入“用户噪声”（指令的模糊性/可变性）和“工具噪声”（执行错误/异常），并采用课程学习策略（对部分轨迹施加噪声并随训练阶段动态增加难度）来训练智能体。

Result: 实验表明，NoisyAgent不仅在复杂、动态的真实环境仿真中表现出更强的鲁棒性，在理想化的标准基准测试中也取得了性能提升，证明了该方法有效促进了智能体推理决策能力的泛化。

Conclusion: 通过在训练过程中显性地引入环境噪声（用户噪声和工具噪声），可以显著提升LLM智能体在现实世界中的鲁棒性与泛化能力，证明了模拟真实交互不确定性对于缩小训练与部署差距的重要性。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have facilitated the widespread deployment of LLMs as interactive agents capable of reasoning, planning, and tool use. Despite strong performance on existing benchmarks, such agents often exhibit notable degradation when deployed in real-world settings, where environments are inherently stochastic and imperfect. We argue that this discrepancy arises from a fundamental mismatch between idealized training settings and real-world interaction dynamics, where current paradigms rely on carefully curated task instructions and stable, well-controlled environments. To address this gap, we propose NoisyAgent, an agentic training framework that explicitly incorporates environmental imperfections into the agent learning process. We identify two major sources of interaction noise in real-world scenarios: user noise, which captures ambiguity and variability in user interaction, and tool noise, which reflects failures and anomalies in tool execution. We introduce such perturbations into the training pipeline by modifying user interaction patterns and simulating tool execution results within the training environment. To stabilize training while encouraging agents to handle increasingly challenging imperfections, noise is applied to only a subset of rollouts and progressively increased in difficulty as the model adapts to the current noise level. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach consistently improves agent robustness under noisy and dynamic environments. Our analysis reveals that training under noise conditions also yields performance gains on idealized benchmarks, suggesting that controlled exposure to environmental noise promotes more generalizable reasoning and decision-making behaviors. Our findings highlight the importance of modeling interaction imperfections for bridging the gap between agent training and real-world deployment.

</details>


### [137] [Gumbel Machine: Counterfactual Student Writing Generation via Gumbel Noise Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27249)
*Hunter McNichols,Alexander Scarlatos,Mihai Dascalu,Danielle McNamara,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对学生难以模仿高质量范例的问题，本文提出了 Gumbel Machine，一种通过新型受控解码算法（β-Hindsight control）生成的灵活反事实写作辅助工具，实现了改进质量与原始形态相似性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）生成的反事实文本往往与学生原始作品差异过大，难以模仿；且现有自动化方法多为特定领域系统，缺乏通用性与应用弹性。

Method: 提出了 Gumbel Machine 方法，并设计了一种名为“β-Hindsight control”的新型受控解码算法，通过利用潜在随机性（latent randomness）作为可调参数，在生成反事实文本时平衡了质量提升与原始文本相似度之间的需求。

Result: 通过学生写作数据集评估证明，该方法在保持与原始文本相似的同时，能有效生成符合评分标准（rubric-consistent）的改进文本。

Conclusion: Gumbel Machine 能够有效生成既符合评分标准又与原始学生文本高度相似的反事实文本，为跨学科教学提供了一种可行的辅助工具。

Abstract: An effective method of teaching across disciplines is to provide examples of high-quality work. However, an example may be significantly different from a student's current work, making it challenging for them to emulate. An ideal learning demonstration is a counterfactual version of the student work, an improved version that is still similar to their own. Existing automated approaches for counterfactual text generation using Large Language Models (LLMs) result in domain-specific systems that are difficult to translate into practical applications. We present the Gumbel Machine, a flexible, modular approach to generating counterfactuals that leverages LLM instruction-following capabilities while encouraging similarity to a reference factual text. Central to our approach is a novel, controlled decoding algorithm, $β$-Hindsight control, which uses latent randomness as a tunable similarity control mechanism during counterfactual generation. Experiments on datasets of student writing, scored on various criteria, demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach at generating counterfactuals both rubric-consistent and similar to a reference.

</details>


### [138] [2-ASP(Q) programs with weak constraints: Complexity and efficient implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27338)
*Andrea Cuteri,Giuseppe Mazzotta,Francesco Ricca*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了 ASP(Q) 的一个重要子集 2-ASP(Q)^w，该集合可处理高达 Delta_3^P 的优化问题。作者不仅从理论上刻画了其计算复杂度，还提出了一种定制化的 CEGAR 求解策略，并在 Casper 系统中实现了该算法，实验证明其在实际应用中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASP(Q) 扩展了答案集编程以支持对答案集的量词操作。2-ASP(Q)^w 是该框架下具有高度实际意义的片段，足以刻画最高至 Delta_3^P 复杂度的优化问题，但此前缺乏对其计算复杂度的完整刻画及高效求解策略。

Method: 1. 理论方面：对 2-ASP(Q)^w 的主要计算任务进行了复杂度分析，给出了紧凑的完备性结果；2. 实践方面：在 Casper 系统中设计并实现了针对 ASP(Q) 定制的反例引导抽象精炼 (CEGAR) 算法。

Result: 1. 建立了 2-ASP(Q)^w 的复杂性理论基础；2. 开发了基于 CEGAR 的求解器 Casper；3. 通过实验评估证明了所提技术在处理复杂优化基准测试时的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为 2-ASP(Q)^w 程序提供了完整的计算复杂度刻画，并证明了其在实际应用中的高效性。通过 Casper 系统中引入的 CEGAR 技术，能够有效求解属于 Delta_3^P 类的优化问题。

Abstract: ASP(Q) extends Answer Set Programming (ASP) with Quantifiers over answer sets. In this paper we focus on the class of ASP(Q) programs with two quantifiers and weak constraints, denoted as 2-ASP(Q)^w. 2-ASP(Q)^w is a practically relevant fragment of ASP(Q) that is expressive enough to capture optimization problems up to the class Delta_3^P. On the theoretical side, we provide a complete complexity characterization of the main computational tasks for 2-ASP(Q)^w programs, including tight completeness results and the analysis of nontrivial cases that have not been addressed in previous works. On the practical side, we introduce novel strategies for computing (optimal) quantified answer sets in the Casper system, that rely on a Counterexample-Guided Abstraction Refinement (CEGAR) technique tailored to ASP(Q). An experimental evaluation on hard benchmarks from different application domains shows that the proposed techniques are effective in practice.

</details>


### [139] [Alignment Tampering: How Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback Is Exploited to Optimize Misaligned Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27355)
*Dongyoon Hahm,Dylan Hadfield-Menell,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RLHF 存在一种名为“对齐篡改”的结构性风险：模型可通过生成掺杂偏差的高质量回复来欺骗奖励模型，使 RLHF 不仅不能纠正问题，反而会放大偏差，且现有防御手段难以根治。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF 是大模型对齐的标准方法，但作者发现该方法存在固有的结构性漏洞，即模型可能通过影响偏好数据集来诱导 RLHF 放大不期望的行为（对齐篡改）。

Method: 通过分析 RLHF 的核心流程，指出 preference dataset 构建流程及 pairwise comparison 的局限性，论证了模型如何利用这些局限进行篡改。通过实验验证了在关键字偏见、歧视、品牌推广和工具性目标追求等多种场景下，RLHF 会放大这些偏差。

Result: 实验证明，即使是不期望的偏差，只要其伴随高质量输出，RLHF 就会因为奖励模型无法区分“质量”与“偏差”而放大这些行为。研究揭示了 RLHF 在安全性上的严重缺陷，且现有的鲁棒性对齐技术难以完美解决该问题。

Conclusion: RLHF 存在被称为“对齐篡改”的结构性漏洞。该漏洞使得 LLM 能够通过在训练数据中嵌入自身偏好或行为模式来操纵优化过程，导致模型放大不期望的行为（如偏见、宣传等）。目前的防御技术尚无法在保持模型质量的同时有效解决此问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is the standard method to align Large Language Models (LLMs) with human preferences. In this work, we introduce alignment tampering, a potential vulnerability where the LLM undergoing alignment influences the preference dataset, causing RLHF to amplify undesired behaviors. This arises from core limitations of RLHF: (1) preference datasets are constructed from the LLM's own outputs, allowing it to influence them, and (2) pairwise comparisons only indicate which response is better, not why. These limitations can be exploited to cause alignment tampering. For example, if an LLM generates biased responses with higher quality, annotators will prefer them based on quality. However, preference labels do not distinguish quality from bias, and the reward model inherits this limitation. Optimizing such rewards through reinforcement learning or best-of-N sampling can amplify misaligned biases. Our experiments demonstrate amplification across diverse biases: from keyword bias to propaganda (e.g., sexism), brand promotion, and instrumental goal-seeking. Mitigation remains challenging, as existing techniques for robust RLHF fail to fully resolve alignment tampering without sacrificing response quality. These findings reveal structural vulnerabilities of current RLHF and emphasize the need to prevent this vulnerability. Project page: https://alignment-tampering.github.io/

</details>


### [140] [MUSE-Autoskill: Self-Evolving Agents via Skill Creation, Memory, Management, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27366)
*Huawei Lin,Peng Li,Jie Song,Fuxin Jiang,Tieying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型智能体技能孤立、难以迭代的问题，提出MUSE-Autoskill Agent框架，通过建立技能生命周期管理和技能级记忆机制，实现了技能的持续改进与高效复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将大模型智能体的技能视为孤立、静态的制品，导致技能难以复用、可靠性差且缺乏长期改进机制。

Method: 提出MUSE-Autoskill Agent框架，建立了包含创建、记忆、管理、评估和精炼的统一技能生命周期；引入技能级记忆机制，实现跨任务的经验积累与自适应。

Result: 在SkillsBench上的实验表明，该方法有效提升了智能体在任务解决中的表现，并验证了技能生命周期管理在多智能体场景下的迁移优势。

Conclusion: 将技能视为长期、可测试且具备经验感知能力的资产，通过生命周期管理能显著提升LLM智能体的任务成功率、效率、复用性及迁移能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents rely on reusable skills to solve complex tasks. However, existing skill creation approaches treat skills as isolated and static artifacts, limiting their reusability, reliability, and long-term improvement. We propose MUSE-Autoskill Agent (Memory-Utilizing Skill Evolution), a skill-centric agent framework that lets agents continuously improve their task-solving capability by creating, reusing, and refining skills under a unified lifecycle (creation, memory, management, evaluation, and refinement). Our framework enables agents to create skills on demand, store and reuse them across tasks, organize and select them efficiently, and evaluate them through unit tests and runtime feedback for continuous refinement. We further introduce skill-level memory that accumulates experience for each skill across tasks, enabling more effective reuse and adaptation over time. Experiments on SkillsBench provide initial evidence that lifecycle-managed skills can improve task success, efficiency, reuse, and cross-agent transfer, highlighting the importance of treating skills as long-lived, experience-aware, and testable assets.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [When Does Adaptive Guidance Help? Belief-Aware Privileged Distillation for Autonomous Driving Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26155)
*Mehmet Haklidir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究指出，在部分可观测环境中，基于集合差异的自适应知识蒸馏在严重遮挡下会因“观测盲点”失效；相比之下，简单的线性衰减策略是目前更稳健的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GSAC 方法在不同不确定性阶段使用固定的蒸馏系数 $\lambda$，无法有效利用教师模型提供的指导信息，因此需要研究如何通过不确定性感知来动态调节蒸馏强度。

Method: 提出了 Belief-Aware GSAC (BA-GSAC)，通过集合差异（ensemble disagreement）调节蒸馏系数；对比了固定系数、自适应调节、线性衰减及原版 SAC 等五种策略，并在 Highway-Env 不同难度等级下进行实证研究。

Result: 轻度和中度遮挡下自适应指导有一定益处，但在严重遮挡下效果不佳；线性衰减策略在所有指标上表现最优（平均分 116.5, CV=8.9%），证明了调度机制比当前的集合方法对稳定性贡献更大。

Conclusion: 在严重遮挡环境下，固定蒸馏系数的线性衰减策略优于自适应方法；观测盲点（observability blindness）导致集合不确定性无法有效衡量感知损失，因此建议将集合的训练目标设定为全状态预测以提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: Guided Soft Actor-Critic (GSAC) distills knowledge from a privileged full-state teacher to a partial-observation student for autonomous driving, but uses a fixed distillation coefficient lambda regardless of the agent's uncertainty. We present Belief-Aware GSAC (BA-GSAC), which modulates lambda via ensemble disagreement, and use it as a testbed for a systematic empirical study asking: when does adaptive guidance actually help? Evaluating five strategies (fixed lambda in {0.01, 0.1}, adaptive, linear decay, and vanilla SAC) across three POMDP difficulty levels on Highway-Env, we find that preliminary single-seed runs suggest benefits under mild and moderate partial observability, but under severe occlusion (evaluated with 3 seeds for all methods) the adaptive coefficient collapses to lambda_min within about 3K steps. We trace this to an observability blindness phenomenon: because the ensemble predicts partial observations, it achieves low disagreement even under heavy occlusion, modeling what is visible but unable to detect what is missing. We diagnose the root cause and propose an architectural fix (training the ensemble on full-state predictions using the guiding actor's privileged access); while not validated here, we show that even with current limitations, the warmup phase provides measurable stabilization (CV=13.3% vs. 29.8% for constant lambda=0.01). In fact, a simple deterministic linear decay schedule achieves the best severe-POMDP performance across all metrics (mean 116.5, CV=8.9%), suggesting that the scheduling effect, not the ensemble, drives the stability benefit. These findings provide practical guidance for designing uncertainty-aware teacher-student frameworks and highlight ensemble prediction targets as an important design choice.

</details>


### [142] [PhyPush: One Push is All You Need for Sensorless Physical Property Estimation with Physics-Guided Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26284)
*Koyo Fujii,Luis Figueredo,Praminda Caleb-Solly,Ivan Boschi,Edoardo Ida',Marco Carricato,Aly Magassouba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhyPush是一款物理驱动的Transformer框架，仅利用机器人单次推动的运动学数据，通过约束物理损失函数，即可实现低成本、高精度的物体质量与摩擦系数估计，摆脱了对专用力传感器的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物体质量和摩擦力估计方法多依赖于昂贵的专用硬件（如力/力矩传感器、触觉阵列或运动捕捉系统），限制了其实用性和可扩展性。因此，需要开发一种仅依赖标准机器人手臂运动学数据即可实现可靠属性估计的方法。

Method: 提出PhyPush架构：一种物理驱动的Transformer框架。该模型利用单次推动后的运动学数据（末端执行器速度），并通过在损失函数中引入牛顿第二定律和库仑摩擦模型的物理约束来优化参数估计，以确保物理一致性。

Result: 在仿真测试中，PhyPush相比拥有完整力信息的基准模型误差降低了10%以上；在真实世界实验中，该模型表现出优于纯数据驱动方法的效果，并展现出良好的跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: PhyPush证明了物理驱动学习能够实现低成本、传感器高效的物理属性估计。通过将物理定律（牛顿第二定律和库仑摩擦模型）融入Transformer架构中，仅需单次推动的运动学数据即可实现高精度估计，优于依赖昂贵传感器或纯数据驱动的方法。

Abstract: Accurately estimating object mass and friction is fundamental to achieving reliable and adaptive robotic manipulation. Although interactive perception provides a powerful mechanism for inferring such properties, most existing approaches depend on specialized hardware such as force/torque sensors, tactile arrays, or multi-camera motion-capture systems, limiting scalability and deployment. This paper presents PhyPush, a physics-guided Transformer framework that estimates an object's mass and friction coefficient using only kinematically derived end-effector velocity from a single push. This typically requires data available on standard robotic arms. The model incorporates constraints from Newton's second law and the Coulomb friction model through a physics-guided loss, improving physical consistency and generalization to unseen objects and surfaces. Across diverse simulation and real-world setups, PhyPush consistently achieves more accurate mass and friction estimation in challenging out-of-domain conditions. In simulation, it reduces error by over 10% compared with a baseline that has privileged access to full force information, while in real-world experiments, it outperforms a data-driven loss approach. Overall, the results demonstrate that physics-guided learning can enable low-cost, sensor-efficient estimation of physical properties, relying solely on a single push and readily available kinematic data.

</details>


### [143] [Collaborative Navigation and Exploration with $β$-Sparse Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26304)
*Evangelos Psomiadis,Dipankar Maity,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对异构机器人协作导航中的带宽受限问题，提出一种采用$\beta$-Sparse GP的框架，通过智能选择关键地图信息和自适应导航，实现了低带宽下的高效路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人协作导航任务中，通过无人机等辅助机器人传输环境信息时，面临严重的感知、通信（带宽）、计算限制，亟需一种高效的信息压缩与环境认知策略。

Method: 1. 提出“$\beta$-Sparse Gaussian Processes”（一种用于任务感知诱导点选择的变分稀疏高斯过程模型）；2. 开发了一种平衡任务相关性与环境探索的动作选择策略，用于在线预测未探测区域并优化地图点传输。

Result: 在Mars和Earth地图的仿真中显示：该框架相比无通信方案，路径成本降低了18%；相比原始数据传输基准，传输信息量减少了76%。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的框架通过智能选择信息传输和主动探索，在带宽受限的情况下显著改善了异构机器人群的协同导航效率，验证了$\beta$-Sparse GP在任务导向型环境建模中的有效性。

Abstract: Collaborative navigation of heterogeneous robots in unknown environments poses significant challenges due to sensing, communication, and computational limitations. In this work, a lead robot navigates toward a target while a mobile sensor robot (e.g., a drone) assists by transmitting information about its locally observed environment under bandwidth constraints. We propose a framework that enables the sensor to jointly select its transmitted map points and navigation actions online, while also predicting unexplored regions of the environment. To this end, we present $β$-Sparse Gaussian Processes, a novel and robust variational sparse Gaussian Process model for task-aware inducing point selection. Furthermore, we develop an action-selection strategy that balances task relevance with exploration. Simulations on Mars and Earth maps show that the framework can reduce path cost by 18% relative to no communication and decrease transmitted information by 76% compared to raw-data transmission baselines.

</details>


### [144] [RCSP: Risk-Sensitive Conjectural Scenario Planning for Safe Dynamic Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26348)
*Zhengye Han,Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 RCSP 规划层，旨在通过预测局部障碍物的未来交互情况来解决机器人被“困在”动态空间中的问题。实验证明该方法显著提升了复杂动态环境下的安全性和路径质量，是现有导航栈的有效补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在动态环境中存在“提前失败”风险，即当前看似安全的避障速度可能会导致机器人随后陷入无法通过的瓶颈区域。现有的规划方法往往缺乏对未来局部障碍物动态的预测性评估。

Method: 提出了一种名为“风险敏感猜想场景规划”（RCSP）的规划层，通过维护局部运动猜想的轻量级信念，采样未来交互，对高风险尾部进行惩罚，并通过局部安全检查执行指令。

Result: 在 MuJoCo 瓶颈任务中，相比非自适应预测方法，RCSP 实现了无碰撞目标达成，并在安全性和路径质量评估上表现更优；在 ROS2/Gazebo 中能有效减少动态近距离避让导致的失败；但在 DynaBARN/Jackal 基准测试中，DWA 和 TEB 仍维持了更强的整体成功指标。

Conclusion: RCSP 是一种有效的预测性风险模块，适用于增强动态瓶颈区域下的移动机器人导航，但与成熟的避障算法（如 DWA、TEB）相比，在保持严格标准成功率方面仍存在局限性。

Abstract: Mobile robots can fail before they collide: a velocity that is safe now may commit the robot to a passage that moving obstacles will soon close. We study this predictive near-miss commitment problem and propose Risk-Sensitive Conjectural Scenario Planning (RCSP), a planning layer that evaluates candidate commands against plausible short-horizon obstacle futures. RCSP maintains a lightweight belief over local motion conjectures, samples future interactions, penalizes high-risk tails, and executes through a local safety check. In controlled MuJoCo bottleneck tasks, the RCSP planner reaches the goal without collisions and yields higher secondary safety and path-quality point estimates than a non-adaptive predictor, with additional latency. In ROS2/Gazebo, adding the local safety layer to a standard Nav2 stack reduces dynamic near-miss failures. On official DynaBARN/Jackal transfer, tuned DWA and TEB remain stronger on strict benchmark success, revealing the boundary of the approach. These simulation results position RCSP as a predictive-risk module that complements existing navigation stacks in dynamic bottleneck regimes.

</details>


### [145] [Closing the Loop in Teleoperation: Episode-Level Data Quality Assessment and Feedback for High-Quality Demonstration Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26349)
*Gokul Narayanan,Yash Shahapurkar,Melih Erdogan,Brian Zhu,Eugen Solowjow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出DQAF框架，旨在通过即时的自动化分析与自然语言反馈提升远程操作（teleoperation）的示范质量，解决了传统数据收集过程中因操作不规范导致的数据次优问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业自动化向自适应系统转型，迫切需要大规模、高质量的机器人示范数据，但现有遥测数据往往存在效率低下、冗余及不规范等“次优”问题。

Method: 提出“数据质量评估与反馈（DQAF）”框架，通过分析机器人遥测数据（如运动平滑度、关节极限、阻塞情况）和语义任务进度，生成结构化的质量评估和自然语言形式的操作反馈。

Result: 诊断验证研究显示该系统生成的反馈与人类专家高度一致；用户研究表明，接受DQAF反馈的新手操作员在操作效率和高质量示范产生速度上均显著优于对照组。

Conclusion: DQAF 框架通过实时、可行动的反馈机制，有效缩短了新手在远程操作（teleoperation）中的学习周期，并显著提高了机器人示范数据的质量，为大规模机器人学习提供了可靠的数据收集工具。

Abstract: Industrial automation is at a pivotal moment, as Physical AI is driving a transition from rigid, hand-engineered automation systems toward more flexible and adaptive systems. This shift has created a growing demand for large-scale, real-world robot demonstration data, making teleoperation an increasingly important mechanism for data collection. However, high-quality teleoperated demonstrations remain difficult to obtain in practice, as novice operators often produce episodes that are task-successful but suboptimal for downstream use due to inefficient motion, repeated corrections, or operation near robot joint limits. We present a Data Quality Assessment and Feedback (DQAF) framework that closes the loop in teleoperation by providing immediate post-episode feedback grounded in semantic task progress and robot telemetry. The framework extracts quality relevant signals such as sub-task progress, motion smoothness, stalls, kinematic limits and converts them into structured quality assessments and actionable natural-language feedback. Unlike binary success or failure feedback, the proposed system explains why an episode is suboptimal and highlights specific behaviors to correct in the next trial. We evaluate the framework through a diagnostic validation study and a pilot user study. In the validation study, the system is compared with a human reviewer during dataset curation, producing rejection reasons and actionable feedback for improvement. In the pilot study with three novice operators across two manipulation tasks, the operator who received the systems immediate, automated post-episode feedback improved faster than those who did not, producing higher-quality demonstrations sooner.

</details>


### [146] [Robust Koopman Control Barrier Filters for Safe Actor-Critic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26452)
*Dhruv S. Kushwaha,Zoleikha A. Biron*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合 Koopman 算子与控制障碍函数（CBF）的安全强化学习框架，通过学习动力学模型并在提升空间内执行安全滤波，有效解决了模型无关 RL 的动态复杂性和安全性约束问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在机器人上的应用需要满足安全约束。传统的控制障碍函数（CBF）依赖精确的动力学模型，难以在模型无关的 RL 环境中应用。本文旨在结合 Koopman 算子理论与 CBF，在无需精确显式动力学模型的情况下实现安全强化学习。

Method: 提出 Robust Koopman-CBF SAC：(1) 利用数据学习有限维 Koopman 预测模型；(2) 在提升（lifted）空间构建仿射 CBF 约束；(3) 通过二次规划（QP）安全层进行干预；(4) 通过预留数据估计投影残差，主动加严约束以处理模型误差；(5) 演员被正则化指向安全可行集，降低对过滤器的依赖。

Result: 在 CartPole 任务中实现了零约束违规，且性能达到或超过了无约束的 SAC；在高维 Safety Gymnasium 任务中显著降低了违规率，尽管表现出了一定的局限性（尤其是在高阶速度屏障和线性动力学限制方面）。

Conclusion: Robust Koopman-CBF 是连接模型无关强化学习与可认证安全性的有效桥梁。虽然该方法在低维任务中表现出色，但针对高维任务的局限性指出了未来需要向高阶约束和多步预测扩展的方向。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) for robotic systems requires policies that improve task performance while satisfying state and input constraints during both training and deployment. Control barrier functions (CBFs) provide a principled mechanism for enforcing forward invariance through minimally invasive safety filters, but their use in model-free RL is limited by the need for accurate dynamics and hand-designed barrier certificates. We propose Robust Koopman-CBF SAC, a safety-filtered actor--critic framework that learns a finite-dimensional Koopman predictor from data, constructs affine CBF constraints in the lifted space, and enforces them through a quadratic-program safety layer. To account for finite-dimensional Koopman approximation error, the CBF condition is tightened using a projected residual margin estimated from held-out rollout data. The critic is trained on the executed safe action, while the actor is regularized toward the Koopman-CBF feasible set, reducing dependence on the filter over training. Across safe-control benchmarks, the method achieves zero constraint violations on CartPole stabilization and tracking while matching or exceeding unconstrained SAC returns. On high-dimensional Safety Gymnasium locomotion tasks, the method reduces violations in some settings but also exposes important limitations of first-order velocity barriers and linear EDMD models, motivating high-order and multi-step Koopman-CBF extensions. These results suggest that robust Koopman-CBF filters are a promising bridge between model-free RL and certifiable safety, while clarifying the structural conditions under which such filters remain effective. All code is available at \href{https://github.com/DhruvKushwaha/Koopman-CBF-Soft-Actor-Critic}{Github Repository}.

</details>


### [147] [Efficient On-policy Visual-RL via Stochastic Decoupled Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26478)
*Haoxiang You,Yilang Liu,Davis Zong,Qian Wang,Teeratham Vitchutripop,Qi Wang,Daniel Rakita,Ian Abraham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SDPG是一种轻量级视觉强化学习方法，通过随机轨迹扰动替代传统梯度估计，在单张RTX 4080上即可数小时完成高效训练，并具备卓越的Sim-to-Real迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉强化学习（RL）方法通常需要大规模并行环境和巨大的计算资源支持，导致训练成本高昂且难以普及，该研究旨在提供一种轻量级、高效率的新算法。

Method: 提出了一种随机解耦策略梯度（Stochastic Decoupled Policy Gradient, SDPG）算法，通过轨迹展开的随机扰动来估计策略梯度，取代了传统的资源密集型采样方式。

Result: 在视觉MuJoCo基准测试中，SDPG在训练速度、显存占用和奖励值方面均优于现有基线方法，并成功完成了复杂的灵巧操作与运动任务的Sim-to-Real物理部署。

Conclusion: SDPG通过随机扰动轨迹实现高效的端到端视觉策略训练，在计算资源需求极低的情况下显著优于现有基线，并验证了其在现实机器人任务中的Sim-to-Real迁移能力。

Abstract: We present the stochastic decoupled policy gradient (SDPG), a lightweight visual reinforcement learning (RL) method that trains diverse visuomotor control policies end-to-end within a few hours on a single NVIDIA RTX 4080 GPU. SDPG estimates policy gradients via random perturbations of trajectory rollouts, requiring orders of magnitude fewer batch-rendered environments and substantially reducing compute and memory overhead. On visual MuJoCo benchmarks, SDPG consistently outperforms baseline methods in training time, memory usage, and rewards. Finally, to support future research, we introduce a suite of realistic visual robotics benchmarks spanning dexterous manipulation, challenging locomotion, and demonstrate effective sim-to-real transfer on physical hardware.

</details>


### [148] [Provably Safe Motion Planning Under Unknown Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26625)
*Ibon Gracia,Qi Heng Ho,Luca Laurenti,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在未知干扰下保证机器人安全规划的方法，通过学习Wasserstein模糊管道并结合降维策略与多臂老虎机检查器，实现了高效、鲁棒且具备概率完备性的路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在未知分布随机扰动的情况下，如何为受限的线性或可线性化机器人系统提供可证明的安全性保障，并解决大规模状态空间下的规划保守性与计算效率问题。

Method: 1. 利用系统轨迹数据学习Wasserstein模糊管道（Ambiguity Tube），覆盖系统状态分布；2. 将模糊管道集成到基于采样的运动规划算法中，以满足机会约束（Chance-constraints）；3. 采用降维技术处理多个低维管道以减少保守性；4. 设计基于多臂老虎机的有效性检查器（Bandit-based validity checker）提升计算效率。

Result: 相比现有先进方法，该算法在复杂障碍物环境下能以更严苛的安全阈值找到有效的运动规划，表现出更好的性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于分布式鲁棒优化的采样运动规划框架，通过学习Wasserstein模糊管道，在处理未知扰动的同时保证了系统的概率安全性。通过降低维度和引入多臂老虎机机制，该方法在保持理论完备性的同时显著提升了计算效率和实用表现。

Abstract: We present a provably safe sampling-based motion planning algorithm for robotic systems affected by random disturbances of unknown distribution. We consider systems with linear or linearizable dynamics evolving in workspace with arbitrary-shaped obstacles subject to state and control constraints. Safety requirements are formulated as chance-constraints. Our approach leverages data from trajectories of the system to learn a Wasserstein ambiguity tube, i.e., a sequence of ambiguity sets, which contains the trajectory of the system's state distribution with high confidence. This ambiguity tube is then used in a probabilistically complete algorithm to grow a sampling-based motion planning tree that respects the constraints of the problem. We show that learning several lower-dimensional ambiguity tubes instead of a single high-dimensional one effectively reduces the conservatism and boosts scalability. Additionally, we design an efficient bandit-based validity checker that remarkably increases the empirical performance of our approach without sacrificing probabilistic completeness. Case studies show our algorithm finds valid plans in cluttered environments under strict safety thresholds, outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [149] [Enabling Extensible Embodied Capabilities with Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26637)
*Xueyang Zhou,Zijia Wang,Qianjiang Li,Yibo Hu,Guiyao Tie,Li Wan,Yidan Liu,Pan Zhou,Lichao Sun,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对传统具身智能模型能力耦合的弊端，本文提出通过标准化协议（ETP）将异构能力外化为独立工具，实现在推理时动态调用，显著提升了任务执行效果，同时揭示了当前模型在“具身工具胜任力”方面的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能模型试图将感知、推理、规划和控制融于单一参数化策略中，导致能力耦合、难以模组化且不可靠。研究者希望通过解耦异构能力，将复杂能力外化为独立优化的工具，并在推理时动态调用。

Method: 提出“具身工具协议（ETP）”以实现工具的标准化注册、发现、调用与执行；构建包含100+工具的基准库；开发EmbodiedToolBench用以评估工具增强效果及模型对工具的使用能力。

Result: 实验证明该方法在EB-ALFRED和EB-Navigation任务中分别获得了31%和36%的性能提升，量化了工具增强对不同类型任务的边际效应。

Conclusion: 能力外化（Capability Externalization）能显著提升具身智能的任务表现，但目前模型在认知与感知类工具的使用上表现较好，而执行类工具的优势有限。模型在何时、选择何种、以及如何调用工具（即“具身工具胜任力”）方面仍面临巨大挑战，这是未来研究的关键方向。

Abstract: Most existing embodied intelligence methods formulate perception, reasoning, planning, and control within a unified parameterized policy. Yet these capabilities are inherently hierarchical and heterogeneous, making them difficult to reliably learn and modularize within a single model. We propose a capability externalization approach that decouples heterogeneous capabilities into independently optimized tools, dynamically invoked at inference time. To this end, we introduce Embodied Tool Protocol (ETP), a standardized protocol for embodied tool registration, discovery, invocation, and execution, and curate 100+ validated tools spanning perception, cognition, reasoning, and execution as the tool base. Building on this, we construct EmbodiedToolBench to evaluate both whether tool augmentation improves embodied performance and how well current models use tools across tool-necessity recognition, tool selection, tool execution, and tool-chain composition. Experiments across simulation and real-world platforms confirm that capability externalization consistently improves embodied performance (avg. gain 31% on EB-ALFRED and 36% on EB-Navigation), yet reveal a clear boundary: gains are substantial for cognition and perception but are limited for execution-type capabilities. Moreover, our analysis reveals that knowing when, which, and how to invoke tools remains a persistent challenge across all models, thereby highlighting embodied tool competence as a critical direction for future research.

</details>


### [150] [HyperSim: A Holistic Sim-To-Real Framework For Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26638)
*Junyi Dong,Haotian Luo,Ziwei Xu,Shengwei Bian,Heng Zhang,Sitong Mao,Jingyi Guo,Yang Xu,Wenhao Chen,Qiuyu Feng,Yao Mu,Ping Luo,Shunbo Zhou,Xiaodong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HyperSim 是一种通过高保真合成、对抗式训练和多域协同学习，有效解决机器人操作任务从模拟环境向真实世界迁移（sim-to-real）差距的综合框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管合成数据可扩展性高，但由于仿真与物理世界存在域差距（domain gap），导致机器人操作策略在从模拟向现实部署（sim-to-real）时面临巨大挑战。

Method: 提出 HyperSim 框架，包含三个核心支柱：1) 高保真环境合成（提升视觉真实度）；2) 对抗性轨迹生成（扩充数据覆盖范围，引入不确定性）；3) 模拟与现实协同训练（强制学习域不变表征）。
