Result: 在 400 次真实世界任务执行中，ACT 和 π_{0} 的模拟到现实成功率分别达到 80% 和 95%；在物理扰动下，策略的完成率提升了 35%。

Conclusion: HyperSim 证明了通过高保真合成、对抗性轨迹生成和模拟与现实协同训练，能够有效缩小仿真与现实之间的差距，从而以低成本、高效率的方式实现机器人操作策略的泛化与部署。

Abstract: Scaling data volume and diversity is critical for generalizing embodied intelligence. While synthetic data generation offers a scalable alternative to expensive physical data acquisition, transferring robotic manipulation policies from simulation to the real world (sim-to-real) remains a formidable challenge due to the domain gap. This paper presents HyperSim, a holistic framework spanning from synthetic data generation to policy training and seamless real-world deployment. To systematically bridge the sim-to-real gap, HyperSim is realized through three core pillars: high-fidelity environment synthesis, adversarial trajectory generation, and sim-and-real co-training. Collectively, these modules address domain discrepancies by enhancing visual fidelity, expanding data coverage, and enforcing domain-invariant representations. We rigorously validate HyperSim through a large-scale empirical study involving 400 real-world task executions across two representative manipulation models. Assessed across three fine-grained metrics, our complete pipeline achieves remarkable sim-to-real success rates of 80% and 95% with ACT and π_{0}, respectively. Furthermore, policies trained on our adversarial trajectories exhibit significantly enhanced robustness against dynamic uncertainties, achieving a 35% higher completion rate under physical perturbations.

</details>


### [151] [L-Learning : A Lyapunov-Based Approach Leveraging Lagrangian Mechanics for Efficient and Stable Robot Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26648)
*Quan Quan,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: L-Learning 是一种新型机器人控制框架，它通过从数据中学习能量函数，在保证系统稳定性和高样本效率的同时，提升了复杂的轨迹跟踪控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制方法在动态或不确定环境下性能下降，而现有的数据驱动方法虽适应性强，却面临样本效率低及缺乏严格稳定性保证的问题。

Method: 提出 L-Learning 框架，将李雅普诺夫稳定性理论（Lyapunov stability theory）与拉格朗日力学（Lagrangian mechanics）集成，通过从数据中显式学习系统的能量函数来实现控制优化。

Result: 该方法在保证闭环反馈稳定性的同时，实现了更优的轨迹跟踪性能，并显著提升了学习过程中的样本效率。

Conclusion: L-Learning 通过将物理演化规律与数据驱动学习相结合，为实际机器人应用提供了一种兼具高控制精度、理论稳定性保证和高样本效率的有效框架。

Abstract: This paper presents L-Learning, a novel data-driven control framework for robotics that integrates Lyapunov stability theory with Lagrangian mechanics to enhance trajectory tracking performance. While traditional control methods often suffer from performance degradation in dynamic and uncertain environments, data-driven approaches, while more adaptable, are frequently limited by high sample complexity and a lack of rigorous stability guarantees. L-Learning mitigates these challenges by explicitly learning the system's energy function from data, thereby optimizing performance while ensuring closed-loop stability intrinsically. Characterized by superior control accuracy, theoretical stability guarantees, and high sample efficiency, L-Learning represents a promising solution for practical robotic applications.

</details>


### [152] [On the Generalization Capabilities, Design Choices and Limitations of Keypoint Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26649)
*Thomas Lips,Marco Moletta,Michael C. Welle,Danica Kragic,Francis wyffels*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过系统评估关键点模仿学习（KIL），证明了利用视觉基础模型提取关键点是提升机器人操纵数据效率的有效手段，其泛化性能优于RGB基准并与现有先进扩散模型持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RGB的模仿学习在处理未见过的物体或场景时泛化能力较差，而视觉基础模型为提取鲁棒的关键点表示提供了可能性，但目前尚不明确如何最优地将这些模型集成到模仿学习中，以及其表现相较于其他表示方法的优劣。

Method: 结合了现有的关键点模仿学习（KIL）方法，通过超过2000次的真实世界部署实验，探究了关键点提取的设计选择与集成方式，并扩展了该方法以处理多物体实例任务。

Result: 在五个任务上实现了75%的总体成功率，显著高于RGB基准（47%），并达到与S2-diffusion相当的表现（73%）。实验验证了其泛化能力，同时也揭示了其对基础模型依赖的局限性。

Conclusion: KIL是一种数据高效的机器人学习方法，能够显著优于RGB基准，但在性能上与S2-diffusion相当，且其表现受限于所使用的视觉基础模型。

Abstract: RGB-based imitation learning requires many demonstrations to generalize to unseen objects or scenes, motivating research into intermediate representations to improve generalization for robotic manipulation. Visual foundation models enable one-shot extraction of keypoints to provide such representation. However, it remains unclear how to integrate them into imitation learning optimally and when they outperform alternative representations. We combine approaches from previous works on keypoint imitation learning (KIL) and investigate several design choices to provide practical guidelines. Using over 2000 real-world rollouts, we also assess the generalization capabilities of KIL to unseen objects and scene variations. KIL achieves a 75% overall success rate across five tasks, significantly outperforming the RGB baseline (47%) and performing on par with S2-diffusion (73%). Finally, we explore the limitations of the foundation models used for keypoint extraction and extend KIL to tasks with multiple object instances. Our results confirm KIL as a data-efficient approach for robot learning, though it does not outperform alternative representations and inherits limitations of the foundation models used for keypoint extraction. All rollout videos, demonstrations, and results are available at https://kil-manipulation.github.io/.

</details>


### [153] [SteelDS: A High-Resolution Video Dataset of E40 Steel Scrap for Object Detection and Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26682)
*Melanie Neubauer,Christian Rauch,Gerald Koinig,Alexia Tischberger-Aldrian,Roland Pomberger,Elmar Rueckert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提供了一个用于工业废料自动化分选的高分辨率视频数据集，支持材料分类、目标检测和实例分割任务，旨在解决钢铁流中铜杂质剔除的自动化难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业废料回收中，从钢材流中剔除铜杂质（铜污染）通常依赖人工，效率低下；亟需高质量数据集以训练机器学习模型实现自动化分选。

Method: 在受控实验室环境下，通过输送带模拟工业后磁选过程，采集视频数据，并提供包含像素级分割掩膜和材料类别分类的精确标注。

Result: 构建了一个包含24,297帧标注数据、覆盖396个钢件和101个铜件对象的数据集，涵盖了不同尺寸、间距和密度，能有效模拟真实工业分选场景。

Conclusion: 该数据集为工业级废料分类自动化提供了高质量的标准基准，有助于推动机器学习模型在复杂金属分选领域的应用，从而减少对人工干预的需求。

Abstract: This dataset provides high-resolution, annotated video sequences of shredded E40-grade steel and copper scrap on a conveyor belt. Captured in a controlled laboratory environment, the data reflects the industrial post-magnetic sorting stage, where manual intervention is typically required to remove copper contaminants. The dataset comprises 24,297 labeled frames across five subsets, featuring 396 steel and 101 copper objects categorized by size. It supports the development of machine learning models for material classification, object detection, and instance segmentation. Variations in object spacing and density are included to simulate realistic industrial sorting conditions. Ground truth annotations include pixel-wise segmentation masks and material classes. This dataset serves as a benchmark for evaluating automated sorting algorithms aiming to identify copper impurities within complex, heterogeneous steel scrap streams.

</details>


### [154] [Look Further: Socially-Compliant Navigation System in Residential Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26710)
*Akira Shiba,Marina Obata,Nathan Kau,Zoltan Beck,Rishi Shah,Michael Sudano,Sabrina Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对室内配送机器人，本文提出了一种超视距（8米外）的“主动换道”社交导航模式，实验证明该方法在直道场景下能有效提升用户的整体感知体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的社交导航通常局限于短距离的个人空间维护，但在更远距离进行提前反应可能进一步改善人机交互体验，特别是在室内长直道场景中。

Method: 提出了一种“主动换道 (Proactive Lane-Changing, PLC)”运动模式，使机器人能够在距行人8米处即开始侧向避让；通过包含42名参与者的用户实验，对比了PLC与传统短距离反应式导航（减速、停止、避障）在不同场景下的表现。

Result: 在直道环境中，PLC模式在安全性、平滑性和礼貌性三个维度上均显著优于传统反应式导航；但在盲区交叉路口，不同导向表现无显著统计学差异。

Conclusion: 在走廊等室内环境下，大范围（如8米以上）的提前社交导航比传统的短距离避障策略更能显著提升人类对机器人运动的安全性、平滑性和礼貌性感知。但在不可见转角（盲区）场景中，预判策略的优势并不显著，用户偏好具有多样性。

Abstract: The distance at which a mobile robot reacts to a person strongly impacts various qualities of the human-robot interaction. In this paper, we focus on the navigation of a mobile delivery robot platform in a residential indoor hallway environment. Social navigation methods typically focus on avoiding uncomfortable human-robot interactions, such as when a robot encroaches on someone's personal space. Since personal space has been shown to be in the range of just a few meters, social navigation methods typically focus on deconflicting and resolving these short-range interactions. In this work, however, we demonstrate that by extending the reaction distance to over eight meters, far beyond the typical interaction distance, we can improve the human's perception of the robot's motion. We introduce the Proactive Lane-Changing (PLC) motion pattern and a navigation system that leverages it to react to people at an increased distance. This pattern consists of changing the robot's lateral position as it navigates down the hallway from the center to the side at an eight-meter distance from an oncoming person.
  We conducted a user study with 42 participants to assess their impressions of the delivery robot based on three service objectives: safety, smoothness, and politeness. In the straight hallway scenario (Frontal Approach), results showed significant improvement in each of these three objectives compared to typical motion patterns found in the literature: slowing down, stopping, and reactive collision avoidance in the proximity of a person. In contrast, in the intersection (Blind Corner) scenarios, none of the approaches performed significantly better than any other, with participants having a diverse range of preferences among robot motion patterns.

</details>


### [155] [Manipulating Tangible Virtual Object Dynamics to Promote Learning of Precision Force Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26782)
*Alberto Garzás-Villar,Alba Riera-Cardona,Alexis Derumigny,J. Micah Prendergast,Jane Murray Cramm,Laura Marchal-Crespo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究利用虚拟现实中的非线性触觉交互来训练精细力控制，发现反对称高斯模型能提高训练精度，但受试者主要依赖位移感知而非力感知，导致跨任务迁移能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决中风康复中被忽视的精细力产生训练问题，探讨如何利用触觉力反馈设备结合虚拟现实技术，通过操纵虚拟对象的动力学属性来提升用户的精细力控制能力。

Method: 招募了50名健康受试者进行虚拟冰壶任务，通过操纵虚拟弹簧的力-位移关系（线性函数、高斯函数和反对称高斯函数）来训练受试者的目标力产生精确度。同时，采用心理评估量表分析了人格特质对任务表现及探索行为的影响。

Result: 反对称高斯（AS-Gaussian）组在训练期间表现出最高的力控制精度；人格特质（如“自由精神”和“挑战转化”）显著影响任务探索行为；然而，不同弹簧模型在长期保持效果上无显著差异，且受试者更倾向于依赖位移（本体感觉）而非力觉，导致在改变刚度后的迁移任务中表现受限。

Conclusion: 通过调节力反馈装置的渲染动力学（尤其是非线性函数）可以有效训练力控制精度，但目前受试者过度依赖本体感觉（位移而非力）导致任务迁移能力受限。未来需进一步优化以增强力控制的泛化能力，再应用于中风后康复。

Abstract: Robotic haptic devices combined with virtual reality offer novel opportunities to train fine force generation, an essential yet overlooked component of post-stroke rehabilitation. This study proposes that manipulating the rendered dynamics of tangible virtual objects can be leveraged to train precise force control while engaging the somatosensory system. We conducted an experiment with fifty healthy participants who performed a curling-inspired task in which they had to stretch a virtual spring to generate a target release force to propel the stone to a predefined location on the ice sheet. During training, the spring's force-elongation relationship was modeled as either a linear or non-linear function, i.e., a Gaussian or antisymmetric Gaussian (AS-Gaussian) function with zero derivative at the release target force. Results indicate that the AS-Gaussian group consistently achieved higher force accuracy during training than the linear group, while the Gaussian group only outperformed the linear group toward the end of training. Analysis of personality traits revealed that higher Free Spirit scores were associated with poorer performance and reduced task exploration under Gaussian dynamics, whereas higher Transform-of-Challenge scores correlated with increased exploration. Despite these training effects, no significant differences in long-term retention were found across spring types or personality traits. Participants primarily relied on learned target elongation rather than target force, as evidenced by performance in a transfer task with a different stiffness but the same target force. While promising for somatosensory neurorehabilitation, these methods require refinement to reduce reliance on proprioceptive cues before testing with neurological patients.

</details>


### [156] [Can VLA Models Learn from Real-World Data Continually without Forgetting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26820)
*Jiarun Zhu,Yijun Hong,Xiaoquan Sun,Zetian Xu,Mingqi Yuan,Zhiyong Wang,Wenjun Zeng,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对VLA模型在真实机器人任务中的“灾难性遗忘”问题，通过构建首个真实世界持续学习数据集进行实证研究，证明了经验回放的有效性并提出了部署建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在通用机器人领域前景广阔，但在现实世界中实现持续学习（在习得新技能的同时保留旧技能）仍缺乏深入研究，现有研究多局限于狭窄的仿真环境。

Method: 1. 构建了一个包含4个连续真实物理操作任务的数据集（涵盖刚体抓取、接触力控制及柔性物体折叠）；2. 对VLA模型在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘进行了全面评估；3. 系统性探究了经验回放（Experience Replay）在VLA任务中的实施因素。

Result: 研究发现VLA模型在学习异构真实世界演示数据时存在严重的灾难性遗忘现象；通过实验揭示了经验回放机制中影响模型性能的关键实施要素。

Conclusion: 本文是首个针对真实场景机器人持续学习的研究，证实了经验回放对减轻VLA模型灾难性遗忘的有效性，并为部署长生命周期机器人策略提供了关键的实践指导。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide a promising foundation for general-purpose robotics. However, their successful deployment in real-world scenarios requires the ability to continually acquire new skills while retaining previously learned behaviors. While pioneering research has studied the continual learning of VLA models in narrowly simulated environments, this challenge remains largely unexplored under realistic conditions. To address this limitation, we construct a real-world continual learning dataset comprising four sequential manipulation tasks, spanning rigid-object pick-and-place, contact-rich pressing, and deformable-object folding. Using this dataset, we conduct comprehensive experiments and find that VLA models suffer significant catastrophic forgetting when continually learning from heterogeneous real-world demonstrations. We then systematically evaluate experience replay and uncover key implementation factors that govern its success. In summary, this work provides the first empirical study of real-world continual VLA learning and offers practical guidance for deploying long-lived robot policies.

</details>


### [157] [Learning Compositional Symbolic Task Rules from Demonstrations with Inductive Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26828)
*Oleh Borys,Karla Stepanova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用归纳逻辑程序设计（ILP）进行层次化任务分解的学习框架，通过在该框架下推导和重用符号规则，提升了机器人任务学习的可解释性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的演示学习不仅需要捕捉任务执行过程，还需具备高层任务结构，以确保机器人任务表示的可检查性、可重用性和人类可解释性。

Method: 采用归纳逻辑程序设计（ILP），通过将复杂任务分解为不同抽象（本体）层次的一系列简单学习目标，从演示数据和先验知识中自动推导符号规则，并实现高层任务结构中已学习规则的重用。

Result: 在合成积木装配场景中，该方法学习到的抽象结构不仅具有良好的可解释性，而且对处理更复杂、包含未知对象的任务展现出了优秀的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 分解式归纳逻辑程序设计（Decomposed ILP）是实现任务级演示学习（LfD）的一种切实可行的方法，能够有效构建可解释且具有强泛化能力的机器人任务表示。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstration~(LfD) should capture not only how a task is executed, but also its high-level task structure that explains the demonstrated behavior. As robots become more autonomous, such task representations must be inspectable, reusable, and human-interpretable. To address this, we study how to represent and learn robotic tasks with inductive logic programming~(ILP) by decomposing a complex task into a series of simpler learning objectives at different abstraction (ontological) levels. The system infers symbolic rules from demonstrations and prior (domain) knowledge, and reuses learned rules when learning higher-level task structure. We evaluate the approach in a synthetic block-assembly scenario and show that the learned abstractions are interpretable and support strong generalization to harder, held-out tasks with unseen objects. These results provide preliminary evidence that decomposed ILP is a feasible approach to task-level LfD.

</details>


### [158] [A Bioinspired Underwater Robot with a Latch-Mediated Soft Bistable Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26936)
*Chongze Bi,Wenjie Wu,Zonghao Zuo,Li Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受螳螂虾等生物启发，作者设计了一种集成了锁扣机构的软体双稳态致动器，仅用单电机即可实现高效的水下推进与多模式机动，显著提升了小型水下机器人的运动表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统小型水下机器人受能源密度限制，而生物界的“锁扣介导弹簧驱动”（LaMSA）机制能够实现高效的能量存储与瞬时释放，但现有研究在小型结构中的复制与精确控制仍具挑战。

Method: 开发了一种生物启发的软体双稳态致动器，集成了单电机驱动的锁扣机构，并通过耦合鳍片结构实现水下推进与机动控制。

Result: 该系统实现了稳定的周期性拍动与精确转向，最大推力达到0.528 N，冲量为0.147 Ns，垂直位移达30 mm，并能通过调节鳍片角度实现垂直上升、前向斜行和侧向平移等多种机动动作。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种基于LaMSA原理的软体双稳态驱动器，为小型水下机器人提供了一种高效、紧凑的运动控制解决方案，具有在探测、环境监测和检查领域应用的潜力。

Abstract: Underwater robotics has advanced significantly over recent decades. however, the development of miniaturized underwater robots remains limited by low energy densities of traditional power sources. Nature offers compelling solutions-organisms like mantis shrimps and fleas utilize latch-mediated spring actuation (LaMSA) systems that achieve rapid movements through a decoupled energy storage and release mechanism. Despite extensive studies of LaMSA, replicating such rapid, asymmetric actuation within simple, compact structures remains challenging. In this work, we introduce a bioinspired, soft bistable actuator with an integrated latch mechanism that enables asymmetric energy input and release using a single motor. Coupled with fin structures, this design facilitates efficient underwater propulsion and maneuverability. Experimental results demonstrate stable periodic flapping, precise steering, and a maximum thrust of 0.528 N, impulse of 0.147 Ns, and vertical displacement of 30 mm. By modulating fin angles, the robot achieves versatile motions, including vertical ascent, diagonal forward movement, and lateral translation. This study presents a novel, energy-efficient approach for controlling motion in compact underwater robots, paving the way for advanced biomimetic designs with potential applications in exploration, environmental monitoring, and inspection.

</details>


### [159] [Object Pose and Shape Estimation for Grasping: Does it Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26944)
*Pavan Karke,Kushal Shah,Gaurav Singh,Md Faizal Karim,K Madhava Krishna,Rajat Talak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究对比了模块化抓取方案（基于姿态/形状估计采样）与端到端抓取合成方法，证明了模块化方案在小物体抓取和通用性上表现更优，并展示了其在视觉语言模型辅助下实现语言引导抓取的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨当前物体姿态和形状估计技术的成熟度是否足以支撑一种比端到端抓取合成更有效、更具泛化能力的模块化抓取方案。

Method: 研究对比了三种模块化抓取方法（基于编码-解码器或扩散模型进行姿态/形状估计，随后进行对拓抓取采样）与一种最先进的端到端抓取合成方法，并在单视角RGB-D输入条件下进行了定量评估与失效模式分析。此外，将视觉语言模型整合进模块化流程以实现语言引导抓取。

Result: 模块化方法在所有实验中均优于端到端方法，能在端到端方法失效的小物体场景下成功生成抓取。此外，该方法通过结合视觉语言模型，在语言引导抓取任务上达到了与现有最先进基线（LERF-TOGO）相当的性能。

Conclusion: 模块化方法（先进行姿态/形状估计再采样）在物体抓取任务上超越了端到端方法，特别是在处理小物体时优势明显。该方法的有效性高度依赖于姿态与形状估计的准确性，在复杂场景中存在一定局限。同时，通过引入视觉语言模型，该框架能够实现有效的语言引导下的抓取。

Abstract: The problem of object pose and shape estimation has seen key advancements lately. Encoder-decoder (e.g., SAM3D, LRM, CRISP) and diffusion-based models (e.g., InstantMesh, Zero123, SceneComplete) have shown category-agnostic shape encoding capacity and open-set generalizability. In this work, we ask the question: Are the object pose and shape estimation methods mature enough, such that when used with antipodal grasp sampling, can outperform the end-to-end grasp synthesis methods? We explore this question in detail by scoping our study to parallel jaw grippers, 7-DoF grasps, and single-view RGB(-D) image as input. We implement and compare a state-of-the-art, end-to-end grasp synthesis method and three modular methods, which first estimate the object pose and shape for all objects in the scene, and generate grasps using antipodal sampling. We observe that the modular methods outperform the end-to-end method in all our experiments. The modular methods are able to synthesize plenty of grasps, even for small objects, where the end-to-end methods fail. The effectiveness of the modular methods is contingent on the accuracy of the pose and shape estimation, and suffers partial degradation in cluttered scenes - a limitation of the existing pose and shape estimation methods. We also analyze the failure modes and run-times for the three modular methods, which use two different ways of object pose and shape estimation: one based on an encoder-decoder model, while another a diffusion model. Finally, we demonstrate that the single-view object pose and shape estimation methods can be augmented with vision-language models to yield language-conditioned grasps from just single-view RGB-D image as input. We notice comparable performance to the state-of-the-art LERF-TOGO baseline.

</details>


### [160] [Trust, Geometry, and Rules: A Credibility-Aware Reinforcement Learning Framework for Safe USV Navigation under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26974)
*Yuhang Zhang,Shuqi Chai,Yukang Zhang,Liusha Yang,Mingchuan Zhang,Wei Wang,Qingjiang Shi,Quanbo Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对USV在不确定感知环境下的自主导航难题，本文提出了结合可信度评估、几何安全防护与连续化规则建模的强化学习新框架，显著提升了导航的可靠性与避碰合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态海事环境中，USV的自主导航面临感知系统不确定性偏差和国际海上避碰规则（COLREGs）带来的强化学习目标不连续性问题，导致现有RL算法难以稳定收敛及保证安全性。

Method: 1. 提出可信度加权价值学习（CW-VL），通过动态信任因子调节Critic损失，缓解感知噪声带来的过拟合；2. 提出协方差膨胀速度障碍法（CI-VO），将位置估计的不确定性转化为几何安全边界，规避危险探索行为；3. 提出风险感知COLREGs职责嵌入，将离散的交通规则转化为连续信号，平滑策略学习。

Result: 仿真实验证明，该框架在处理感知偏差时具有更高的鲁棒性，并在碰撞规避性能和COLREGs规则合规性方面均优于现有基准方法。

Conclusion: 该框架通过引入置信度感知学习、几何安全防护和连续规则嵌入，有效解决了动态海事环境下USV因感知不确定性和规则不连续性导致的导航失效问题，显著提升了训练鲁棒性及避障合规性。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation of Unmanned Surface Vehicles (USVs) that is safe and compliant with the International Regulations for Preventing Collisions at Sea (COLREGs) remains a formidable challenge in dynamic maritime environments, particularly when perception systems exhibit miscalibrated uncertainty. Existing Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based methods often falter because state-estimation errors induce unreliable belief states that mislead the value function, while discrete traffic rules introduce discontinuity in the learning objective. To address these challenges, we propose a framework integrating credibility-aware learning, geometric safety shielding, and continuous rule-aware embedding. First, Credibility-Weighted Value Learning (CW-VL) introduces a dynamic trust factor derived from the discrepancy between filter-estimated covariance and empirical error statistics to modulate the critic's heteroscedastic loss, preventing policy overfitting to noisy samples. Second, the Covariance-Inflated Velocity Obstacle (CI-VO) maps position-estimation uncertainty into set-wise angular margins, forming a conservative geometric shield that overrides hazardous exploratory actions. Third, Risk-Aware COLREGs Duty Embedding relaxes binary encounter duties into continuous rule-aware signals, providing smooth sector-transition information and suppressing oscillation from sparse rule rewards. Simulated encounter studies demonstrate improved training robustness against perceptual inconsistency and superior collision avoidance and COLREGs compliance over baselines.

</details>


### [161] [Towards Shared Embodied Intelligence in Humanoid Robots through Optimization Development and Testing of the Human Aware ergoCub Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26991)
*Carlotta Sartore,Mohamed Elobaid,Lorenzo Rapetti,Giulio Romualdi,Stefano Dafarra,Nicola A. Piga,Ines Sorrentino,Paolo Maria Vicecone,Silvio Traversaro,Ugo Pattacini,Luca Fiorio,Francesco Draicchio,Giovanna Tranfo,Lorenzo Natale,Marco Maggiali,Daniele Pucci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合“共享智能”与“具身认知”的机器人架构，通过以人类工效学为优化指标来设计人形机器人（如 ergoCub）的硬件与控制策略，从而增强机器人与人类进行安全、有效物理协作的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人机协作机器人设计往往缺乏对人类身体特征和认知特性的深度融合。为了使人形机器人能安全且高效地与人协作，迫切需要一种既能通过硬件适配人类需求，又能通过感知与运动控制模拟人类协作逻辑的“共享具身智能”设计方法。

Method: 1. 提出了一套统一的架构，将共享智能（对他人动作的内在表征）与具身认知（身体与环境的优化适配）相结合。2. 将人机交互建模为机器人硬件配置的函数，利用人类工效学指标优化机器人的结构设计与物理控制参数。3. 实现并验证了人形机器人 ergoCub。

Result: 研发了专门针对人机协作优化的 ergoCub 人形机器人，实验证明该架构能够通过将人类运动智能嵌入机器人物理层，实现以人为本的协作能力，在提升协作效率的同时兼顾人体工程学需求。

Conclusion: 该研究通过 ergoCub 机器人证明了将“共享智能”与“具身认知”集成于人形机器人设计中的可行性。该框架为工业和辅助机器人领域提供了以人类工效学为核心的设计范式，能够显著提升人机协作的安全性和有效性。

Abstract: Collaboration is central to human behavior, enabling tasks beyond individual capability. This ability arises from coordinating actions through internal representations of others, a concept known as shared intelligence. Additionally, humans are characterized by physical bodies and cognitive abilities that are optimized in response to their environment, a phenomenon referred to as embodied cognition. Designing humanoid robots that collaborate safely and effectively with people requires unifying these principles. Here we propose an architecture that integrates shared intelligence and embodied cognition to enable robots to physically collaborate with humans, where robot hardware and control are optimized for human metrics, using representations of the human body and motion intelligence. The ultimate goal is to achieve a form of shared embodied intelligence. Specifically, our architecture optimizes robot hardware and physical intelligence parameters with respect to human ergonomic metrics. This is accomplished by modeling human-robot interaction as a function of hardware configurations and embedding human models into the robot's physical intelligence. As a concrete implementation, we present the humanoid robot ergoCub, whose morphology and control have been optimized for collaborative tasks with humans. Our approach provides a framework for designing humanoid robots that prioritize human ergonomics at both the hardware and physical intelligence levels, with applications in industrial and assistive robotics.

</details>


### [162] [TPS-Drive: Task-Guided Representation Purification for VLM-based Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27038)
*Jiaxiang Li,Yumao Liu,Ke Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对 VLM 在自动驾驶领域面临的空间语义与 3D 几何精确度难以平衡的问题，TPS-Drive 提出了任务引导的空间净化机制，通过剔除环境冗余、聚焦核心智能体特征，显著提升了驾驶规划的安全性与预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在自动驾驶应用中存在两难困境：文本对齐方法导致空间失真与“空间幻觉”；稠密视觉方法则因背景冗余导致“表征干扰”。研究旨在建立一种既保留空间拓扑又精简冗余信息的表征框架。

Method: 1. 提出“基于智能体（Agent-Centric）的 Tokenizer”，利用任务引导的向量量化机制（受冻结的 3D 检测头监督），剥离背景冗余，增强动态智能体特征。2. 构建“解耦推理流水线”，将场景理解、未来预测和动作生成分阶段处理。3. 采用“三阶段渐进式训练范式”，并引入奖励驱动的微调以超越单纯的模仿学习。

Result: 在 nuScenes 开环评估中提升了智能体状态预测精度并降低了碰撞率；在 NAVSIMv1 和 NAVSIMv2 闭环基准测试中创下新的安全性记录。

Conclusion: TPS-Drive 通过任务引导的表征净化（Task-Guided Representation Purification）有效解决了 VLM 在自动驾驶中面临的“空间幻觉”和“表征干扰”问题，在 nuScenes 和 NAVSIM 基准测试中实现了卓越的空间预测精度和安全性，证明了该方法在复杂驾驶场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) provide a promising foundation for autonomous driving planning, yet bridging semantic reasoning and precise 3D spatial forecasting remains a critical challenge. Existing representation strategies generally follow two paths: text-aligned methods flatten continuous spatial states into symbols, which compromises geometric structure and induces "spatial hallucinations"; dense visual methods preserve spatial topology but overwhelm standard tokenizers with redundant background textures, leading to "representation interference". To address these limitations, we introduce TPS-Drive, a novel framework centered on Task-Guided Representation Purification that empowers VLMs to Think in Purified Space. At its core, an Agent-Centric Tokenizer utilizes a task-guided vector quantization mechanism supervised by a frozen 3D detection head, which explicitly reallocates limited codebook capacity from pervasive static backgrounds to critical dynamic agents and effectively isolates spatial redundancy. Leveraging this purified spatial vocabulary, TPS-Drive employs a decoupled reasoning pipeline that sequentially performs scene understanding, future forecasting, and action generation. The framework is optimized via a progressive three-stage training paradigm, culminating in reward-driven refinement that surpasses pure imitation learning. Extensive experiments validate our approach: TPS-Drive achieves accurate agent spatial state forecasting and reduces collision rates in open-loop nuScenes evaluations, while establishing new safety records on the rigorous closed-loop NAVSIMv1 and NAVSIMv2 benchmarks.

</details>


### [163] [Learning to Balance Motor Thermal Safety and Quadrupedal Locomotion Performance with Residual Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27046)
*Yuhang Wan,Weixian Lin,Letian Qian,Yiqi Zou,Weiwei Wu,Shengwei Wu,Chuanlin Zhao,Xin Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将全身热模型嵌入强化学习的两阶段框架，通过引入残差策略实时调整运动行为，显著提升了四足机器人在高负载下的热稳定性和长时运动性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动四足机器人电机过热是限制其在高负载、长时作业场景下运动能力的关键瓶颈，现有研究往往忽视了电机热管理。

Method: 提出了一种两阶段强化学习训练框架：第一阶段为预训练基准运动策略；第二阶段基于全身热模型，训练一个残差策略，根据电机热状态输出修正动作，以防止过热。

Result: 仿真和实机（Unitree A1）测试表明，该策略在负载3kg的情况下稳定运行时间超过13分钟，而仅使用基准策略则在大约5分钟后因过热停止。

Conclusion: 将热模型集成到强化学习中进行热管理是可行的，通过残差策略可以有效地在保证运动性能的同时延长四足机器人的持续作业能力。

Abstract: Motor thermal management is often overlooked in the context of electrically-actuated robots, particularly legged robots, but motor overheating is a key factor that limits long-duration locomotion especially under payload conditions. This paper integrates a whole-body thermal model of a quadruped robot into the reinforcement learning pipeline to update motor temperatures, and proposes a two-stage training framework for motor thermal management. In this framework, a nominal policy is first pre-trained as a locomotion baseline capable of traversing diverse terrains. A residual policy is then trained on top of the nominal policy to provide corrective actions based on the robot's thermal state, ensuring high performance under low-temperature conditions and preventing motor overheating under high-temperature conditions. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed policy achieves an effective balance between motor thermal safety and locomotion performance. Real-world experiments on a Unitree A1 quadruped robot further validate the approach: under a 3 kg payload, the robot achieves stable locomotion across multiple terrains for over 13 minutes, while the nominal policy alone leads to motor overheating in about 5 minutes.

</details>


### [164] [VR-DAgger: Immersive VR for Dexterous Data Collection and Uncertainty-Guided On-Policy Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27114)
*René Zurbrügg,Tifanny Portela,Arjun Bhardwaj,Aravind Elanjimattathil Vijayan,Maximum Wilder-Smith,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人模仿学习中数据收集成本高、专家资源浪费的问题，提出优化专家干预策略，通过聚焦关键故障点显著提升了学习效率与模型稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作学习方法严重依赖高质量演示数据的收集，成本高昂且效率低下；同时，模型在分布偏移下表现脆弱，且专家资源被浪费在重复的、低价值的纠正上。

Method: 提出了一种基于交互式学习的框架（推断），核心在于优化专家干预的时间分配，重点针对模型表现脆弱或关键的失败场景进行纠正，而非盲目收集任务执行数据。

Result: 减少了专家纠正冗余错误的时间，提升了机器人面对分布偏移时的稳健性，有效地解决了从演示中学习的瓶颈问题。

Conclusion: 通过针对关键失败案例（而非冗余数据）进行交互式纠正，可以显著提高从演示中学习机器人操作的效率并缓解分布偏移问题。

Abstract: Learning from demonstrations is effective for robotic manipulation, but collecting sufficient task-specific data remains a major bottleneck. Under distribution shift, small errors compound, performance degrades, and expert time is often spent on redundant, low-value corrections instead of the few critical failure cases.

</details>


### [165] [Towards Drone-based Mapping of Volcanic Gases using Gas Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27180)
*Marius Schaab,Niklas Karbach,Antonia Rabe,Thomas Wiedemann,Patrick Hinsen,Dmitriy Shutin,Thorsten Hoffmann,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无人机监测火山气体时旋翼下洗影响测量的问题，本文提出了一种集成拉格朗日模型的燃气层析成像方法，成功实现了火山气体排放的远程精确映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机旋翼产生的下洗气流（downwash）会分散火山气体羽流，导致机载原位传感器无法准确测量；同时火山气体监测对气候和灾害预测至关重要。

Method: 提出了一种新型的基于模型的燃气层析成像重建方法，该方法引入了拉格朗日模型来补偿风致平流效应。

Result: 在Salinelle dei Cappuccini泥火山的实验表明，原位传感器未能检测到CO2，而开放路径（open-path）遥感结合新提出的层析成像方法成功绘制了气体分布图，且与人工原位实测数据高度吻合。

Conclusion: 基于模型的燃气层析成像技术能够有效克服无人机旋翼下洗气流带来的干扰，实现对火山气体排放的精确映射。

Abstract: Volcanoes emit large amounts of CO2, directly influencing human lives. Mapping volcanic gas emissions helps to forecast eruptions and understand the impact of volcanoes on climate and the environment. Drone-based gas sensing significantly reduces risks in volcanic monitoring but faces technical limitations when measuring gas, as rotor downwash disperses the gas plume before detection. Gas Tomography using remote gas sensing addresses this challenge. At the Salinelle dei Cappuccini mud volcanoes, we demonstrate that while drone-mounted in-situ sensors failed to detect CO2 emissions due to aerodynamic disturbance, open-path sensing successfully enabled remote gas distribution mapping. We present a novel model-based gas tomographic reconstruction approach that incorporates a Lagrangian model to compensate for wind-induced advection. The resulting gas distribution maps align with manually collected in-situ measurements, confirming that model-based gas tomography effectively overcomes downwash limitations and enables accurate mapping of volcanic emissions.

</details>


### [166] [FineVLA: Fine-Grained Instruction Alignment for Steerable Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27284)
*Xintong Hu,Xuhong Huang,Jinyu Zhang,Yutong Yao,Yuchong Sun,Qiuyue Wang,Mingsheng Li,Sicheng Xie,Yitao Liu,Junhao Chen,Yixuan Chen,Yingming Zheng,Shuai Bai,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FineVLA通过引入细粒度语言指导（明确动作执行细节）弥补了现有VLA模型在指令遵循上的不足，实现了机器人作业在保持目标达成率的同时，大幅提升了行为的可控性和精确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人数据集大多仅提供粗粒度的目标级语言描述（如“拿起杯子”），缺乏关于执行细节的指导（如“用哪只手”、“从哪个方向 접근”），限制了机器人策略的可控性和视频理解能力。

Method: 提出了FineVLA框架，包含：(1) 构建了FineVLA-Data数据集（合并了近100万条轨迹，精炼出4.7万条人工验证的细粒度轨迹）；(2) 建立了一个包含视频、原子事实和VQA的基准测试集；(3) 开发了专门针对机器人领域的VLM自动化标注器；(4) 训练了一个支持细粒度与目标级指令混合输入的VLA策略模型。

Result: 1. 细粒度监督不仅能保持目标任务完成率，还能提升成功率（+1.4至+8.1个百分点）；2. 细粒度与粗粒度指令存在互补性，混合比例为1:2至1:1时效果最佳，真实环境下的成功率显著高于仅使用粗粒度指标（62.7 vs 49.9）；3. 在姿态、颜色、接近方向等需要细化控制的维度上，细粒度监督带来了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 细粒度语言指导应与目标级语言共同使用，不仅说明“做什么”，更要明确“如何做”。该研究证明了细粒度标注对于提升机器人策略的可控性和执行性能至关重要。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are increasingly expected to not only complete robot tasks, but also follow human instructions about how those tasks should be executed. However, existing robot datasets usually pair trajectories with coarse goal-level language, leaving execution-critical details such as active arm, approach direction, and contact region unspecified. This limits steerable policy learning and robotic video understanding. We introduce FineVLA, an open framework for action-aligned fine-grained VLA supervision. The framework includes: (1) a data construction tool that unifies 972,247 trajectories across 85K tasks from 10 open-source robot datasets and builds FineVLA-Data, a human-verified dataset of 47,159 fine-grained trajectories; (2) a held-out benchmark with 500 videos, 10,816 atomic facts, and 1,030 VQA questions; (3) a robotics-specialized VLM annotator for scalable fine-grained annotation; and (4) a steerable VLA policy trained with controlled mixtures of fine-grained and raw goal-level instructions. Our experiments yield three findings. First, fine-grained supervision does not sacrifice goal-level success: FG-only improves over Raw-only by +1.4 to +8.1 success-rate points across settings. Second, fine-grained and raw instructions are complementary, following a consistent inverted-U trend peaking at FG:Raw = 1:2 to 1:1. The best mixed setting reaches 86.8%/82.5% in RoboTwin simulation and 62.7/100 in real-world dual-arm manipulation (vs. 49.9 Raw-only). Third, fine-grained supervision improves steerable control: the largest real-world gains appear on pose (+23), color (+18), and approach direction (+18)--factors where goal-level instructions provide no guidance. Overall, fine-grained language should augment goal-level instructions: specifying how to execute alongside what to achieve. Project page: https://finevla.xlang.ai/

</details>


### [167] [Riding the Shifting Potential: When Reactive Control Suffices for Multi-Goal Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27314)
*Vito Mengers,Oliver Brock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用动态零空间投影和图世界模型的方法，解决了反应式控制在多目标任务中因冲突导致的局部极小值问题，在仿真与真实机器人实验中展现了卓越的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反应式控制因采用静态编码而无法处理动态多目标任务中的冲突，导致系统容易陷入局部极小值。本文旨在克服这一局限，实现更灵活的冲突协调。

Method: 提出了一种基于图世界模型（graph-based world model）的方法，通过零空间投影（nullspace projection）解决冲突。具体而言，将低优先级目标的梯度投影到高优先级目标的零空间中，并且优先级是根据当前状态动态调整的。

Result: 在非凸障碍物导航和非凸物体平面推动任务中，该方法表现优异。在平面推动任务中达到了100%的成功率，显著优于基准方法（最速下降法0%，扩散策略55%），且能直接迁移至真实机器人。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过动态优先级排序和零空间投影，反应式控制可以有效解决由目标冲突引起的多目标任务局部极小值问题。该方法具有良好的泛化性和跨领域迁移能力，无需示范或重训练。

Abstract: Reactive control is often considered insufficient for multi-objective tasks because conflicting objectives give rise to local minima. We argue this limitation is not inherent but arises from static encodings that fail to reflect how objectives currently interact. We exploit the interaction structure encoded in a graph-based world model by extending it with nullspace projections: conflicts are resolved where they arise by projecting lower-priority gradients into the nullspace of higher-priority ones, with priorities determined continuously from the current state. We demonstrate this in two domains where conflicts between objectives are central: navigation around non-convex obstacles, where static potential fields fundamentally fail, and planar pushing of non-convex objects, where our method achieves $100\%$ success across one-hundred configurations versus $0\%$ for the steepest-descent baseline and ${\sim}55\%$ for diffusion policy, without demonstrations or retraining. The same formulation transfers directly to a real robot with additional perceptual and kinematic constraints, accommodating them through the same mechanism.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Credit-assigned Policy Gradient for Early Stage Retrieval in Two-stage Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26385)
*Haruka Kiyohara,Mihaela Curmei,Ariel Evnine,Shankar Kalyanaraman,Israel Nir,Ana-Roxana Pop,Nitzan Razin,Sarah Dean,Thorsten Joachims,Udi Weinsberg*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对大规模检索/推荐系统中 ESR 训练方差过大的问题，本文提出了一种 CA-PG 策略，通过边缘化候选集组合实现精确的信用分配，显著提高了训练的稳定性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传统的两阶段排序系统中，早期阶段排序器（ESR）的端到端强化学习训练面临挑战，特别是标准的策略梯度算法（V-PG）在候选集规模较大时存在严重的梯度方差爆炸问题。

Method: 提出 CA-PG（Credit-Assigned Policy Gradient）算法，通过对目标项在所有包含它的候选集中的选择概率进行边缘化处理（而非直接作用于整个候选集），从而实现高效的信用分配。

Result: 实验结果表明，与传统的 V-PG 相比，CA-PG 在使用 Plackett-Luce 模型时，对于大型候选集环境，显著提升了训练的收敛速度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新型的“信用分配”策略梯度（CA-PG）算法，有效解决了早期阶段排序器（ESR）端到端训练中方差爆炸的问题，在处理大规模候选集时展现出显著的收敛速度和训练稳定性优势。

Abstract: Large-scale search, recommendation, and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically employ a two-stage architecture: an early-stage ranker (ESR) generates a candidate set, which is subsequently re-ranked by a late-stage ranker (LSR). While there are many reinforcement learning (RL) methods for training the LSR, end-to-end training of the ESR has proven challenging. In particular, naive application of "vanilla" policy gradient (V-PG) is not scalable for candidate-set sizes relevant for practical use due to exploding variance. This issue arises because V-PG propagates the gradient to the joint probability of the candidate sets, ignoring the contribution of each specific item in the candidate set to the reward. To mitigate this issue, we propose a novel "credit-assigned" policy gradient (CA-PG), which computes gradients with respect to the probability that the target item is chosen in any candidate set, i.e. marginalizing over all candidate sets that contain it. Our theoretical analysis reveals that CA-PG significantly reduces the variance of V-PG by marginalizing over the specific composition of the candidate set, while preserving the ability to learn the correct ranking of items under a reasonably aligned LSR policy. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world data demonstrate that CA-PG improves the convergence speed and training stability for ESRs utilizing the canonical Plackett-Luce model, especially when the candidate-set size is large.

</details>


### [169] [Plans for Evaluating Structured Generative Search Summaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26400)
*Tetsuya Sakai,Jina Lee,Hanpei Fang,Young-In Song*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文为大型语言模型生成的结构化搜索摘要提出了一套评估框架，涵盖概览、章节化内容及引用溯源，意在解决此类生成式搜索内容的评估难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大型语言模型生成并置于自然搜索结果之上的“结构化搜索摘要”缺乏有效评估手段的问题，旨在建立一套客观的评估标准。

Method: 提出一套包含概览、带标题章节以及引用源文档列表的结构化摘要评估框架，并制定了后续的实施与评估计划。

Result: 提出了评估框架的构思及其实施评估的规划路径。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个用于评估搜索引擎顶端结构化生成式摘要的框架，为衡量大模型在搜索场景下的表现提供了标准化参考。

Abstract: We propose a framework for evaluating structured generative search summaries that are placed atop organic web search results. A structured summary, generated by a large language model, typically consists of an overview, several sections with section titles, and a list of source documents that are cited within the summary. We then describe our plans for implementing and evaluating the framework.

</details>


### [170] [Uniboost: Global Coordination with Value Alignment for Fair and Efficient Traffic Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26424)
*Ge Fan,Nan Zhao,Kai Meng,Cong Luo,Yang Fu,Huiping Chu,Jialin Liu,Yuning Jiang,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Uniboost 框架，通过引入后验价值对齐和独立线性提升机制，解决了工业级推荐系统在重排序阶段面临的分配策略耦合、缺乏可解释性等痛点，实现了更精准、可控的流量分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统的重排序（Blending）阶段存在分配策略耦合严重、分数膨胀以及缺乏可解释性等问题，难以有效平衡多元业务目标。

Method: 提出名为 Uniboost 的统一流量分配框架，核心包含两个模块：1. 后验价值对齐机制（Posterior Value Alignment），将模型分数校准为具有明确业务语义的指标；2. 独立线性提升范式（Independent Linear Boosting），解耦复杂的加权方案，实现贡献度的精确归因。

Result: 线上 A/B 测试及深入分析显示：1. 降低加权分数总权重可减少业务干扰，提升分配效率；2. 事后分析提供宏观洞察；3. 提出的“有效完成分数”（Effective Completion Score）作为后验指标，为内容推荐提供了可靠的锚定点。

Conclusion: Uniboost 提供了一种高效、可控且具备业务解释性的交通分配解决方案，不仅提升了微观层面的推荐性能和流量分配效率，也为宏观层面的系统迭代提供了指导。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of internet services, recommendation systems have become indispensable. In particular, the blending (re-ranking) stage plays a pivotal role in allocating traffic across diverse business objectives. However, existing approaches often suffer from coupled allocation plans, score inflation, and a lack of interpretability. To address these challenges, we propose Uniboost, a unified traffic allocation framework. Uniboost introduces a posterior value alignment mechanism that calibrates abstract model scores to anchor metrics with explicit business semantics, significantly enhancing interpretability. Furthermore, it employs an independent linear boosting paradigm to decouple complex weighting schemes, enabling precise attribution of each plan's contribution. We validate the effectiveness of Uniboost through online A/B tests and in-depth data analysis, demonstrating three key findings: 1) Reducing the overall weight of weighted scores effectively mitigates unintended business interference, yielding a more efficient micro-level traffic allocation strategy; 2) Post-hoc analyses and aggregated dashboards provide intuitive, macro-level insights that guide the design of the overall traffic allocation mechanism; 3) The proposed "Effective Completion Score" serves as an easily obtainable post-metric that offers a reliable anchor for content recommendation pipelines. Collectively, our experiments show that Uniboost not only improves traffic allocation efficiency and recommendation performance at the micro level but also provides macro-level guidance for system iteration. Thus, this work provides an efficient and controllable traffic regulation solution for large-scale industrial recommendation systems.

</details>


### [171] [Is Position Bias in Dense Retrievers Built In-or Learned from Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26578)
*Daegon Yu,SeungYoon Han,Woomyoung Park*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了密集检索器的位置偏置深受训练数据中证据分布的影响，并提出通过平衡训练数据位置分布可作为缓解该问题的有效策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从模型架构层面解释密集检索器的位置偏置现象，但尚未深入探讨训练数据中证据的位置分布如何影响检索器的位置偏置方向。

Method: 构建了具有位置偏移（分别位于文档开头、中间、结尾）的合成训练集，对八种架构各异的预训练模型进行微调，并评估了位置偏差分布的影响及位置平衡训练的效果。

Result: 实验表明，训练数据的位置分布会诱导检索器产生相应的偏好。在受到位置偏移训练的模型中，偏置方向与训练数据位置一致；采用位置平衡的数据训练可使位置敏感度降低 57%-87%，同时保持竞争力。此外，微调能改变模型的偏好，但模型预训练的固有偏好仍有残留。

Conclusion: 训练数据中的位置分布是导致密集检索器产生位置偏置的关键可控因素；通过位置平衡的数据构建可以显著缓解检索器的位置偏置问题，且不会显著牺牲检索性能。

Abstract: Dense retrievers exhibit positional bias, favoring documents whose query-relevant information appears near the beginning and degrading retrieval performance when the information appears later. While prior work on positional bias in dense retrievers has largely focused on architectural explanations, we study how the positional distribution of evidence in training data affects retrieval-level bias direction. To test this, we construct synthetic position-targeted training sets in which query-relevant evidence appears at the beginning, middle, or end of documents, and fine-tune eight architecturally diverse pretrained models under position-skewed and balanced training distributions. At the ranking level, we observe a strong directional pattern across the examined models: skewed training distributions favor evidence at the corresponding positions. Position-balanced training reduces positional sensitivity by 57--87\% on position-aware benchmarks, with competitive mean retrieval performance in our controlled setting. Representation-level analyses further suggest that fine-tuning often reshapes learned positional preferences, although pre-existing architectural or pretraining-specific tendencies persist in some models. These results identify training-position distribution as a major controllable factor in retrieval-level position bias and suggest balanced data curation as a practical mitigation strategy.

</details>


### [172] [L2Rec: Towards Dual-View Understanding of LLMs for Personalized Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26717)
*Pingjun Pan,Tingting Zhou,Peiyao Lu,Tingting Fei,Hongxiang Chen,Chuanjiang Luo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 个性化推荐中行为与语义信号融合难的问题，L2Rec 通过 DPMoE 机制在参数层面实现双视图联合建模与自适应融合，显著提升了推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统利用 LLM 时在输入或输出层整合行为与语义信号存在分布鸿沟（distribution gaps）或信息利用不充分的缺陷，缺乏端到端的有效融合手段。

Method: 提出 L2Rec 框架，采用双视图个性化专家混合（DPMoE）机制，通过对 LLM 参数施加个性化的低秩扰动，同时实现行为与语义的适应，并配合自适应跨视图融合模块整合双视图输出。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验结果表明 L2Rec 表现优于现有 SOTA 模型，且在大型工业平台的在线 A/B 测试中显著提升了关键用户参与指标。

Conclusion: L2Rec 通过在参数层面统一行为与语义建模，解决了现有推荐系统在大语言模型适配中面临的分布差异和监督信息缺失问题，实验证明其在离线评测及工业在线落地中均具有显著优势。

Abstract: Adapting large language models (LLMs) for personalized recommendation requires aligning their general-purpose capabilities with user-specific preferences while effectively leveraging both behavioral and semantic signals. Existing approaches typically integrate these signals at either the input level (e.g., injecting behavioral embeddings into the token space) or the output level (e.g., contrastive alignment of separate encoders), suffering from distribution gaps or lack of end-to-end task supervision. In this work, we introduce L2Rec, which unifies behavioral and semantic understanding at the parameter level of LLMs. Our key insight is that the same set of Transformer parameters can serve as a shared medium for both views: by applying view-specific, personalized low-rank perturbations via a Dual-view Personalized Mixture-of-Experts (DPMoE) mechanism, L2Rec enables a single LLM backbone to produce complementary behavioral and semantic adaptations for each user with minimal representation-level misalignment. An adaptive cross-view fusion module further integrates the dual-view outputs into a unified user preference. Experiments on four datasets show that L2Rec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, and online A/B testing on a large-scale industrial platform validates significant improvements in key engagement metrics.

</details>


### [173] [RAGEAR: Retrieval-Augmented Graph-Enhanced Academic Recommender](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26819)
*Francesco Granata,Lorenzo Lamazzi,Misael Mongiovì,Francesco Poggi,Valeria Secchini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RAGEAR是一个结合了讲义内容文本语义检索与符号化知识图谱约束的学术课程推荐系统，通过图感知聚合算法实现了更精准的课程匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有课程推荐系统多依赖有限的课程元数据，忽视了具体的教学内容，导致推荐精度不足且缺乏灵活性。因此，作者旨在引入细粒度的教学内容分析，并结合学术环境中的逻辑约束（如学分、培养方案）来提升推荐质量。

Method: 采用神经符号（Neurosymbolic）方法：1. 结合基于密集检索的文本语义挖掘（利用Lecture transcripts）；2. 使用知识图谱（Knowledge Graph）进行符号逻辑约束（学分、先修课等）；3. 设计了一种图感知聚合函数（Graph-aware aggregation function），将检索到的片段级证据聚合为课程级评分。

Result: 在包含152个学生查询的评估中，RAGEAR在检索质量上优于仅基于元数据的方法，且相比基于转录本的标准化SumP基线，在top-rank推荐中表现更优。

Conclusion: RAGEAR通过结合细粒度课程讲义内容与符号知识图谱的逻辑约束，显著优于仅使用元数据的传统方法，证明了神经符号处理方法在学术课程推荐中的有效性。

Abstract: We present RAGEAR (Retrieval-Augmented Graph-Enhanced Academic Recommender), a neurosymbolic recommender system for academic course recommendation. RAGEAR combines dense retrieval over full lecture transcripts with a symbolic Knowledge Graph modelling courses, lessons, transcript chunks, credits, study plans, and curricular information. The Knowledge Graph supports symbolic filtering and contextualisation based on structured constraints, such as credits, academic disciplines, study plans, and prerequisites. Unlike metadata-based approaches, it exploits fine-grained instructional content by retrieving transcript chunks semantically aligned with a student's query. The main contribution is a graph-aware aggregation function that propagates chunk-level evidence to course-level recommendations. The score combines three factors: the share of retrieved similarity associated with a course, the rank-based strength of its relevant chunks, and the distribution of evidence across lessons. We evaluate RAGEAR on 152 student-like queries through a human evaluation sample and a large-scale LLM-based relevance assessment. Results show that lecture transcripts improve over metadata-only retrieval, and that RAGEAR further improves ranking quality over a transcript-based normalized SumP baseline, especially for top-ranked recommendations.

</details>


### [174] [ICICLE: Expanding Retrieval with In-Context Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26902)
*Yu-Chen Den,Yung-Yu Shih,Zhi Rui Tam,Kuan-Yu Chen,Pu-Jen Cheng,Yun-Nung Chen,Eugene Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对生成式检索在处理新文档时的重训练成本与灾难性遗忘问题，ICICLE 框架通过上下文学习（In-context Learning）机制，在推理阶段即可引入新文档，有效实现了增量式检索并保持了模型原有的检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的生成式检索（GR）在扩展语料库时需要更新模型参数，导致高昂的训练成本，并存在灾难性遗忘问题，无法适应实时更新的文档集合。

Method: 提出了 ICICLE 框架，将增量生成式检索转化为上下文检索问题。核心技术包括：1) 基于 [COPY] 的路由机制（区分参数化记忆与上下文信息）；2) 基于偏好的校准方法；3) 大上下文适配技术。

Result: 在 MS MARCO 和 NQ320K 数据集上的实验表明，ICICLE 在提升新文档检索能力的同时，能够完美保留对已训练文档的检索性能，且无需进行针对特定语料库的重新训练。

Conclusion: ICICLE 框架成功通过上下文学习解决了生成式检索在动态语料库扩展中的局限性，实现了在无需重训练模型的情况下，有效检索新文档并保持原有文档的检索性能。研究指出，路由失败是限制上下文生成式检索扩展性的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Generative retrieval (GR) maps queries directly to document identifiers (docids) using parametric knowledge, However, this design makes corpus expansion costly: adding new documents requires updating model parameters to encode new document-docid associations incurs repeated training and catastrophic forgetting of previously indexed documents. In this work, we revisit incremental GR as an in-context retrieval problem, where newly added documents are supplied as inference-time document-docid evidence. We propose ICICLE, an in-context indexing framework that performs source-aware docid generation over both parametric memory and context-provided document-docid pairs. ICICLE combines a `[COPY]`-based routing mechanism, preference-based calibration, and large context adaptation to distinguish context-grounded retrieval from parametric retrieval. Experiments on MS MARCO and NQ320K show that ICICLE improves retrieval of newly introduced documents while preserving seen-document retention without corpus-specific retraining. Our analysis further shows that high-shot degradation is mainly caused by routing failure, highlighting source-selection calibration as a key bottleneck for scaling in-context generative retrieval.

</details>


### [175] [The 2nd EReL@MIR Workshop on Efficient Representation Learning for Multimodal Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26941)
*Junchen Fu,Xuri Ge,Xin Xin,Alexandros Karatzoglou,Ioannis Arapakis,Xi Wang,Qijiong Liu,Qian Li,Joemon M. Jose*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对大型多模态模型在信息检索（MIR）场景下计算效率低的问题，提出通过举办MM 2026 EReL@MIR研讨会，集思广益，探索降低模型参数负担、提升落地效率的路径与标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大规模预训练多模态模型（如Qwen, LLaVA, CLIP）在MIR任务中表现优异，但其巨大的参数量导致训练、部署和推理阶段的效率低下，阻碍了其在工业界的广泛落地。

Method: 通过举办专题研讨会（Workshop）的形式，促进产学研交流，梳理新兴解决方案，探索针对大语言/多模态模型在MIR任务中的降本增效方法。

Result: 发起了MM 2026 EReL@MIR研讨会，建立了官方网站及交流平台，以推动解决模型应用中的核心效率问题。

Conclusion: 该研讨会（EReL@MIR）旨在汇聚学术界与工业界力量，共同探讨多模态信息检索（MIR）在大模型时代的效率瓶颈，并致力于建立新的评估指标与基准。

Abstract: Multimodal representation learning has attracted increasing attention in AI, driven by the strong performance of large, pretrained multimodal foundation models such as Qwen, LLaVA, and CLIP. These models deliver impressive performance on a range of multimodal information retrieval (MIR) tasks, including web search, cross-modal retrieval, and recommender systems. Yet their massive parameter counts create major efficiency bottlenecks when adapting their representations for IR tasks during training, deployment, and inference. These limitations hinder the practical use of foundation models for representation learning in information retrieval. To address these issues, we propose organizing the EReL@MIR workshop at MM 2026, bringing together researchers from academia and industry to discuss emerging solutions, open challenges, and new efficiency metrics and benchmarks for multimodal IR representation learning in the foundation-model era. The workshop's official website is available at https://erel-mir.github.io/.

</details>


### [176] [MuChator: Enabling Active Music Discovery via Conversational Music LLMs in Douyin Music](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27103)
*Jiahao Liang,Linzhi Huang,Xuannan Liu,Xukai Wang,Xuanpu Luo,Yongchun Zhu,Jingwu Chen,Feng Zhang,Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对音乐应用中主动式和情境化搜索意图表达不足的问题，MuChator 提出了一个基于 MusicLLM 的交互式框架，通过系统的知识增强、情境化指令微调和偏好对齐，实现了更智能的音乐发现体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抖音音乐现有的流媒体推荐模式限制了用户表达具体意图的能力；传统搜索难以处理模糊、情境化及口语化的音乐需求；现有通用大模型在音乐领域知识匮乏且缺乏个性化理解，难以支撑高质量的主动式音乐发现。

Method: 1. 音乐知识预训练：采用三阶段方案，依次注入客观知识、主观知识及个性化偏好。
2. 情境感知指令微调：构建自动合成流水线生成“用户-查询-音乐”三元组，实现多模态/语义指令与音乐意图的对齐。
3. 混合奖励模型偏好对齐：联合建模意图相关性、个性化偏好与基础约束，利用 GRPO 强化学习进行优化。

Result: MuChator 在主流工业音乐推荐数据集上性能超过包括 Gemini-3-Pro 在内的领先模型；在字节跳动旗下抖音音乐应用的 A/B 测试中，用户活跃天数提高了 46.49%。

Conclusion: MuChator 有效解决了传统流媒体平台在主动搜索与情境化意图表达方面的不足，通过多阶段知识注入、指令微调与混合奖励模型对齐，显著提升了音乐发现的交互体验与用户活跃度，并在工业级应用中验证了其实际落地的优越性。

Abstract: Douyin Music, a large-scale platform with millions of daily users, adopts an immersive, feed-based discovery paradigm, where users passively explore music through continuous recommendations. While effective for passive music discovery, this paradigm restricts users to recommendation results and provides limited support for explicitly specifying listening intents. Unlike conventional search, where users express well-defined intents through explicit queries such as specific songs or artists, real-world active music discovery is often situational and colloquial, involving vague or underspecified requests. While LLMs enable natural language interaction, their direct use in music discovery remains limited by insufficient music-domain knowledge, lack of music-query collaborative reasoning, and shallow understanding of personalized preferences. To address these challenges, we introduce MuChator, an interactive MusicLLM-based framework that enables users to actively express situational music intents in natural language. MuChator incorporates three key components: (1) Music Knowledge Pre-training, a three-stage scheme that incrementally injects objective music knowledge, subjective music knowledge, and personalized music preferences into LLMs; (2) Context-aware Instruction Tuning, which constructs high-quality user-query-music triplets through an automated synthesis pipeline to align LLMs with active and situational user intents; and (3) Preference Alignment with Hybrid RM, which jointly models intent relevance, personalized preferences, and basic constraints, and is optimized using GRPO-based reinforcement learning. Extensive evaluations on industrial music recommendation datasets demonstrate that MuChator outperforms leading proprietary models, such as Gemini-3-Pro. The model has been deployed on Douyin Music App within ByteDance, with 46.49\% improvement of user active days in online A/B test.

</details>


### [177] [Lost in the Evidence? Reproducing Document Position and Context Size Effects in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27105)
*Jorge Gabín,Anxo Perez,Javier Parapar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性的重现性研究，揭示了 RAG 系统中“丢失在中间”等现象受到主题抽样方差和评估协议的显著影响，证明理想化评估条件下的结论难以简单外推至实际检索管道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于 RAG 系统中检索文档排序和上下文窗口大小对模型性能影响的经验性研究结论不一致，且难以在不同模型、数据集和评估协议间重现。该研究旨在系统性地验证这些现象，识别偏见源，并探索在更真实场景下这些观察规律的适用性。

Method: 1. 针对“丢失在中间”（Lost in the Middle）现象进行再现性研究，并扩展至现代 LLM；2. 提出一种基于多次子集抽样的校准流程，以解决主题抽样带来的方差问题；3. 在非 oracle 检索（即存在检索误差）的真实 RAG 场景中进行评估；4. 系统分析由检索质量、模型选择、检索顺序和上下文窗口大小构成的多维度交互影响。

Result: 研究发现：1. 主题抽样是导致评估结果方差大的主要原因；2. 现代 LLM 仍存在明显的定位敏感性与位置偏差；3. 在引入真实检索器（非 oracle）后，由于评估指标选择（如 LLM 评判者限制）和主题覆盖不足，以往部分业界研究的结论存在偏差；4. 检索质量对顺序效应和上下文利用率有显著调节作用。

Conclusion: 在理想化设置中得出的结论并不总能直接转化为现实世界的 RAG 管道。检索顺序和上下文大小对下游性能的影响与检索质量及模型选择紧密相关。作者强调了评估协议的稳健性和数据覆盖度的重要性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems rely on retrieved documents being concatenated into a model's input context, making both document ordering and context size critical yet controversial design choices. Prior work reports position-based effects such as lost in the middle and related long-context phenomena. However, empirical findings remain inconsistent and hard to reproduce across models, datasets, and evaluation protocols. In this paper, we present a systematic reproducibility study that revisits these claims and examines how they evolve with contemporary LLMs under a controlled evaluation framework. We first show that topic sampling is a major source of variance: small topic sets can mask or exaggerate ordering effects. Based on repeated subset sampling across multiple topic budgets, we provide a practical calibration procedure that identifies topic counts yielding stable trends at feasible cost. Using these fixed topic sets, we then reproduce and extend results on position sensitivity, re-evaluating lost in the middle and positional biases in modern LLMs. Then, we also study a more realistic RAG scenario in which relevance is mediated by a retriever rather than oracle access to ground-truth documents. In this setting, we re-examine a recent industry study and identify discrepancies to evaluation choices such as limited topic coverage and reliance on LLM-based judges. Finally, we conduct an analysis of how retrieval order and context size affect downstream LLM performance under imperfect retrieval. Our results demonstrate that both factors interact strongly with retrieval quality and model choice, and that conclusions drawn from idealised setups do not always transfer to real-world RAG pipelines. We release all code and configurations to support reproducibility and future work on robust RAG evaluation.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Attributing the System's Overall Effect to its Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26643)
*Chenxi Wang,Lei Wang,Wanling Gao,Fanda Fan,Guoxin Kang,Hongxiao Li,Yuchen Su,Jianfeng Zhan*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 针对计算机系统整体性能难以拆解归因于单一组件的难题，本文提出了一种比传统行业基准（SPEC CPU2017）和通用实验方法更高效、更准确的严谨评估方法，能显著降低系统配置差异带来的结果波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的计算机系统是一个高度集成的整体，现有手段难以准确量化单一组件（如CPU）对整体系统性能的贡献。行业标准（如SPEC CPU2017）无法区分组件影响，且在不同配置下引入了巨大的不可控波动（最高可达436.80%），导致评估结果准确性极低。

Method: 作者提出了一种严谨的行为归因方法，通过理论分析与受控实验，在系统级评估中从整体效应中剥离并定位特定组件（如CPU）的性能贡献，并将其与SPEC CPU2017、实验设计（DoE）和随机对照试验（RCTs）进行了对比验证。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够以成本高效的方式实现组件级别的性能归因，相比之下，SPEC CPU2017存在严重的控制变量缺失问题，而DoE和RCTs在解决此类复杂系统特性评估时表现出明显的固有局限性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种能够有效解耦系统组件性能贡献的严谨方法，克服了传统基准测试（如SPEC CPU2017）和一般实验设计方法（如DoE、RCTs）在处理系统级组件评估时的局限性，为计算机组件的评估与设计提供了更可靠的衡量标准。

Abstract: In a computer system, multiple indispensable components-such as the CPU, memory, and others-work together with other essential components to produce an overall effect, which can only be measured on an independently running system. Since the system operates as an integrated whole, isolating the effect of individual components is challenging. Accurately attributing the system's overall effect to its specific component is crucial for both computer design and evaluation.
  Taking CPU evaluation as a benchmark, our experiments reveal that the general-purpose rigorous methodologies, like DoE, RCTs, can not address this issue efficiently; A single-purpose empirical methodology, SPEC CPU2017, which is the industry-standard CPU benchmark, only reports the overall effect. Even more concerningly, for the identical CPU, the undefined configurations of other indispensable components introduce uncontrolled variability, with the SPEC scores fluctuating from 12.16\% to 436.80\%.
  We propose a rigorous methodology that can attribute the overall effect to its specific component, which can be utilized in computer component evaluations and design, as well as in other areas. Through theoretical analysis and pioneering controlled experiments, we systematically compare our methodology against three established methodologies: SPEC CPU2017, DoE, and RCTs. The results show our methodology can achieve its goal in a cost-efficient way, while others exhibit inherent limitations.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Generalized Range Filtering Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search: Containment and Overlap [Technical Report]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26474)
*Yingfan Liu,Tong Wu,Jiadong Xie,Yang Zhao,Jeffrey Xu Yu,Jiangtao Cui*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对带范围-范围（RR）谓词的近似最近邻搜索问题（RRANN），提出了多段树图（MSTG）索引结构，在支持通用谓词的同时，显著提升了查询性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的近似最近邻（ANN）搜索方法（如 RFANN）无法支持具有任意范围-范围（RR）谓词的查询（如包含、重叠等关系），而此类需求在价格区间或时间间隔查询中应用广泛。

Method: 提出了一种新的索引结构——多段树图（Multi-Segment Tree Graph, MSTG）。该方法通过避免遍历不满足谓词的节点来高效处理任意 RR 谓词。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验表明，该方法在 RRANN 查询中实现了高达 12.5 倍的速度提升，且精度与基准方法相当；在其他变体（IFANN、TSANN）上的表现也优于当前主流方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的多段树图（MSTG）方法能够高效处理任意范围-范围（RR）谓词的近邻搜索，在保持与现有技术相当的索引大小和构建时间的同时，实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search with range filters has recently garnered significant attention. This paper delves into a generalized form of this problem, i.e., ANN search with exact range-range (RR) predicates on a range-valued attribute, named RR filtering ANN (RRANN). Specifically, given $n$ vectors in $\mathbb{R}^d$, each vector $v_i$ is associated with a numeric range $[l_i, r_i]$, symbolizing aspects like a price range or time interval. An RRANN query $(v_q, l_q, r_q)$ aims at finding $k$ vectors closest to $v_q$ within the vectors satisfying an arbitrary RR predicate defined between the query range $[l_q, r_q]$ and the object range $[l_i, r_i]$. The RR predicate remains unspecified, enabling user-defined conditions. It may encompass containment ($[l_i, r_i] \subseteq [l_q, r_q]$ or $[l_q, r_q] \subseteq [l_i, r_i]$), overlap ($l_i \le l_q \le r_i \le r_q$ or $l_q \le l_i \le r_q \le r_i$), or a disjunction of them. RRANN has broad applications in queries related to price ranges or time intervals, and it generalizes existing variants of ANN search with range filters. However, existing dedicated approaches for these problems lack the capacity to support queries with arbitrary RR predicates. Hence, we introduce a new approach, labeled multi-segment tree graph. It efficiently handles arbitrary RR predicates by avoiding traversal through non-predicate-satisfied nodes, and keeps equivalent index size and construction time to state-of-the-art methods for RFANN. Extensive experiments on real-world data demonstrate the efficacy of our approach in RRANN queries, achieving up to 12.5x speedups with the same accuracy as the baselines. Moreover, our approach attains comparable RFANN search performance and notably superior IFANN and TSANN search performance compared to the respective state-of-the-art approaches. Our code is available at https://github.com/FanEDG/MSTG.

</details>


### [180] [RT-RkNN: Reverse k Nearest Neighbor Queries as a Graphics Ray Casting Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26671)
*Zhengyang Bai,Peng Chen,Mohamed Wahib*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种利用GPU硬件光线追踪核心处理RkNN查询的新方法，通过将查询转化为光线投射问题，有效解决了传统空间索引在复杂查询条件下的性能衰减问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于空间索引（如R-tree）的RkNN算法在面对大规模用户、稀疏设施分布或大k值等复杂场景时，剪枝效率会显著下降，导致计算性能瓶颈。

Method: 将二维空间中的反向k近邻（RkNN）查询重构为计算机图形学中的“光线投射（ray-casting）”问题，将用户建模为光线，设施建模为几何基元；首次实现了在现代GPU专用光线追踪核心（RT Cores）上执行的算法和系统。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多种测试场景下均优于现有的前沿算法，特别是在传统剪枝策略效率低下的极端情况下，表现出极强的性能优势。

Conclusion: 通过将RkNN查询重构为光线投射问题并利用GPU硬件加速，本研究成功突破了传统空间索引（如R-tree）在处理大数据集、高密度用户或大k值场景下的性能瓶颈，提供了一种高效且具备良好扩展性的通用查询方案。

Abstract: Reverse k nearest neighbor (RkNN) queries are fundamental in spatial databases, location-based analytics, and recommendation systems. Existing state-of-the-art techniques rely on spatial pruning supported by R-trees and their variants. However, their pruning effectiveness degrades significantly in challenging scenarios where the number of facilities is small, the user population is dense, or the value of k is large. To overcome these limitations, this work reformulates the RkNN query problem in two-dimensional geometric spaces as a graphics ray-casting problem, where users are modeled as rays and facilities are represented as geometric primitives. Based on this formulation, the first algorithm and implementation exploiting dedicated hardware ray-tracing cores on modern GPUs are developed. This novel approach preserves strong filtering performance even for large values of k, dense user populations, and highly sparse facility distributions. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art algorithms across diverse settings, particularly in scenarios where traditional pruning strategies become inefficient.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Edge AI Deployment Beyond Models: A BSP-Aware Systems Framework for Industrial Embedded Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26119)
*Pitchai Muthu M*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出工业边缘AI部署应从单一模型开发转向系统工程思维，并提出了一个涵盖硬件到运维的五层框架，以解决嵌入式部署中的复杂性并提升实际生产可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工业边缘AI多以“模型优先”的开发模式，忽视了嵌入式平台（如长生命周期、供应商内核、异构加速器、安全约束）的系统复杂性，导致后期部署阶段出现性能与可靠性问题。

Method: 提出了一种面向BSP（板级支持包）的五层工业嵌入式平台部署框架：硬件层、BSP/操作系统适配层、运行时与加速层、应用/推理层以及运维/验证层。

Result: 建立了一套将底层平台工程与部署指标（如可诊断性、持续吞吐量、现场可靠性）直接挂钩的实践框架，并基于NXP i.MX、NVIDIA Jetson等行业主流架构进行了论证。

Conclusion: 工业边缘AI的部署不应仅视为模型封装，而必须将其作为涵盖硬件、BSP、运行时、推理和运维的完整系统工程问题来处理，以确保在复杂嵌入式环境下的可重复性、可靠性和性能。

Abstract: Industrial Edge AI programs often begin with the model and only later confront the platform. That sequencing is attractive because it allows early demonstrations, but it breaks down when the deployment target is an embedded system with long product lifecycles, vendor-specific kernels, heterogeneous accelerators, safety constraints, and nontrivial I/O paths. In that environment, a model is only one component of a larger execution chain that begins at the sensor, traverses the board support package (BSP), and ends in a production service loop. This paper argues that robust Edge AI deployment must be treated as a systems problem rather than a late-stage application packaging exercise. The paper presents a BSP-aware framework for industrial embedded platforms organized around five layers: hardware, BSP/operating-system adaptation, runtime and acceleration, application/inference, and operations/validation. The discussion is grounded in vendor architecture documentation for Android, NXP i.MX, NVIDIA Jetson, ONNX Runtime, and TensorRT, and in systems literature on embedded AI benchmarking, device instability, and heterogeneous edge fleets. The result is a practical framework that connects low-level platform work to measurable deployment outcomes such as reproducibility, diagnosability, sustained throughput, and field reliability.

</details>


### [182] [Semantic-aware Token Selection and Resource Optimization for Communication-efficient Split Federated Fine-tuning in Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26120)
*Xianke Qiang,Zheng Chang,Geyong Min*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对边缘设备部署大规模 Transformer 的通信与计算瓶颈，ST-SFLora 通过语义令牌选择与联合资源优化算法，有效降低了拆分联邦学习中的带宽占用与能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模视觉 Transformer 模型部署于边缘设备时，面临计算限制、联邦学习中本地微调压力大、以及拆分联邦学习（SFL）中高维激活令牌传输带来的通信开销过大等挑战。

Method: 提出基于语义令牌的拆分联邦学习框架 ST-SFLora；引入“语义传输效率”（STE）指标以平衡语义保留与传输成本；制定并求解联合资源优化问题（涉及令牌选择、带宽分配和发射功率），采用交替算法通过混合整数非凸优化方法进行求解。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架在多个基准测试中达到了最低的客户端资源消耗，并成功在通信效率与模型精度之间取得了优秀的平衡。

Conclusion: ST-SFLora 框架通过语义令牌选择与资源优化，在保障视觉大模型性能的同时，显著降低了边缘设备的计算与通信负载，实现了通信效率与模型性能的最佳权衡。

Abstract: Deploying large Transformer-based vision models on resource-limited mobile devices at network edge is severely constrained by hardware limitations and dynamic wireless environments. While federated learning (FL) enables collaborative training without sharing raw data, strictly local fine-tuning of such massive models remains computationally prohibitive for edge devices. Split federated learning (SFL) alleviates this burden by offloading deep layers to the edge server, yet it suffers from heavy communication overhead when transmitting high-dimensional activation tokens. To address this bottleneck, we propose ST-SFLora, a semantic token-based split federated LoRA fine-tuning framework. We introduce a new metric, \emph{Semantic Transmission Efficiency} (STE), to balance semantic retention and transmission cost. Based on STE, we formulate a joint resource optimization problem that dynamically determines token selection, uplink bandwidth allocation, and transmit power under latency and energy constraints. The resulting mixed-integer nonconvex problem is efficiently solved via an alternating algorithm. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that ST-SFLora achieves the lowest client-side resource consumption among baselines while delivering a favorable trade-off between communication efficiency and model performance.

</details>


### [183] [Agentic AI Workload Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26297)
*Yichao Yuan,Ankita Nayak,Souvik Kundu,Nishil Talati*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过端到端追踪分析智能体工作负载，揭示了智能体执行以解码为主且表现出随时间演变的工具依赖行为，指出高效的服务架构应专注于持久化上下文缓存及智能化的任务调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI（Agentic AI）正将LLM服务从单一的提示-生成请求转变为多轮、有状态的执行模式；现有的服务系统未能充分理解智能体特有的推理和工具调用负载特性。

Method: 采用端到端追踪基础设施（Tracing Infrastructure），在五个智能体基准测试中分析基于Gemma和Qwen模型的ReAct风格智能体，考察其在LLM推理和工具执行层面的行为。

Result: 1. 智能体工作负载不仅是长提示的问题：通过上下文缓存，大部分输入标记（tokens）在不同轮次间重复使用，使得执行过程以解码（decode）为主，且对长生命周期的KV缓存依赖度增加。2. 工具使用具有明确的时间结构：智能体在执行早期倾向于阅读/探索行为，后期转为执行/写入行为。

Conclusion: 高效的智能体服务（Agentic Serving）需要联合管理重复的模型重入、持久化的上下文状态以及与负载相关的工作流工具行为，而非仅仅优化长提示词处理。

Abstract: Agentic AI shifts LLM serving from isolated prompt-generation requests to stateful, multi-turn executions that repeatedly invoke the model, call tools, and grow context over time. This paper characterizes ReAct-style agents from both the LLM-serving and tool-execution perspectives using an end-to-end tracing infrastructure across reasoning and non-reasoning Gemma and Qwen configurations on five agentic benchmarks. Our study shows that agentic workloads are not simply long-prompt workloads: with effective context caching, most input tokens are reused across turns, making execution decode-dominated while increasing dependence on long-lived KV-cache state. We also find that tool use has a clear temporal structure, with agents shifting from read/explore behavior early in execution to execute/write behavior later. These results show that efficient agentic serving must jointly manage repeated model re-entry, persistent context state, and workload-dependent tool behavior.

</details>


### [184] [GridPilot: Real-Time Grid-Responsive Control for AI Supercomputers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26384)
*Denisa-Andreea Constantinescu,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GridPilot 是一种新型的三层预测控制系统，它使 AI/HPC 数据中心能够在毫秒级时间内响应电网需求，成功将大规模计算负载转化为可控的电力弹性资源，并有效解决了冷却开销导致的功率调节偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球数据中心电力需求增长迅速，而电网对能够快速（秒级即时响应）调节负载以匹配风能/太阳能等可再生能源波动性的需求日益迫切，但目前缺乏从软件层到物理功率输出的有效控制方案。

Method: 设计并实现了一个名为 GridPilot 的三层预测控制器（涵盖毫秒、秒、小时级响应），引入了确定性的“安全岛”旁路机制以确保响应速度，并集成了实时 PUE（电源使用效率）校正逻辑以确保电表侧的功率调节准确性。

Result: 在 NVIDIA V100 测试平台上实现了 97.2 ms 的毫秒级端到端响应，远超北欧快速频率响应（FFR）要求的 700 ms 标准；在跨欧洲六个电网的模拟负载调节中，PUE 感知控制器成功抵消了 2.5-5.8 个百分点的冷却能耗偏差，提升了调节精度。

Conclusion: GridPilot 证明了通过精细的软件栈控制，大规模 AI/HPC 设施完全可以作为电网的灵活调节负载，实现毫秒级的响应速度，从而支撑可再生能源的消纳。

Abstract: At global scale, data-center electricity demand is growing faster than the grids that supply it, while system operators increasingly require large flexible loads that can adjust power within seconds to absorb variable wind and solar generation. For multi-megawatt AI/HPC facilities, the key unresolved question is practical and measurable: how quickly can the software stack translate a grid request into a real change in GPU power at the facility meter, where commitments are settled? We answer this on real hardware with GridPilot, a three-tier predictive controller operating across milliseconds, seconds, and hours, augmented by a deterministic safety-island bypass for fast response. On a three-GPU NVIDIA V100 testbed, GridPilot achieves a measured end-to-end trigger-to-target response of 97.2 ms, which is 6.9x faster than the 700 ms requirement of Nordic Fast Frequency Reserve. We further incorporate an instantaneous Power Usage Effectiveness (PUE) correction so dispatched commitments remain robust at meter level rather than only at IT load level. In replay experiments across six representative European grids (from Sweden to Poland), the PUE-aware controller closes 2.5-5.8 percentage points of cooling-overhead drag. GridPilot is released as open source and serves as a proof of concept that MW-scale AI/HPC demand can be engineered as controllable, grid-responsive flexibility by design.

</details>


### [185] [Characterization-Guided GPU Fault Resilience in NVIDIA MPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26461)
*Rixin Liu,Xingqi Cui,Kaijian Wang,Xinheng Ding,Zirui Liu,Yuke Wang,Jiarong Xing*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对NVIDIA MPS在多租户环境下严重故障容忍度不足的问题，提出了故障隔离与快速恢复双重机制，在保障高性能的同时实现了对GPU故障的有效抵御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVIDIA MPS虽能提高GPU利用率，但在多租户环境中使用时，单个进程故障会波及所有协同进程（缺乏容错性），限制了其大规模应用。

Method: 1. 系统性表征GPU故障并深度分析端到端处理管线；2. 在开源GPU驱动内核模块中设计故障隔离机制，针对主导的内存相关故障；3. 对于 proprietary 软件内的故障，设计了基于虚拟内存的GPU驻留状态共享（GPU-resident state sharing）快速恢复机制。

Result: 评估结果表明，所提机制能有效处理相应的GPU故障，并保持了极小的系统开销。

Conclusion: 提出了容错版NVIDIA MPS，通过故障隔离和快速恢复机制，有效地解决了由于单个进程故障导致整机协同进程崩溃的问题，且性能损耗极低，使其适用于多租户等对容错性要求极高的场景。

Abstract: NVIDIA Multi-Process Service (MPS) enables fine-grained GPU sharing by allowing multiple processes to execute concurrently on the same GPU, making it an important mechanism for improving GPU utilization. However, MPS has weak fault resilience: a fault in one process can terminate all co-running processes, limiting its adoption in resilience-critical settings such as multi-tenant GPU clusters. In this work, we design fault-resilient MPS to solve this problem. Our design is guided by insights from a systematic characterization of GPU faults and a deep analysis of their end-to-end processing pipeline. Based on these insights, we design two complementary mechanisms. A fault isolation mechanism for the dominant memory-related faults that can be fully isolated by software intervention in the open GPU driver kernel module. For other faults whose process is within proprietary software, we design a practical mechanism -- fast recovery using virtual memory based GPU-resident state sharing. Our evaluation on different GPUs and workloads shows that these mechanisms can handle corresponding faults effectively with minimal overhead.

</details>


### [186] [StreamSplit: Continuous Audio Representation Learning via Uncertainty-Guided Adaptive Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26523)
*Minh K. Quan,Pubudu N. Pathirana*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: StreamSplit 是一种用于边缘设备的流式对比学习框架，通过新型分布架构与基于强化学习的云边任务自适应分割，有效解决了大批次对比学习在资源受限环境下的精度与延迟冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代对比学习（CL）依赖大批次训练，这与边缘设备有限且波动的计算资源存在冲突；现有静态模型压缩方案难以应对边缘环境的计算波动，且卸载到云端会带来高延迟和带宽成本。

Method: 1. 提出基于分布的流式框架，利用混合损失函数（Hybrid Loss）解耦表示质量与局部批次大小，缓解稀疏更新带来的影响；2. 设计“不确定性引导的自适应分割器（Uncertainty-Guided Adaptive Splitter）”，使用轻量级强化学习（RL）策略，结合实时资源监控与嵌入模糊度动态分配云边计算任务。

Result: 在不同硬件上测试显示，StreamSplit 相比云端基准模型，单样本延迟降低最高达 4.7 倍，带宽使用减少 77.1%，能耗降低 52.3%，且精度仅损失 2.2% 以内。

Conclusion: StreamSplit 证明了通过自适应、分布式计算，边缘设备在资源受限的情况下也能实现高质量的流式对比学习，是边缘计算生态的可行发展路径。

Abstract: Large-batch Contrastive Learning (CL), the foundation of modern representation learning, is fundamentally incompatible with the volatile resource constraints of edge devices. This conflict creates a dilemma: small on-device batches degrade model fidelity, while offloading to the cloud incurs unacceptable latency and bandwidth costs. Existing solutions often resort to static model compression, which fails to adapt to the runtime volatility of edge environments. To bridge this gap, we present StreamSplit, a novel framework that makes streaming CL practical across heterogeneous ARM client platforms. StreamSplit resolves the conflict between the continuous nature of ambient audio and the discrete batch requirements of models like CLAP and COLA. We introduce: (1) A distribution-based streaming framework that decouples representation quality from local batch size, using a tractable Hybrid Loss to maintain fidelity despite sparse updates; and (2) An Uncertainty-Guided Adaptive Splitter that uses a lightweight Reinforcement Learning (RL) policy to dynamically partition computation. Uniquely, this policy integrates real-time resource monitoring with embedding ambiguity to optimize the accuracy-latency trade-off on the fly. We evaluate StreamSplit on diverse hardware, from the resource-constrained Raspberry Pi 4 to the high-performance Apple M2. Results demonstrate that StreamSplit reduces per-sample latency by up to 4.7x and cuts bandwidth by 77.1% and energy by 52.3% compared to server-centric baselines. Crucially, it maintains accuracy within 2.2% of server-centric models, proving that adaptive, distributed learning is a viable path for the modern edge ecosystem.

</details>


### [187] [A Formal Semantics of C with OpenMP Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26527)
*Ke Du,Anshu Sharma,Liyi Li,William Mansky*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出并建立了一种用于标注了 OpenMP 指令的 C 代码的形式化语义，旨在解决 OpenMP 并行编程中易出现数据竞争的问题，并为检测此类并行错误提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 OpenMP 简化了并行编程，但通过简单的注释（pragmas）极易引入逻辑错误和数据竞争，缺乏严谨的语义支持来保障代码的正确性。

Method: 在 CompCert 验证编译器及其并发扩展的基础上，开发了一套适用于包含 OpenMP 指令的 C 语言形式化语义模型。

Result: 成功构建了能够捕捉 OpenMP 指令与变量状态之间复杂交互的形式化模型，并证明了在该语义框架下，任何成功的执行过程都被保证不存在数据竞争。

Conclusion: 本文构建的 OpenMP 形式化语义为并行程序的正确性验证提供了坚实基础，尤其是能够确保通过该框架验证的执行路径不存在数据竞争，从而弥补了以往语义描述在变量状态交互方面的模糊地带。

Abstract: OpenMP is a popular parallelization framework that lets users transform sequential code into parallel code with a few simple annotations. Unfortunately, it is also easy to inadvertently introduce errors by adding OpenMP pragmas into otherwise correct programs, including both logic errors and race conditions. We present a formal semantics for C code with OpenMP directives, building on the C semantics of the CompCert verified compiler and its extension to concurrency.
