Our semantics captures subtle interactions between OpenMP directives and variable state that have been obscured by previous OpenMP semantics, and provides a basis for detecting undesired behaviors introduced by incorrect annotations: in particular, any successful execution is guaranteed to be free of data races.

</details>


### [188] [Reducing Internal State in Eigenvalue-Only Divide-and-Conquer Tridiagonal Eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26599)
*Ruiyi Zhan,Shaoshuai Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于计算对称矩阵特征值的边界行分治算法，通过减少内存占用和计算开销，克服了传统分治法在仅需特征值时不必要的全矩阵变换问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的特征值分解方法中，传统的分治法（D&C）在合并阶段产生过大的矩阵存储需求（平方级空间复杂度）或涉及冗余计算，导致许多高性能库在仅需计算特征值（不需特征向量）时倾向于使用受限的QR方法。

Method: 提出了一种仅需部分边界行/列即可完成合并阶段的“边界行分治算法”（Boundary-row D&C），通过在递归过程中直接传递这些边界数据，避免了对完整特征向量矩阵的存储或重演。

Result: 将内存需求从二次方级别降低至线性级别，消除了非必要的矩阵-向量运算；提供了基于CPU和GPU的优化实现，并在性能上优于标准的QR和传统D&C例程。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的边界行分治算法通过降低空间复杂度和消除冗余计算，实现了针对仅求解特征值问题的更优性能，是传统QR方法和传统D&C方法的高效替代方案。

Abstract: Divide and Conquer (D&C) is a widely used algorithmic strategy for symmetric eigenvalue decomposition. Its natural parallelism makes D&C attractive on modern multicore CPUs and GPUs, but existing eigenvalue-only routines often default to QR-based methods because conventional D&C still materializes or replays large transformation matrices during the conquer phase. This paper proposes a boundary-row D&C algorithm for eigenvalue-only computation. The key observation is that the conquer phase only needs selected boundary rows/columns rather than the full accumulated eigenvector matrix. By propagating these boundary rows directly through the recursion, the proposed algorithm reduces the memory requirement from quadratic to linear space while also eliminating unnecessary matrix-vector work in the conventional lazy-replay formulation. We provide the algorithm, its time and space complexity analysis, correctness and stability arguments, optimized CPU and GPU implementations, and an evaluation against QR and D&C routines in standard numerical libraries.

</details>


### [189] [Nonlinear spectral clustering with C++ GraphBLAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26975)
*Dimosthenis Pasadakis,Olaf Schenk,Verner Vlacic,Albert-Jan Yzelman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出并基于GraphBLAS实现了$p$-范数多路谱聚类算法，通过线性代数优化显著提升了计算效率，在大规模图数据集上实现了高性能的聚类分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性谱聚类虽然具有数学优势，但在估计多个非线性特征向量时计算代价过高，亟需提升计算效率并优化大规模数据的处理能力。

Method: 提出了一种基于C++ GraphBLAS API的$p$-范数（$p \in (1,2]$）多路谱聚类算法，将聚类核心运算表达为线性代数操作（稀疏矩阵与稠密向量运算），并在共享内存架构上进行了并行化实现。

Result: 在人工测试集上验证了算法的准确性，在平衡图割指标（balanced graph cut metric）方面表现出色；在多达800万节点和4800万边的真实数据集上展现了强大的强扩展性能。

Conclusion: 基于GraphBLAS实现的$p$-范数多路谱聚类算法在计算效率和扩展性上表现优异，能够有效处理大规模图数据（千万级节点/边），并保持高质量的聚类效果。

Abstract: Nonlinear reformulations of the spectral clustering method have gained a lot of recent attention due to their increased numerical benefits and their solid mathematical background. However, the estimation of the multiple nonlinear eigenvectors is associated with an increased computational cost. We present an implementation of a direct multiway spectral clustering algorithm in the $p$-norm, for $p\in(1,2]$, using a novel C++ GraphBLAS API. The key operations are expressed in linear algebraic terms and are executed over the resulting sparse matrices and dense vectors, parameterized in the algebra pertinent to the computation. We demonstrate the effectiveness and accuracy of our shared-memory algorithm on several artificial test cases. Our numerical examples and comparative results against competitive methods indicate that the proposed implementation attains high quality clusters in terms of the balanced graph cut metric. The strong scaling capabilities of our algorithm are showcased on a range of datasets with up to $8$ million nodes and $48$ million edges.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [LearnedCache: An eBPF-Integrated Perceptron-Based Eviction Policy for the Linux Page Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26168)
*Zejia Qi*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出了 LearnedCache，一种集成在内核中利用 eBPF 和单层感知机实现的 AI 驱动页缓存置换策略，实测证明其在多种真实工作负载下的表现优于传统 FIFO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 Linux 页缓存（page cache）置换策略主要依赖启发式算法，灵活性不足。虽然 AI 驱动的方法在用户空间应用（如 CDN）中表现出色，但尚未在 Linux 内核层面得到有效实践。

Method: 提出 LearnedCache，这是一种基于 eBPF 集成和单层感知机（SLP）的缓存置换策略。该模型通过来自多种 Linux 工作负载的真实内核数据进行训练，并将其嵌入内核中进行实时性能评估。

Result: 模型预测页面重用时间的 AUC 中位数接近 80%；在 50 次配对测试中，LearnedCache 的插入率（缓存命中率的变体）比 FIFO 提高了高达 10%，且系统开销极低。

Conclusion: LearnedCache 证明了基于机器学习的缓存置换策略在 Linux 内核中是可行的，能够以极小的开销超越传统 FIFO 策略，并在特定负载下显著提升缓存命中率。

Abstract: Linux is the foundation of the digital age, accounting for the majority of the cloud and mobile OS markets. Any device that runs Linux uses the Linux page cache, a central pillar in OS and application performance, serving to reduce extraneous disk access. Many page cache eviction policies have been developed but remain bound by the rigidity of heuristics. The rise of AI-driven tools in recent years, melded with the ever-increasing variety of workloads for Linux devices, sets the stage for machine-learning-driven cache eviction policies. Promising research has been done in this field, but only in the field of user-space applications such as CDNs. We develop LearnedCache, an eBPF-integrated single-layer perceptron-based cache eviction policy for the Linux page cache, trained on real kernel data from diverse workloads. We demonstrate median AUCs of nearly 80% over multiple linear models modeling page reuse time, then take a step further by embedding these models inside the Linux kernel for real-time performance evaluation. Through statistical testing over 50 paired trials against a baseline of FIFO for each workload, LearnedCache reveals that machine-learning-derived cache eviction policies are practical in the Linux kernel under representative empirical workloads and are able to surpass conventional FIFO by statistically significant margins of up to 10% in insertion rate, a frequency-adjusted derivation of cache hit rate, in specific workloads while incurring minimal overhead.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Learning Nonlinear Factor Models with Unknown Monotone Links from Incomplete and Noisy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26271)
*Yutong Chao,Resat Gökhan,Jalal Etesami,Ali Habibnia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RKHS的非线性因子模型，通过投影块坐标下降算法实现了对低秩潜在因子及非线性链接函数的联合恢复，并提供了严谨的收敛性理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非线性因子模型通常面临严重的非凸性和可识别性问题，且缺乏在这些约束下对低秩潜在因子、因子载荷及链接函数的稳健恢复方法。

Method: 1. 采用再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）对未知的单调链接函数进行非参数建模；2. 制定了低秩因子、因子载荷与非线性链接函数的联合恢复问题；3. 提出了一种带有显式正则化的投影块坐标下降（BCD）算法，以处理尺度和旋转模糊性问题。

Result: 在温和的因子非相干性及标准采样条件下，作者建立了算法在无噪和有噪环境下的收敛性保证，以及链接函数更新的次线性后悔界；合成实验证明了该方法具有良好的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将经典的线性因子模型扩展至非线性领域，并提供了一个学习非线性潜在结构的原则性框架，在非线性因子分析领域具有重要理论价值。

Abstract: We study a nonlinear factor model in which observed responses depend on low-rank latent factors through an unknown monotone link function. This setting is challenging and largely underexplored due to severe nonconvexity and identifiability issues. The link function is assumed to lie in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS), enabling flexible nonparametric modeling while preserving identifiability. We formulate the problem as the joint recovery of the low-rank factors, loadings, and the nonlinear link function from possibly incomplete and noisy observations and propose a projected block coordinate descent (BCD) algorithm with explicit regularization to address scale and rotational ambiguities. Under mild incoherence of factors and standard sampling conditions, we establish convergence guarantees in both noiseless and noisy regimes, along with sublinear regret bounds for the link-function updates. Our results extend classical linear factor models to a broad nonlinear regime and provide a principled framework for learning nonlinear latent structures. We evaluate the proposed approach using controlled synthetic experiments, indicating promising performance.

</details>


### [192] [Beyond Differences: Doubly Robust Meta-Learners for Ratio-Based Treatment Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26288)
*Michael Fuchs,Dominik Kreiss*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 Q-Learner，这是一种通过转换分类任务来估计比率型条件平均处理效应（CATE）的新方法，并结合双重稳健增强技术，解决了低转化率及存在混杂偏差场景下比率建模的估计难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的比率型处理效应（Ratio-based CATE）估计方法要么依赖参数假设（如对数线性），要么使用缺乏鲁棒性保证的通用回归模型，无法有效处理不平衡数据或观测数据中的混杂偏差。

Method: 1. 提出了 Q-Learner：将比率 CATE 分解为两个优势比（odds ratios），转换为两个倾向评分分类任务。
2. 派生了针对 S/T 型和 Q 型比率学习器的双重稳健（Doubly Robust, DR）增强方法。
3. 对不同方法的鲁棒性特性进行了理论表征。

Result: 1. RCT 基准测试：Q-Learner 在低转化率场景下表现最稳定，优于基于结果模型的估计器。
2. 观测数据测试：所提出的 DR 学习器在处理混杂因素方面表现最佳，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Q-Learner 提供了一种处理比率型处理效应的稳健估计框架，特别是在低转化率的随机对照试验（RCT）中表现优异。对于存在混杂因素的观测数据，本文提出的双重稳健（DR）增强方法是首选方案。

Abstract: When treatment effects are naturally expressed as ratios -- as in medicine, pricing, and marketing -- the ratio-based CATE $τ(x) = E[Y|W=1,X=x] / E[Y|W=0,X=x]$ is the appropriate estimand. Yet existing estimators either impose a log-linear parametric structure or apply generic regression without robustness guarantees for this functional. We introduce the Q-Learner, which decomposes $τ(x)$ into a product of two odds ratios, reducing ratio-CATE estimation for binary outcomes to two propensity classification tasks. We further derive doubly robust augmentations for both S/T- and Q-style ratio learners and characterize their distinct robustness properties. In benchmarks on seven RCT datasets, the Q-Learner is the most consistently competitive method in low-conversion regimes, where its propensity-only construction sidesteps the imbalanced regression that hurts outcome-based estimators. On four observational datasets, where propensity must be estimated and confounding cannot be ruled out, the DR learners introduced here decisively come out on top, making them practitioners' natural default for confounded observational data.

</details>


### [193] [When Does LeJEPA Learn a World Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26379)
*David Klindt,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文从数学上证明了 LeJEPA 能够通过对齐和高斯正则化，在特定条件下精确恢复世界的潜在变量，从而为构建具备可靠规划和泛化能力的世界模型提供了坚实的理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表示学习模型如果无法解耦世界的真实自由度，就难以支持可靠的规划和组合泛化。研究旨在探究如何通过数学保证确保世界模型能够准确恢复潜在的世界结构。

Method: 利用谱分解（spectral decomposition）分析非线性程度的惩罚机制，证明了在平稳、加性噪声转换条件下，LeJEPA 通过对齐（alignment）和高斯正则化（Gaussian regularization）能够线性地恢复 latent 变量。采用数学证明与多维度实验验证（从 2D 示例到高维像素级机器人控制）相结合的方法。

Result: 理论分析证明了 LeJEPA 具备线性可辨识性及其在高斯分布下的唯一性；实验结果显示，线性正交可辨识性能够支持最优的潜在空间规划（latent-space planning），且在各种实验场景下验证了理论的正确性。

Conclusion: LeJEPA 被证明是唯一能在广泛世界模型假设下实现线性可辨识性的方法，且高斯先验分布是实现这一理论保证的唯一条件。该研究为构建能够可靠恢复世界结构的世界模型奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: A representation that scrambles the true degrees of freedom of the world cannot support reliable planning or compositional generalization. We prove that LeJEPA (alignment plus Gaussian regularization) linearly recovers the world's latent variables from nonlinear observations, a property known as linear identifiability, in a broad class of worlds where latents evolve under stationary, additive-noise transitions. Our main result is that among all such worlds, the Gaussian is the unique latent distribution for which this guarantee holds. The forward direction rests on a spectral decomposition in which each degree of nonlinearity is strictly penalized by alignment, making the linear map the optimum; the converse rules out every non-Gaussian alternative. We further prove an approximate identifiability result where the guarantee degrades gracefully, and show that linear, orthogonal identifiability enables optimal latent-space planning. We validate the theory with experiments ranging from 2D examples to 1024-dimensional latents, including distributional ablations and pixel-based robotic control. Our theory turns an empirically successful recipe into a mathematical guarantee, providing the foundation for building World Models that provably recover the structure of the world.

</details>


### [194] [CART Random Forests as Sequential Allocation over Random Opportunity Sets: A Stochastic-Control Theory of Ensemble Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26675)
*Tianxing Mei,Yingying Fan,Mingming Leng,Jinchi Lv*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过随机控制理论将CART随机森林“非黑盒化”，识别出由特征子采样和分割策略所决定的两个关键设计杠杆，从而定量解释了复杂随机森林算法的MSE性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CART随机森林虽在工业界应用广泛，但其内部机制和复杂行为常被视为“黑盒”，缺乏系统的机制层面的理论解释，难以对算法的MSE性能进行解析。

Method: 提出了一种基于随机控制的CART-ROSA（CART随机机会集分配）框架，将随机子采样解释为“随机可行动作集”，将CART分割规则解释为“掩蔽动作分配策略”，并基于该框架导出了特征子采样率和分割策略强度这两个关键设计杠杆。

Result: 证明了CART策略具有局部稳定性（收敛于信息分割分配），但在森林层面上可能存在全局次优性；通过线性模型推导出了明确的MSE风险扩展。

Conclusion: 通过引入运筹学视角，成功将CART随机森林黑盒机制转化为易于数学处理的随机控制模型，明确了特征子采样和分割策略对MSE的贡献，为优化算法设计提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: CART random forests are among the most widely used modern predictive methods, with well-documented empirical success. Yet, at the mechanistic level, the algorithm is often treated as a black box because of its complexity. In this paper, we develop a stochastic-control perspective on feature-subsampled CART random forests, named CART random opportunity-set allocation (CART-ROSA). At each node, the random subset of features is interpreted as a random feasible action set, and the CART split rule as a masked-action allocation policy. This policy induces a controlled stochastic process over informative split-count states, whose terminal law determines both single-tree error and cross-tree interaction terms in the forest mean squared error (MSE). Such representation opens the black box of CART-forests by separating two design levers: the informative-opportunity rate induced by feature subsampling, and the contraction strength from the within-mask split policy. We establish that the CART policy is locally stabilizing: it contracts imbalances in informative split allocations and concentrates terminal tree geometry. At the system level, however, it can be globally suboptimal for the forest objective. Specializing to the linear model, we derive the MSE risk expansion explicitly. Our results show how an operations-research perspective makes tractable a theoretical gap difficult to access from the standard algorithmic description of CART forests.

</details>


### [195] [Constrained Bayesian Experimental Design via Online Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26990)
*Yujia Guo,Daolang Huang,Xinyu Zhang,Sammie Katt,Samuel Kaski,Ayush Bharti*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将离线预训练策略与在线情景树规划相结合的新型BED框架，成功解决了动态约束环境下的实验设计难题，实现了更高质量的顺序实验决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的贝叶斯实验设计（BED）方法无法适应现实任务中常见的动态约束（如预算限制、成本波动或物理约束），从而限制了其在实际场景中的应用。

Method: 提出了一种结合离线预训练（Amortized Policy和Posterior Network）与在线多步前瞻规划（使用Scenario Trees）的混合框架，以处理具有动态约束的BED问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在多种受约束的BED任务中，相比现有方法能产生信息量显著更高的设计序列，且仅引入了适度的额外计算开销。

Conclusion: 本文提出的结合离线预训练摊销策略与在线情景树多步规划的方法，有效解决了贝叶斯实验设计（BED）在动态约束下的应用难题，实现了在约束条件下更具信息量的序列设计决策。

Abstract: Bayesian experimental design (BED) is a principled framework for data-efficient design of sequential experiments. However, existing BED methods are unable to adapt to dynamic constraints inherent in real-world tasks due to budget limitations, varying costs, or physical constraints that restrict how designs evolve over time. In this paper, we introduce a novel approach to BED that enables constrained optimization of experimental designs by combining offline pre-training of an amortized policy and a posterior network with online multi-step lookahead planning using scenario trees. We empirically demonstrate that our method yields substantially more informative design sequences than existing methods across a range of constrained BED tasks, while incurring only a modest additional computational overhead.

</details>


### [196] [Causal Representation Learning for Generalisable Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27043)
*Yorgos Felekis,Michael O'Riordan,Oriol Corcoll,Ciarán M. Gilligan-Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对推荐系统中训练数据与部署环境分布不一致的问题，本文提出了一种基于因果表征学习的解耦方法，成功提升了模型在分布偏移下的线上泛化性能，且无需在推理时增加额外计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决推荐系统等预测模型在训练数据（由以往策略、用户行为和系统过滤导致）与部署时分布不一致（分布偏移）的问题，从而消除线下衡量指标无法准确预测线上表现的偏差。

Method: 基于因果表征学习（CRL），提出了一种信息论解耦准则，通过证明该准则的最优解仅依赖于输入的因果分量，并导出了一个可在观测数据上进行优化的变分下界（vlb）。该方法可应用于现有的监督学习模型，无需推理时额外开销。

Result: 在Spotify生产环境（数百万用户AB测试）以及KuaiRand、合成数据集上进行测试，结果显示该方法在离线指标持平的情况下，实现了显著的在线参与度提升，证明了其在分布偏移场景下优于基线的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入信息论解耦准则，本文实现了针对分布偏移问题的有效建模。实验证明，该方法在不增加推理成本的前提下，显著提升了推荐系统在离线评估与在线表现之间的相关性，并在大规模生产环境和公开数据集中验证了其优越的泛化能力。

Abstract: Predictive models trained on observational data often fail to generalise to the distributions they encounter when deployed, especially when the training data is a product of the system being optimised. Recommender systems are a canonical example: they are trained on interaction logs confounded by the deployed policy, past user behaviour, and platform filtering. As a result, the training distribution differs substantially from the candidate distribution scored at serving time, a gap that makes offline metrics unreliable predictors of online performance. We address the distribution shift problem with a method motivated by causal representation learning (CRL). We propose an information-theoretic disentanglement criterion and prove that its optimum depends only on the causal components of the input. We then derive a tractable variational lower bound that makes the criterion optimisable from finite observational data alone. The scope of our method is narrower than that of much of the CRL literature, in that we target better generalisation under distribution shift, not full identification of all latent causal factors. This narrower target is what makes the method practical, requiring only the existing confounded logs, applying to any standard supervised model, and adding no inference-time cost. Our headline evaluation is an A/B test with millions of users on Spotify, applied to a production ranker for personalised playlist generation. A capacity-matched CRL variant performed on par offline but delivered substantial online gains in listener engagement. Complementary evidence on the public KuaiRand recommendation dataset and a synthetic benchmark with known causal structure shows the same pattern: offline parity with baseline, gains under distribution shift. Across all three settings, adding our causal disentanglement objective yields meaningfully better out-of-distribution generalisation.

</details>


### [197] [Gaussian Process-based learning with new MCMC-based implementation of Wishart prior on correlation matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27093)
*Kane Warrior,Dalia Chakrabarty*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对高维输入空间中GP调参困难的问题，本文提出了一种具有自适应能力的“自组装Wishart先验”，通过动态更新尺度矩阵优化MCMC推断，有效提升了复杂模型学习的鲁棒性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在处理高维输入空间时，高斯过程（GP）需要同时学习多个长度尺度参数，这导致推断过程极具挑战性且难以收敛。

Method: 提出了一种“自组装（self-assembled）”Wishart先验，用于GP内核的协方差矩阵；利用MCMC采样进行贝叶斯推断，并通过一个跨越近期MCMC迭代的“回顾窗口（look-back window）”来动态定义时间步长相关的尺度矩阵，从而实现链条的自适应性。

Result: 通过合成数据和真实世界数据集的实验验证，该方法不仅能够改善多维GP的推断性能，还能作为诊断GP模型中弱信息输入特征的工具。

Conclusion: 直接对协方差矩阵施加先验分布是一种有效的策略，特别是在处理高维输入数据时，有助于识别和诊断GP模型中的弱信息特征，并提高模型稳定性。

Abstract: In probabilstic supervised learning of an input-output relationship - as a sample function of a Gaussian Process (GP) - priors are typically specified for the hyperparameters of the kernel that parametrises the covariance function of the GP, where the induced covariance matrix of the (resulting multivariate Normal) likelihood, governs the learning and prediction. When the sought function is highly multivariate, multiple lengthscale parameters must be learnt simultaneously, making inference difficult. We develop a ``self-assembled'' Wishart prior for the covariance matrix, while undertaking Bayesian inference on the kernel hyperparameters using MCMC. The construction uses a look-back window over recent MCMC iterations to define a time-step dependent scale matrix, thereby introducing adaptiveness to the chain. Results suggest that direct prior specification on the covariance matrix can be useful for diagnosing weakly informative inputs within the GP-based learning paradigm. We support our prior development with two distinct empirical illustrations - one on synthetic data, and another on a real-world dataset.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Where to Split and When to Charge: Optimal Route Construction from Customer Permutations in Electric Vehicle Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26816)
*Leon Stjepan Uroić,Marko Đurasević*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对电动汽车路径规划中的固定序列解码问题，提出了一种精确动态规划解码算法，解决了路由拆分与充电决策耦合的难题，并量化了不同解码简化策略对解质量和性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于排列的电动汽车路径规划元启发式算法在解码环节（如何将客户序列拆分为路线并插入充电站）往往依赖启发式策略，缺乏全局最优性，且难以有效处理载货能力与电池约束的耦合关系。

Method: 将该任务形式化为“固定序列拆分与充电问题”（Fixed-Permutation Splitting and Charging Problem），并提出了一种基于动态规划和支配剪枝的精确前向标记算法（Forward Labeling Algorithm）。同时，设计了三种不同约束程度的简化解码策略进行对比分析。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的精确解码器在求解效率上具有可行性。研究建立了一套解码策略的性能层级结构：最严格的变体运行速度接近启发式方法，但解质量更高；较宽松的变体则在提供更高鲁棒性和解质量的同时，适度增加了计算开销。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了所提出的精确联合解码算法在实际应用中是可行的，并揭示了不同解码策略在效率与解质量之间的权衡关系。限制较少的变体能平衡运行时间和解质量，而精确解码器为评估固定序列下的优化潜力提供了基准。

Abstract: Permutation-based metaheuristics are widely used for electric vehicle routing, where candidate solutions are represented as ordered sequences of customers. Such sequences, however, do not directly define feasible vehicle routes: they must be decoded by choosing where to split the permutation into routes and where to insert charging-station visits, subject to cargo capacity and battery constraints. These decisions are inherently interdependent, since each return to the depot both separates consecutive routes and restores the vehicle battery. This paper formalizes the task as the Fixed-Permutation Splitting and Charging Problem and proposes an exact forward labeling algorithm that constructs a minimum-distance feasible decoding of a fixed customer permutation using dynamic programming with dominance pruning. We further derive restricted variants representing increasingly simplified decoding strategies: first separating route splitting from charging-station insertion, and then additionally limiting each inter-customer segment to at most one charging-station visit. Computational experiments on benchmark and randomly generated instances, including comparisons with heuristic decoders from the literature, confirm that the exact decoder remains tractable in practice and reveal a clear hierarchy among decoding strategies. The most restrictive variant achieves runtimes close to those of heuristic decoders while delivering substantially higher decoding success rates and better solution quality. Less restrictive variants further improve quality and robustness at the cost of additional runtime. The exact joint decoder provides the optimal reference for each fixed permutation, clarifying the trade-offs introduced by common decoding simplifications.

</details>


### [199] [Parsimonious Learning-Augmented Online Metric Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26886)
*Yongho Shin,Phanu Vajanopath*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对在线度量匹配问题，该文提出了一种节俭型学习增强算法，通过引入虚拟预测策略平衡了预测成本与算法性能，并经理论分析与实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习增强算法在处理大规模在线问题（如缓存和度量任务系统）时，由于生成预测的高昂计算成本，迫切需要研究在限制预测次数的情况下如何维持算法性能。

Method: 将“追随预测（Follow-the-Prediction）”框架扩展至节俭设置：在缺乏外部预测时，引入虚拟预测机制，并结合在线度量匹配算法以在执行过程中维护良好的中间匹配。

Result: 开发了适用于在线度量匹配的节俭型算法，建立了相应的性能下界，并通过实验证明了该算法在减少预测依赖的同时依然具有良好的性能表现。

Conclusion: 该研究为在线度量匹配问题提出了“节俭型（parsimonious）”学习增强算法框架，通过虚拟预测策略在不依赖持续提供预测的情况下实现了性能权衡，证明了该方法在理论和实践上的有效性。

Abstract: Learning-augmented algorithms have received significant attention in recent years, particularly in the context of online optimization. Motivated by the high computational cost of generating predictions, a growing line of work studies the tradeoff between performance guarantees and the number of predictions used in learning-augmented algorithms for problems such as caching and metrical task systems. In this paper, we extend this line of research to online metric matching by developing parsimonious learning-augmented algorithms and establishing lower bounds on their performance. Our approach extends the Follow-the-Prediction framework to the parsimonious setting by filling in a virtual prediction in the absence of an actual prediction, using an online metric matching algorithm that maintains good intermediate matchings throughout its execution. We complement our theoretical results with an empirical evaluation, demonstrating the practical effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [200] [Virtual-Memory Powersort](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27147)
*Finn Moltmann,Tamio-Vesa Nakajima,Sebastian Wild*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出 Virtual-Memory Powersort，通过内部缓冲技术显著降低了自适应归并排序的内存占用（降至 O(sqrt(n log n))），同时确保了优异的运行速度，实现了高效的几乎原地稳定排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自适应归并排序（Powersort）虽然高效，但内存占用较高（O(n)）；而现有的原地归并排序算法虽能减小空间开销，但会显著降低运行速度。研究旨在寻求一种空间与时间效率更优的折中方案。

Method: 采用内部缓冲（internal buffering）技术，通过优化数据缓冲区管理，将额外空间复杂度从 O(n) 降低至 O(sqrt(n log n))，并保持了与原有 Powersort 相当的数据移动和比较次数。

Result: 成功将缓冲区需求从 n/2 个对象降低至 O(sqrt(n log n)) 个对象，且实验表明该算法在多种场景下表现出极低的额外开销，性能优于或媲美现有主流稳定排序算法。

Conclusion: 提出 Virtual-Memory Powersort 算法，证明了在几乎原地（almost in-place）的内存消耗下，仍能保持与传统 Powersort 相当的运行效率，实现了空间效率与运行时间的平衡。

Abstract: We give a more space-efficient implementation of adaptive mergesort: Virtual-Memory Powersort. Using internal buffering techniques, we significantly reduce the memory consumption of the algorithm; specifically, for sorting $n$ objects the required buffer area is reduced from space for $n/2$ objects to $O(\sqrt{n \log n})$ objects. While this space-efficiency can be achieved (indeed reduced to $O(1)$) conceptually very easily with known inplace merging algorithms, using these as a drop-in replacement for the standard merge algorithm incurs a substantial slow-down. Virtual-Memory Powersort, by contrast, uses the same number of moves and comparisons as previous Powersort implementations up to an additive $O(n)$ term. We report on an empirical running-time study comparing our implementation against other Powersort variants and state-of-the-art stable sorting methods, demonstrating that almost in-place stable sorting can be achieved with negligible overhead in many scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [GEM: Geometric Entropy Mixing for Optimal LLM Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26121)
*Yue Min,Ziyun Qiao,Ruining Chen,Yujun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 数据混合的效率难题，本文提出了 GEM 框架，通过几何优化方法解决聚类失效问题，实现了更均衡的数据分布和更优的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大规模语言模型（LLM）预训练效率受制于数据配比，但传统的人工分类方法存在语义对齐偏差，而基于欧几里得距离的聚类方法又无法解决嵌入空间的各向异性（anisotropy）问题。

Method: 提出了 GEM (Geometric Entropy Mixing) 框架，将数据混合重构为超球面上的变分问题；引入了混合平衡正则化项；利用 MM (Minorize-Maximize) 算法优化目标函数；利用教师-学生蒸馏实现大规模语料扩展；设计了 GIS (Geometric Influence Score) 指标实现可解释的分类。

Result: 在 1.1B 参数模型上的实验表明，GEM 与 DoReMi 和 RegMix 等主流混合策略结合后，下游任务准确率领先现有方案最高达 1.2%。

Conclusion: GEM 通过几何流形视角有效解决了数据混合中的聚类塌陷问题，为大规模模型训练提供了更科学、可预测的混合策略，显著提升了下游任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: LLM pre-training efficacy increasingly depends on data composition rather than sheer volume. Yet, optimal mixing is hindered by categorization flaws: human taxonomies suffer from ontological misalignment, and Euclidean clustering fails to address embedding anisotropy. We introduce GEM (Geometric Entropy Mixing), a framework reformulating data curation as a variational problem on the hypersphere augmented with a mixing-balance regularizer. By decoupling the generative prior and optimizing the objective via a provable MM (Minorize-Maximize) algorithm, GEM effectively counteracts the cluster collapse to discover balanced semantic structures invisible to Euclidean heuristics. We employ teacher-student distillation to scale this geometric fidelity to web-scale corpora and introduce the Geometric Influence Score (GIS) for interpretable taxonomy generation. Experiments with 1.1B-parameter models demonstrate that GEM establishes a new state-of-the-art when integrated into mixing strategies like DoReMi and RegMix, improving average downstream accuracy by up to 1.2% and offering a robust coordinate system for predictable data mixing.

</details>


### [202] [AirCast-SR: A Foundation Model for Kilometer-Scale Atmospheric Super-Resolution via Latent Consistency Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26130)
*Somnath Luitel,Manmeet Singh,Joshua Durkee,Abdullah Al Fahad,Naveen Sudharsan,Prabhjot Singh,Cenlin He,Harsh Kamath,Zong-Liang Yang,Krishnagopal Halder,Sandeep Juneja,Parthasarathi Mukhopadhyay,Saptarishi Dhanuka,Amit Kumar Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究者提出了AirCast-SR（EarthMind-SR），这是一种基于扩散架构的气象超分辨率大模型，能将0.25度的全球气象预报高效提升至1km分辨率，且具备优异的降尺度精度与零样本全球迁移潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值天气预报（NWP）在公里级尺度上计算成本过高，限制了能源、农业和灾害管理等领域对细粒度气象数据的需求。

Method: 提出了一种基于潜空间一致性模型（LCM）的扩散框架，核心结构为三维U-Net。该模型以GraphCast的0.25度预报为输入，以NOAA的AORC数据集为训练目标，实现从粗分辨率到1km分辨率的空间下采样。

Result: 模型在所有变量和预报时效上实现了接近零的偏差，有效恢复了10km至100km波长的精细大气结构，并在印度和德国的验证中表现出无需微调的强零样本全球迁移能力。

Conclusion: AirCast-SR（EarthMind-SR）证明了基于扩散模型的超分辨率技术在生成公里级精细天气预报方面的有效性，提供了一种比传统NWP更高效、且具备跨区域零样本迁移能力的通用方案，为精细化气候服务奠定了基础。

Abstract: Operational weather prediction at kilometer scales remains computationally prohibitive for traditional numerical weather prediction (NWP) models, limiting forecast access for applications in energy, agriculture, and disaster management that require fine-grained spatiotemporal detail. Here we introduce AirCast-SR, a foundation model for atmospheric super-resolution that downscales global AI weather forecasts from 0.25 degree (~28 km) to 1 km horizontal resolution at hourly temporal resolution, producing 67-hour forecasts of eight coupled surface variables simultaneously. EarthMind-SR employs a three-dimensional U-Net conditioned within a Latent Consistency Model (LCM) diffusion framework, trained on patch-based samples over the contiguous United States (CONUS) using GraphCast forecasts as input and NOAA's Analysis of Record for Calibration (AORC) as the target. The model achieves near-zero bias across all variables and lead times, and its radial power spectral density analysis demonstrates preservation of fine-scale atmospheric structure at wavelengths of 10 km to 100 km where coarser models lose spectral power. We validate EarthMind-SR across three CONUS case studies spanning winter, summer, and spring seasons, and demonstrate zero-shot global transferability over India and Germany using independent surface station observations without any retraining or fine-tuning. As an open-weights foundation model, EarthMind-SR establishes a new paradigm for kilometer-scale AI weather prediction and provides a platform for regional fine-tuning, distillation, and downstream applications in climate services and hazard forecasting.

</details>


### [203] [SilIF: Silhouette-Augmented Isolation Forest for Unsupervised Transaction Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26135)
*Venkatakrishnan Gopalakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为 SilIF 的隔离森林（IF）增强算法，通过引入特征空间中的轮廓系数（Silhouette score）优化异常得分，在特定欺诈检测基准上验证了其有效性，并提供了客观的性能边界分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督异常检测在金融欺诈场景中至关重要，但现有经典方法（如隔离森林）在特征利用上存在局限，需进一步提升检测精度。

Method: 提出 SilIF 方法。首先利用隔离森林生成每个点的路径长度向量（指纹），通过聚类识别结构组，计算每个点的轮廓分数；最后将轮廓分数与原始 IF 分数结合（通过超参数 alpha），增强异常检测效果。

Result: 在 IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection 基准上，SilIF 相比传统 IF 提升了 0.0080 的 AUC-PR；在 Sparkov 合成数据集上效果未见提升，并分析了导致性能差异的潜在条件。

Conclusion: SilIF 通过轮廓系数（Silhouette score）增强了隔离森林（IF），在部分数据集上展现了优于传统 IF 的检测性能，同时指出了该方法的局限性与适用条件。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection is widely used in transaction fraud detection where labels are scarce. Isolation Forest (IF) is among the most popular classical methods due to its scalability and ease of deployment. We propose SilIF, an augmentation of Isolation Forest that adds a silhouette-based scoring layer computed in a representation space induced by the trees of the forest. For each point, we extract a vector of per-tree path lengths, cluster these "fingerprints" into structural groups, and compute a silhouette score that measures how well the point fits its assigned group versus the nearest alternative. The silhouette signal is combined with the base IF score via a single hyperparameter alpha. On the IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection benchmark (~590K transactions, 3.5% fraud), SilIF with alpha=1.0 improves over plain Isolation Forest by +0.0080 AUC-PR on average across five seeds, with SilIF winning on all five seeds (paired t-test p=0.046). We also report results on a synthetic credit-card dataset (Sparkov) where the silhouette augmentation does not improve over plain IF, and we characterize the conditions that distinguish the two outcomes. The paper presents SilIF as a tunable, easy-to-deploy enhancement to Isolation Forest with honest reporting of when it helps and when it does not. Code at https://github.com/venkat15vk/silif-anomaly-detection.

</details>


### [204] [On the Push-Based Asynchronous Federated Learning: A Bias-Correction Aggregation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26162)
*Jiahui Bai,Hai Dong,A. K. Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PushCen-ADFL 是一种高效的异步去中心化联邦学习框架，通过质心表示空间压缩和修正偏差技术，显著提升了异构环境下的训练稳定性和通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADFL 虽然去除了中心化和同步限制，但面对大规模异构系统时，频繁的 P2P 通信、异步更新及 Non-IID 数据会导致通信开销巨大、聚合偏差和严重的模型漂移。

Method: 提出了 PushCen-ADFL 框架：1) 引入共享质心表示空间以压缩通信；2) 采用保持平均的 push-sum 混合技术来修正聚合偏差；3) 应用轻量级质心正则化以缓解模型漂移；4) 利用有界发送者去重缓冲区处理异步连接。

Result: 在视觉数据集上的实验表明，与现有算法相比，PushCen-ADFL 在数据异构性下准确率最高提升了 6%，同时将单次推送的通信成本降低了 80% 以上。

Conclusion: PushCen-ADFL 通过在共享质心空间内耦合通信、聚合与正则化，有效解决了异步去中心化联邦学习（ADFL）中的通信开销、聚合偏差及模型偏移问题，在异构环境下实现了更优的精度与通信效率平衡。

Abstract: Asynchronous decentralized federated learning (ADFL) eliminates central coordination and global synchronization, making it attractive for large-scale and heterogeneous systems. However, frequent peer-to-peer communication, asynchronous updates on directed topologies, and non-IID data jointly lead to excessive communication overhead, biased aggregation and severe model drift. We propose PushCen-ADFL, a communication-efficient ADFL framework that enables stable training under asymmetric communication and delayed client participation. PushCen-ADFL couples communication, aggregation, and local stabilization in a shared centroid representation space, forming a closed loop between compression and optimization. Clients exchange centroid-form messages, apply average-preserving push-sum mixing to correct aggregation bias, and use a lightweight centroid regularization anchored in the same centroid space to mitigate drift under heterogeneity and staleness. A bounded, sender-deduplicated buffer further improves robustness under irregular asynchronous arrivals. Experiments on vision datasets demonstrate that PushCen-ADFL improves accuracy under data heterogeneity by up to 6\% while reducing per-push communication cost by more than 80\%, achieving a favorable accuracy-communication trade-off.

</details>


### [205] [Beyond Coefficients: Forecast-Necessity Testing for Interpretable Causal Discovery in Nonlinear Time-Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18751)
*Valentina Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Michael Coppedge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Nonlinear machine-learning models are increasingly used to discover causal relationships in time-series data, yet the interpretation of their outputs remains poorly understood. In particular, causal scores produced by regularized neural autoregressive models are often treated as analogues of regression coefficients, leading to misleading claims of statistical significance.
  In this paper, we argue that causal relevance in nonlinear time-series models should be evaluated through forecast necessity rather than coefficient magnitude, and we present a practical evaluation procedure for doing so. We present an interpretable evaluation framework based on systematic edge ablation and forecast comparison, which tests whether a candidate causal relationship is required for accurate prediction. Using Neural Additive Vector Autoregression as a case study model, we apply this framework to a real-world case study of democratic development, modeled as a multivariate time series of panel data - democracy indicators across 139 countries. We show that relationships with similar causal scores can differ dramatically in their predictive necessity due to redundancy, temporal persistence, and regime-specific effects.
  Our results demonstrate how forecast-necessity testing supports more reliable causal reasoning in applied AI systems and provides practical guidance for interpreting nonlinear time-series models in high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [206] [From Privacy to Generalization: Linear Max-Information Bounds for DP-SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26222)
*Christoph H. Lampert,Hossein Zakerinia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Understanding the relationship between generalization and privacy remains a central challenge in modern machine learning theory, particularly for deep networks trained by variants of differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD). In this work we make progress on this persistent open problem by proving a finite-sample bound on the approximate max-information of DP-SGD that exhibits scaling properties comparable with (Dwork et al, 2015)'s classic result for $ε$-differentially private algorithms, namely at most linear in the dataset size. From our result we obtain a general-purpose PAC-Bayes generalization bound in which the necessary prior distribution can be learned by DP-SGD, as well as a generalization bound for DP-SGD-trained models themselves, with a complexity term that is fully explicit and controlled by the optimization hyperparameters.

</details>


### [207] [InfoQuant: Shaping Activation Distributions for Low-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26175)
*Ke Li,Dong An,Xiaoling Zang,Can Ye,Liang Xie,Qibo Qiu,Chen Shen,Xiaofei He,Wenxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 低位激活量化难度大、分布不匹配的问题，提出 InfoQuant。该方法利用信息论视角进行分布设计，通过 PSOT 变换和自适应离群点处理，在不训练的前提下显著提升了模型量化后的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前 LLM 的低位激活量化面临巨大挑战，核心问题在于激活值包含离群点且分布与均匀量化器不匹配。现有的量化方法往往过度关注抑制峰值或缩小误差，却忽略了从信息论视角设计适合量化的分布。

Method: 提出 InfoQuant 方法，包含两项核心技术：1. 峰值抑制正交变换（PSOT）：通过变换将激活分布塑造得更易于量化；2. 自适应离群点标记（Adaptive Outlier-token Selection）：增强量化过程中 PSOT 的鲁棒性。该方法为无需训练（train-free）的后训练量化方案。

Result: 在多种 LLM 系列测试中表现优于现有 PTQ 及端到端训练基准。在 W4A4KV4 配置下，平均保留了 97% 的浮点精度，并将 LLaMA-2 13B 模型的性能差距较前沿方法缩小了 42%。

Conclusion: InfoQuant 通过 PSOT 和自适应离群点选择成功优化了激活分布的量化友好性，在超低位（如 W4A4KV4）量化下实现了显著的性能提升，成为目前 LLM 后训练量化（PTQ）的先进方案。

Abstract: Low-bit activation quantization remains a major bottleneck in efficient large language model (LLM) deployment. The difficulty is not only that activations contain outliers, but that their distributions are often poorly matched to a low-bit uniform quantizer. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods suppress peaks, balance channels, or minimize reconstruction error, yet they rarely specify what activation distribution is actually easy to discretize. As a result, activations may appear numerically smoother while still incurring large quantization error because the quantization range remains wide or most values collapse into a few levels near the mean. We recast activation transformation as quantizer-facing distribution design and analyze quantization error from an information-theoretic perspective. Our analysis shows that quantization-friendly activations should jointly have a smaller numerical range and sufficient dispersion within that range. Guided by this analysis, we propose InfoQuant, a train-free method that employs Peak Suppression Orthogonal Transformation (PSOT) to shape activations into more quantization-friendly distributions. We further introduce adaptive outlier-token selection to improve the robustness of PSOT during optimization. Across multiple LLM families, InfoQuant consistently outperforms prior PTQ and end-to-end training baselines. Under W4A4KV4, it preserves 97% of floating-point accuracy on average and reduces the LLaMA-2 13B performance gap by 42% over the previous state of the art. Code is available at [https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant](https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant)

</details>


### [208] [GAC: Noise-Aware Adaptive Mixing for Hybrid SFT-RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26184)
*Yuelin Hu,Zhenbo Yu,Zhengxue Cheng,Wei Liu,Li Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种噪声感知控制器（GAC），通过动态自适应权重方案优化了监督微调与强化学习的混合后训练过程，有效提升了模型在复杂任务中的综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合后训练（监督微调与强化学习结合）通常使用固定的混合策略，无法应对训练过程中两种信号相对噪声水平的动态变化。

Method: 提出了一种名为“噪声感知控制器（GAC）”的方法，该方法通过在线估计梯度方差和监督微调与强化学习信号之间的差异，动态计算混合权重，并引入了平滑、先验引导和更新限制等机制。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，GAC的表现优于固定的或基于规则的基线方法，尤其在更大规模的模型上收益更显著，且训练开销不足1%。

Conclusion: GAC通过动态调整混合权重，能够有效优于固定的混合策略，在数学、代码、科学和逻辑推理任务中展现出更好的性能，且训练开销极低。

Abstract: Hybrid post-training usually combines supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, but fixed mixing schedules cannot adapt when the relative noise of the two signals changes over time. We propose GAC, a noise-aware controller that derives an adaptive mixing weight from online estimates of gradient variance and disagreement between the two training signals. The method adds smoothing, prior guidance, and bounded updates while reusing existing training tensors. Experiments on math, code, science, and logic benchmarks show that GAC consistently improves hybrid post-training over strong fixed and rule-based baselines, with larger gains at larger model scales and less than 1% training overhead.

</details>


### [209] [Online Learning on Hidden-Convex Losses via Algorithmic Equivalence: Optimal Regret, Geometric Barrier, and Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26373)
*Anas Barakat,Andreas Kontogiannis,Vasilis Pollatos,Ioannis Panageas,Antonios Varvitsiotis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究证实了在隐藏凸损失函数设置下，简单的在线梯度下降（OGD）能够达到与在线凸优化相同的最优 $\Theta(\sqrt{T})$ 重悔界，并给出了实现该性能所需的精确几何条件——Hessian 兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决 Ghai, Lu 和 Hazan (2022) 遗留的两个公开问题：一是隐藏凸优化在对抗环境下能否达到与在线凸优化相同步的 $\Theta(\sqrt{T})$ 最优重悔界；二是明确隐藏凸性算法等价性所需的必要几何条件。

Method: 采用了精细化的离散时间算法等价性参数（discrete-time algorithmic equivalence argument），比较了 OGD 与镜像下降（OMD）的动力学；引入了 Hessian 兼容性条件作为判定算法性能的核心几何依据；在反馈方面使用了球形平滑（spherical smoothing）技术以适配单点反馈下界。

Result: 证明了 OGD 在满足 Hessian 兼容性条件时可达到 $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ 重悔，匹配了在线凸优化的最优速率；建立了 Hessian 兼容性是保证 OGD 性能必不可少的特性，并将其推广至 Bandit 反馈，实现了 $\mathcal{O}(T^{3/4})$ 的预期重悔。

Conclusion: 在线梯度下降（OGD）可以在隐藏凸损失函数上达到最优的 $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ 重悔界。通过引入“Hessian兼容性”条件，明确了算法等价性的几何要求。若该条件不满足，OGD 将遭受线性 $\Omega(T)$ 重悔。此外，该分析推广至单点反馈设定，实现了 $\mathcal{O}(T^{3/4})$ 的预期重悔界。

Abstract: We study adversarial online learning with hidden-convex losses, i.e., nonconvex losses that become convex after a nonlinear reparameterization. Ghai, Lu and Hazan (2022) proved that, under geometric and smoothness assumptions, online gradient descent (OGD) on such nonconvex losses approximately simulates online mirror descent (OMD) on the underlying convex losses with a suitable regularizer, yielding $\mathcal{O}(T^{2/3})$ regret. They left open whether the optimal $Θ(\sqrt{T})$ regret from online convex optimization can be recovered in this hidden-convex setting. We answer this question affirmatively. More specifically, via a sharper discrete-time algorithmic equivalence argument, we prove that OGD achieves $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret under the same assumptions, matching the optimal worst-case rate for adversarial online convex optimization. We also address another open question of Ghai, Lu and Hazan (2022) by clarifying the geometry required for this algorithmic equivalence. We replace the diagonal-Jacobian sufficient condition with a necessary-and-sufficient Hessian compatibility condition, thereby expanding the class of admissible reparameterizations. We complement our tight regret bound with a lower bound showing that the Hessian compatibility assumption is essential for OGD; when it fails, we construct a smooth reparameterization and an adversarial sequence of hidden-convex losses for which OGD suffers $Ω(T)$ regret. Finally, we extend our analysis to one-point bandit feedback and prove a $\mathcal{O}(T^{3/4})$ expected regret bound for bandit OGD with spherical smoothing, matching its classical rate on convex losses.

</details>


### [210] [Max-Window Scale Estimation for Near-Lossless HiF8 W8A8 Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26189)
*Yingying Cheng,Jinquan Shi,Li Zhou,Zhiyang He,Zhaoyi Sun,Fan Zhang,Jie Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统分析了低比特LLM量化训练中的两个隐蔽失效模式（amax饱和与灾难性遗忘），并提出了通过DTS优化与预热策略相结合的解决方案，在极其微小的性能损耗下实现了高效的量化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特浮点数QAT能够显著提升LLM部署效率，但常伴随难以通过常规指标排查的微妙失效模式，本研究旨在系统地识别、拆解并解决这些失效模式。

Method: 针对OpenPangu-Embedded-1B模型，通过8组受控实验，引入延迟张量缩放（DTS）机制，对比分析了amax饱和与灾难性遗忘两种失效模式，并提出了相应的修正策略（包含历史窗口窗口策略与BF16预热）。

Result: 最终配置下，模型在MMLU、HellaSwag及ARC-Challenge上的性能下降分别仅为0.43%、0.58%和0.22%，训练损失的绝对百分比误差（APE）仅为0.11%。

Conclusion: 本研究通过采用保守的max-algorithm DTS策略（64步历史窗口）与BF16预热及小学习率结合，成功解决了低比特量化训练中的amax饱和与灾难性遗忘问题，在保持高性能的同时实现了高效的LLM部署。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) with low-bit floating-point formats enables efficient LLM deployment, yet introduces subtle failure modes invisible to standard training metrics. We present a systematic study of HiF8 W8A8 QAT for OpenPangu-Embedded-1B through the lens of Delayed Tensor Scaling (DTS). Across eight controlled experiments, we identify and disentangle two orthogonal failure modes: (i)amax saturation, where delayed scale estimates silently corrupt knowledge-sensitive representations via forward-pass clipping, and (ii)catastrophic forgetting, where an aggressive learning rate overwrites pretrained commonsense knowledge independently of quantization. Neither is detectable from training loss alone. We address amax saturation with a conservative max-algorithm DTS strategy over a 64-step history window, and mitigate forgetting via a 500-step BF16 warmup followed by QAT at lr=10^{-5}. Both fixes are necessary and sufficient: our final configuration achieves 0.43% MMLU drop, 0.58% HellaSwag drop, and 0.22% ARC-Challenge drop versus a matched BF16 baseline, with a training loss APE of only 0.11% over 10,000 steps.

</details>


### [211] [Function-Valued Causal Influence in Nonlinear Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26408)
*Valentina V. Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Michael Coppedge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对非线性因果发现，该论文指出传统的标量评分掩盖了因果效应的复杂函数特性，因此提出了一种基于函数值评估的分析框架，能更有效地捕捉时间序列模型中的状态依赖因果关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列因果发现方法过度依赖标量边缘分数（scalar edge scores），这种做法掩盖了非线性模型所学习到的关键信息——即因果效应在不同状态、量级和情境下表现出的函数依赖关系。

Method: 提出了基于加性贡献可分解架构的“函数值因果影响”形式化定义；引入了基于个体条件期望（ICE）的实用框架，并以“神经加性向量自回归”（Neural Additive Vector Autoregression）为代表架构进行实现。

Result: 实验表明，具有相同标量分数的因果边，其函数表现（如单调性、阈值效应、饱和效应或符号反转）可能完全不同；应用案例研究显示，该方法成功揭示了民主发展中被传统方法忽略的体制特定及非对称效应。

Conclusion: 标量因果分数在描述非线性因果关系时存在严重的信息瓶颈。研究证明，“函数值因果影响”比传统的标量评分更能揭示系统不同状态、机制和情境下的因果动态，是进行因果发现的更优表达方式。

Abstract: Causal discovery in time series is increasingly performed using nonlinear machine-learning models, yet the resulting causal relationships are almost always summarized by scalar edge scores. We argue that this practice obscures the true object learned by nonlinear autoregressive models: a state-dependent function whose effect varies across regimes, magnitudes, and contexts. We formalize function-valued causal influence for additive, contribution-decomposable architectures and show that scalar causal scores constitute a severe information bottleneck, conflating between-state variation with within-state residual noise. Using Neural Additive Vector Autoregression as a representative architecture, we introduce a practical framework based on Individual Conditional Expectation for estimating causal response functions directly from trained models. Through controlled synthetic experiments, we demonstrate that edges with indistinguishable scalar scores can exhibit qualitatively different functional behaviors, including monotonic, thresholded, saturating, and sign-changing effects. An applied case study on democratic development further shows that function-valued analysis reveals regime-specific and asymmetric causal structure systematically missed by score-centric approaches.

</details>


### [212] [When Does Deep RL Beat Calibrated Baselines? A Benchmark Study on Adaptive Resource Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26418)
*Guilin Zhang,Chuanyi Sun,Kai Zhao,Shahryar Sarkani,John Fossaceca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过RLScale-Bench基准测试发现，现阶段精心校准的规则基线在成本控制上普遍优于主流DRL算法，强调了评估协议与奖励工程较单纯算法调优更为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑DRL在自动资源控制中的实际价值，旨在探究在何种条件下DRL能通过有效的基准评估超越传统的基于规则的自动伸缩方案。

Method: 构建了RLScale-Bench基准测试与评估协议；在Kubernetes HPA上测试了6种主流DRL算法（PPO, DQN, A2C, SAC, TD3, DDPG）与校准规则基线的对比；覆盖6种工作负载模式，通过匹配架构、训练预算和反馈函数进行受控评估。

Result: (1) 校准后的规则控制器在所有测试的工作负载中成本最低；(2) 离散动作空间算法在约束违规表现上远优于连续动作空间算法；(3) 没有单一算法在所有工作负载下表现最优，算法排名波动剧烈。

Conclusion: 在资源自动伸缩场景中，强化学习（DRL）并未显著优于精心校准的基于规则的控制器。研究指出，行业瓶颈不在于算法选择，而在于基线校准、奖励设计及评估协议。

Abstract: A properly calibrated rule-based autoscaler can beat every one of six mainstream deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithms on cost across every workload we test - so when, if ever, does DRL actually help? We study this in RLScale-Bench, a reproducible benchmark and evaluation protocol for DRL on adaptive resource control, where an agent allocates compute to a dynamic workload under cost and service-level constraints. We evaluate PPO, DQN, A2C, SAC, TD3, and DDPG under matched architectures, training budgets, and reward functions against a calibrated rule-based baseline across six workload patterns and five seeds (240 runs), instantiate the benchmark on Kubernetes Horizontal Pod Autoscaling, and probe distribution-shift generalization. Three findings challenge common assumptions: (i) the calibrated controller achieves the lowest cost on all six workloads, though it trails the best RL agents on bursty and flash traffic; (ii) discrete-action algorithms outperform continuous-action ones by one to two orders of magnitude in constraint violations due to action-space mismatch; and (iii) no single algorithm dominates across workloads, with rankings shifting by up to four positions. The bottleneck in RL-based resource control is not algorithm selection but baseline calibration, reward engineering, and realistic evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [213] [Modeling Dynamic Mixtures of Time-Delay Systems from Streaming Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26191)
*Ren Fujiwara,Yasuko Matsubara,Yasushi Sakurai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DelayMix 是一种针对时间序列流的在线框架，通过利用马尔可夫参数构建系统张量并结合张量分解，实现了对时延系统动态变化的快速、鲁棒且内存高效的自适应预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列数据流中存在快速的环境变化（状态转移）和输入时延变动，导致模型性能下降。现有方法在处理多模式时，难以平衡预测准确性、鲁棒性与内存占用之间的权衡。

Method: 提出了一种名为“DelayMix”的在线框架。核心思路是将流式时间序列视为时延系统的动态混合：通过马尔可夫参数序列构建系统张量来表示过去的状态模式（封装了系统动态与输入输出延迟），并利用张量分解算法在环境变化时提取并选择最匹配当前模式的模型。

Result: 在真实数据集上的测试表明，DelayMix 在预测精度和对时延变化的适应速度上均优于对照方法，尤其在高度非平稳数据场景下表现稳健。

Conclusion: DelayMix 通过将动态系统建模为系统张量的混合，有效实现了对非平稳时间序列的快速自适应，在保持高预测精度的同时优化了内存占用，是处理具有时延特性的动态系统的有效方案。

Abstract: This research addresses the problem of adaptive modeling in time-series data streams with clear input-output relationships. This problem is challenging because rapid system changes (regime shifts) caused by environmental factors or input delay changes degrade model performance, and the trade-off among accuracy, robustness, and memory usage arises when using multiple small models for each time-series pattern. To address these issues, this paper presents an online framework/method that treats streaming time series as dynamic mixtures of time-delay systems. This framework maintains robustness of model tracking and reduces memory usage by summarizing past regimes using a fixed-length representation that captures both the system dynamics and input-output delays. Concretely, this approach constructs a summary system tensor using the system's Markov parameter series, capturing both dynamic behavior and delay characteristics. If necessary, a tensor decomposition algorithm extracts relevant past models from the tensor and helps select the system that best fits the current regime. This method enables rapid adaptation to environmental changes and is computationally efficient. Tests on real datasets show that DelayMix consistently outperforms other methods, achieving superior forecast accuracy and faster adaptation to delays, especially for highly non-stationary data.

</details>


### [214] [Few-shot Cross-country Generalization of Tabular Machine Learning and Foundation Models for Childhood Anemia Prediction under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26589)
*Yusuf Brima,Marcellin Atemkeng,Lansana Hassim Kallon,David Niyukuri,Antoine Vacavant,Samuel Saidu,Ding-Geng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了Transformer架构模型（TabPFN）在预测全球儿童贫血方面的表现，发现在数据稀缺情境下其优于传统方法，但预测能力主要取决于人口群体间的异质性而非模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球约40%的6-59个月儿童患有贫血，成因复杂且具有高度异质性，导致现有模型的泛化能力受限；研究旨在评估基于Transformer的表格基础模型在跨国和数据稀缺场景下相较于经典统计/机器学习方法的表现。

Method: 研究使用来自全球16个国家的DHS人口健康调查数据（n=68,856），对比了逻辑回归、XGBoost、LightGBM及Transformer架构的表格基础模型TabPFN v2.6。通过AUC-ROC、Brier分数和ECE进行评估，并采用了LOCO（留一国家交叉验证）、反向LOCO及少样本学习进行泛化能力测试，同时使用SHAP进行特征重要性分析。

Result: TabPFN在少样本（<200个样本）环境下优于经典模型，且在全量数据下表现出最佳的校准性能（最低Brier和ECE分数）。AUC-ROC范围在0.59-0.76之间，模型间差异较小。研究发现预测性能主要受地理/人口背景影响，SHAP分析明确了儿童年龄、海拔和身高Z评分是核心预测因子。

Conclusion: 在儿童贫血预测中，模型表现更多受人口变异影响而非模型架构本身。TabPFN在数据稀缺场景下具有显著的鉴别力和校准优势，证明了基础模型在数据受限的全球健康预测中的潜力。

Abstract: Childhood anemia affects around 40% of children aged 6-59 months globally and arises from heterogeneous factors, limiting model generalizability. We evaluate a transformer-based tabular foundation model against classical supervised methods under cross-country and data-scarce settings.
  We used DHS data from 16 countries across Africa, Asia, Latin America, the Caucasus, and the Middle East (n=68,856). We compared Logistic Regression, XGBoost, LightGBM, and TabPFN v2.6. Performance was assessed using AUC-ROC, Brier score, and ECE. Generalization was evaluated using leave-one-country-out (LOCO), reverse-LOCO, and few-shot settings. Subgroup analyses included sex, age, residence, maternal education, and wealth. Feature importance was estimated using SHAP.
  TabPFN outperformed classical models in low-data regimes (<200 samples), showing higher discrimination and better calibration. Across countries, it achieved the lowest Brier score (0.042) and ECE (0.203). Under full-data settings, AUC-ROC ranged from 0.59-0.76 with small between-model differences ($\leq 0.05$). LOCO performance was stable (0.58-0.69), driven by country context. Reverse-LOCO showed asymmetric transferability. Subgroup performance was consistent with no systematic demographic bias. SHAP identified child age, altitude, and height-for-age z-score as dominant predictors, followed by wealth and maternal education.
  Performance in childhood anemia prediction is driven more by population variation than model choice. TabPFN provides advantages in low-resource settings through improved discrimination and calibration, highlighting foundation models as promising tools for data-scarce global health prediction.

</details>


### [215] [Co-folding model guided by structural proteomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26192)
*Alon Shtrikman,Nitzan Simchi,Michal Ran Shchory,Sagie Brodsky,Eran Seger,Kirill Pevzner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIMS-Fold 是一种新型引导扩散框架，通过将 XL-MS 和 HDX-MS 实验数据引入蛋白质结构生成过程，显著提高了对复杂动态蛋白质构象（如诱导邻近药物靶点）的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式模型难以准确预测蛋白质复合物的构象，特别是对于抗体、PROTACs 等诱导邻近（induced proximity）药物设计至关重要的动态构象，且缺乏将实验性蛋白质组学数据（如 XL-MS、HDX-MS）整合到此类模型中的有效方法。

Method: 提出了 AIMS-Fold，这是一个推理阶段（inference-time）的引导扩散框架。该框架利用可微物理势，将交联质谱（XL-MS）的空间约束和氢氘交换质谱（HDX-MS）的溶剂可及性数据集成到预训练蛋白质生成模型的采样过程中，动态调整构象采样轨迹。

Result: 实验结果表明，XL-MS 和 HDX-MS 数据能显著增强模型预测能力，且两者结合具有协同效应；AIMS-Fold 在处理挑战性构象目标的结构预测上，性能优于当前最先进的无引导计算模型（如 Boltz-2）。

Conclusion: AIMS-Fold 通过集成实验质谱数据，有效弥补了纯计算模型在处理诱导邻近复杂结构时的不足，证明了实验引导的生成式建模在结构辅助药物设计中的实用价值。

Abstract: Protein structure generative models excel at predicting single protein static structures from sequence, but routinely fail to capture the correct conformational state of protein complexes, critical for protein design and induced proximity modalities such as antibodies and PROTACs. While structural proteomics techniques like Cross-Linking Mass Spectrometry (XL-MS) and Hydrogen-Deuterium Exchange (HDX-MS) offer valuable spatial and dynamic insights, integrating these sparse, heterogeneous measurements into these models remains an open challenge. Here, we bridge this gap by combining structural proteomics data with the rich biophysical priors learned by pretrained diffusion models. We introduce AIMS-Fold, an inference-time guided-diffusion framework that actively steers the generative sampling trajectory using differentiable physical potentials derived from XL-MS spatial restraints and HDX-MS solvent accessibility profiles. We demonstrate that these structural methods individually enhance predictive accuracy, and their integration yields synergistic improvement. Crucially, by leveraging these experimental restraints, AIMS-Fold achieves higher accuracy on challenging induced proximity targets than purely computational, unguided state-of-the-art models like Boltz-2. This establishes our framework as a powerful, integrative computational approach for the structure based drug design of induced proximity drugs. Evaluation code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [216] [More Expressive Feedforward Layers: Part I. Token-Adaptive Mixing of Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26647)
*Mingze Wang,Jinbo Wang,Yikuan Xia,Kai Shen,Shu Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MoA（Mixture of Activations），通过允许FFN层根据输入动态混合多种激活函数，在极小开销下提升了Transformer模型的表达能力与训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Transformer FFN层大多使用单一固定的激活函数（如ReLU、GELU或SwiGLU），缺乏针对不同Token的自适应非线性表达能力。

Method: 1. 提出混合激活（MoA）：通过轻量级输入依赖门控混合激活函数字典；2. 提出可学习激活（LA）：通过输入无关的方式对激活函数进行线性组合；3. 理论分析：证明了固定激活、LA与MoA在有限宽度下的表达能力存在严格包含关系（MoA > LA > 固定激活）。

Result: 在0.12B至2B参数规模的模型（包括稠密模型和MoE模型）上进行的预训练实验表明，MoA始终能实现更低的最终损失，展现出更优的缩放行为（scaling behavior），且计算和参数开销极低。

Conclusion: MoA是一种简单且高效的机制，通过Token自适应的激活函数混合，显著增强了LLM中FFN层的表达能力，且仅带来极小的额外开销。

Abstract: Feedforward network (FFN) layers account for a large fraction of parameters and nonlinear expressivity in Transformer-based large language models (LLMs). Despite the evolution from ReLU and GELU to gated variants such as SwiGLU, most FFN designs still use a single fixed activation function, applying the same nonlinear transformation to all tokens. In this work, we propose Mixture of Activations (MoA), a token-adaptive FFN design that mixes a dictionary of activation functions using lightweight input-dependent gates while sharing the same linear projections. As an input-independent counterpart, we also introduce learnable activations (LA), which form linear combinations of activation functions for both ReLU-type and SwiGLU-type FFNs. Theoretically, we establish strict finite-width expressive separations among fixed-activation FFNs, LA, and MoA: LA strictly contains fixed-activation FFNs, while MoA strictly contains LA, with the additional expressivity arising from input-dependent nonlinear hybridization. Empirically, we evaluate MoA through extensive pre-training experiments on dense and MoE language models ranging from 0.12B to 2B parameters under different token budgets, optimizers, and learning rate schedules. MoA consistently achieves lower terminal loss and exhibits more favorable scaling behavior than well-tuned baselines, with minimal parameter and computational overhead. These results suggest that token-adaptive activation mixing is a simple and effective mechanism for improving FFN expressivity in LLMs.

</details>


### [217] [ReMoE: Boosting Expert Reuse through Router Fine-Tuning in Memory-Constrained MoE LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27081)
*Xiongwei Zhu,Xiaojian Liao,Tianyang Jiang,Yusen Zhang,Liang Wang,Limin Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReMoE 是一种通过微调 MoE 路由器以增强专家重用性的框架，通过提升专家访存的时间局部性，显著降低了内存受限场景下的 I/O 开销，实现了推理性能的大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型在内存受限场景下，专家频繁的置换（Eviction）和从外部存储（如 UFS）读取导致严重的 I/O 开销，成为推理性能的瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种路由器微调框架 ReMoE，通过偏置路由决策，使得连续的 Token 更倾向于选择相同的专家（提高时间局部性），从而契合缓存的替换策略，减少从外部存储读取专家的频率。

Result: 在 DeepSeek 和 Qwen 模型上实验显示，专家重用率提升了 26%；在 Jetson Orin NX 上使解码速度提升了 1.77-1.99 倍，TPOT 降低了 43.6-49.8%，且不影响模型的下游任务性能。

Conclusion: ReMoE 通过在微调过程中引导路由器优化专家缓存的局部性，无需额外推理开销即可有效解决 MoE 模型在内存受限设备上的 I/O 瓶颈，显著提升了推理吞吐量和降低了延迟。

Abstract: Fine-grained Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models sparsely activate only a subset of experts per token, reducing activated computation while maintaining high model capacity. However, in memory-constrained inference scenarios, only a small set of experts can be cached. Experts not in the cache must be fetched from slow external storage (e.g., UFS), leading to frequent evictions and substantial I/O overhead.
  We propose ReMoE, a router fine-tuning framework designed to boost token-wise expert reuse. ReMoE biases the router toward recently selected experts, producing temporally stable routing that better matches cache locality constraints. By increasing short-horizon expert reuse, ReMoE reduces expert fetches from storage without adding inference-time computation.
  Experiments on DeepSeek and Qwen models show that ReMoE improves expert reuse by 26% while maintaining downstream task performance. Real-system evaluations further confirm these benefits, improving output throughput by 8.4% under vLLM GPU-CPU expert offloading and reducing TPOT by 43.6-49.8% under llama.cpp on Jetson Orin NX, corresponding to a 1.77-1.99$\times$ decode speedup across diverse workloads. Checkpoints and usage instructions are available at https://github.com/BUAA-OSCAR/ReMoE.

</details>


### [218] [Bridging Classification and Reconstruction: Cooperative Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26193)
*Qideng Tang,Dai Chaofan,Wubin Ma,Yahui Wu,Haohao Zhou,Tao Zhang,Huan Li,Dalin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CoAD 框架，通过协同分类与重构两种深度学习范式，解决了现有 TSAD 方法在细微异常检测、泛化能力及掩码不对齐方面的局限性，在实现高精度的同时保持了优异的运行速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于深度学习的 TSAD 方法在检测细微和持续性异常时表现不佳。现有的 OE 方法泛化能力差，而 MAE 方法存在掩码（masking）不对齐问题，亟需一种能结合二者优势并规避各自缺陷的统一框架。

Method: 提出 CoAD 框架，通过协同机制统一了“异常暴露 (OE)”和“掩码自编码器 (MAE)”范式：由分类模块生成概率感知掩码引导重构模块，重构模块反过来辅助分类模块提高泛化能力；同时优化了分类粒度和频率信息利用。

Result: 在多个高质量基准数据集上超越了现有的 SOTA 深度学习及传统数据挖掘方法；在检测细微/复杂异常方面效果显著，且具备轻量化与高计算效率的特性。

Conclusion: CoAD 成功融合了分类与重构两种范式，证明了深度学习在时间序列异常检测（TSAD）中的有效性。该框架不仅提升了检测精度（尤其是针对细微和复杂异常的表现），还保持了高效的推理速度，极具工业应用价值。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) has long been a hot research topic in data mining due to its various applications. Recent studies challenge the effectiveness of popular deep learning methods for TSAD, suggesting their failure in detecting subtle and prolonged anomalies. Outlier Exposure (OE) and Masked Autoencoder (MAE) emerge as two promising paradigms (classification and reconstruction) for solving the above problems. However, OE-based methods are constrained by poor generalization, while MAE-based methods are limited by masking misalignment issues. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel framework, CoAD, which unifies the two paradigms to leverage their complementary strengths while mitigating their respective weaknesses. In this framework, the classification module generates probability-informed soft masks for the reconstruction module, which in turn alleviates the generalization problem of the classification module. This cooperative design enables CoAD to effectively detect subtle and complex anomalies that are often overlooked by existing methods. Additionally, the classification module is carefully designed to resolve issues related to improper classification granularity and the neglect of frequency information. Extensive experiments on high-quality benchmark datasets, conducted under rigorous evaluation protocols, demonstrate that CoAD significantly outperforms both state-of-the-art deep learning and traditional data mining methods, highlighting the potential of deep learning in TSAD. Moreover, CoAD is lightweight and substantially faster than existing SOTA methods, demonstrating its practical value for large-scale, real-time applications.

</details>


### [219] [Bilevel Optimization over Saddle Points of Zero-Sum Markov Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26654)
*Zihao Zheng,Irwin King,Songtao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决具有竞争性下层环境的双层强化学习问题，作者提出了 PANDA 算法，该算法通过 Nikaido-Isoda 函数实现首阶优化，不仅具备理论收敛保障，且在效率和性能上均优于现有的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层强化学习方法多局限于单策略下层环境，无法处理如激励设计中常见的竞争性结构（多策略交互）。需要一种能处理下层为零和马尔可夫博弈的双层优化框架。

Method: 提出了一种基于惩罚函数的首阶策略梯度方法 PANDA（Penalty-augmented Nikaido-Isoda Descent-Ascent），该方法利用 Nikaido-Isoda 函数来处理下层的 min-max 博弈结构，避开了计算上层超梯度的需求。

Result: 理论证明 PANDA 在不依赖凸性假设的前提下收敛至平稳点，收敛率为 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1})$，样本复杂度为 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3})$，并与现有单策略下层双层强化学习的最优速率持平；实验验证了其优于基准方法。

Conclusion: PANDA 是一种有效的首阶双层强化学习算法，能在不需要二阶信息或显式超梯度计算的情况下，解决下层为零和马尔可夫博弈的双层优化问题，并达到理论最优的收敛速率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) often has a hierarchical structure, where an upper-level (UL) learner selects model parameters and a lower-level (LL) decision-making process responds, naturally leading to a bilevel optimization problem. Most existing bilevel RL methods assume a single-policy LL Markov decision process (MDP), and therefore fail to capture competitive structures arising in applications such as incentive design, where multiple policies interact. We study bilevel optimization problems in which the LL problem is a regularized min-max zero-sum Markov game and the UL objective is optimized through the saddle-point equilibrium induced by the LL game. In this work, we propose penalty-augmented Nikaido-Isoda descent-ascent (PANDA), a penalty-based first-order policy-gradient method based on the Nikaido-Isoda function. By exploiting the min-max game structure, PANDA avoids computing UL hypergradients and does not require second-order information. We prove that PANDA converges to stationary points without convexity assumptions on either the UL or LL objectives. Moreover, PANDA reaches an $ε$-stationary point in $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1})$ iterations with sample complexity $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3})$, matching the best-known rates for bilevel RL with single-policy LL MDPs. Experiments demonstrate the superior performance of PANDA over closely related baselines.

</details>


### [220] [On the Role of Inductive Bias in Time-Series Pretraining: A Case Study in Learning Generalizable Representations for Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26194)
*Sharmita Dey,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 PathoFM，用于解决临床时间序列学习在小样本和跨任务泛化上的挑战。实验证明，结合局部重构、时间连续性和上下文条件的动态预训练目标，比单一的对比或重构目标具有更好的临床任务迁移表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间序列学习常受限于小样本、异构队列和协议漂移。虽然基础模型预训练很吸引人，但尚不明确应该施加何种归纳偏差，才能使表征有效地迁移到分类（如病理诊断）和回归（如时间预测）等不同任务中。

Method: 提出 PathoFM（一个以编码器为中心的 Transformer 模型），并在脊髓损伤（SCI）病理步态分析任务上进行了验证。该模型采用了三种互补的预训练目标：1. 局部补全（Local Completion，重构被遮蔽的连续片段以强化局部结构）；2. 时间连续性（Temporal Continuity，预测基于观测前缀的未来片段以强化平滑性和因果一致性）；3. 无监督上下文动态（Unsupervised In-Context Dynamics，基于受试者示例窗口进行支持-查询重构）。

Result: 研究发现，对比学习类目标虽然有利于区分任务，但可能损害回归所需的量级保真度；纯重构类目标能保持波形结构，但在分类任务上表现较弱。动态导向的混合模型在分类和回归任务之间取得了最佳的迁移平衡。

Conclusion: 动态特性（Dynamics-centric）的预训练混合目标能够实现最佳的迁移效果平衡。将局部重构与时间连续性结合，并在可能的情况下引入上下文调节，能够产生具有鲁棒性和良好受试者泛化能力的表征。

Abstract: Clinical time-series learning is routinely constrained by small, heterogeneous cohorts and protocol drift, while its downstream use spans both classification (e.g., pathology diagnosis) and regression (e.g., temporal forecasting). These constraints make foundation-model pretraining appealing, but raises an important question of which inductive biases should the pretraining objective impose so that representations transfer across task types and subjects. We study this question in pathological gait analysis for spinal cord injury (SCI) via PathoFM, an encoder-centric transformer pretrained on multivariate gait windows with three complementary objectives: Local Completion (reconstruct contiguous masked spans to enforce local structure), Temporal Continuity (predict a masked mid-horizon continuation from an observed prefix to enforce smoothness and causal consistency), and Unsupervised In-Context Dynamics (support-query reconstruction conditioned on subject exemplar windows via attention). Empirically comparing objective families (grouping/contrastive, dynamics-based, and generative reconstruction), we find that dynamics-centric mixtures produce the most balanced transfer: grouping objectives favor discriminative margins but can degrade magnitude fidelity needed for continuous targets, whereas reconstruction-only objectives preserve waveform structure but may underperform on classification. Overall, combining local reconstruction with temporal continuity, and adding in-context conditioning when exemplar access is realistic, yields robust subject-generalizing representations.

</details>


### [221] [Negligible in Size, Significant in Effect: On Scale Vectors in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26895)
*Mingze Wang,Shuchen Zhu,Yuxin Fang,Binghui Li,Kai Shen,Shu Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文深入解析了大模型中归一化层缩放向量的优化机制，发现其并非通过提升表达能力，而是通过改善优化过程发挥作用，并据此提出了一系列高效的改进策略以提升模型训练表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大模型中归一化层的缩放向量（scale vector）被广泛使用，但其在模型表达能力、优化动力学等方面的作用尚缺乏系统性理解，且其设计往往具有随机性。

Method: 1. 通过理论分析（Pre-Norm架构下对表达能力与优化过程的影响）及实证研究揭示缩放向量的作用；2. 区分Input-Norm与Output-Norm并分析权重衰减（Weight Decay）的差异化影响；3. 提出三项改进：分支特异性异构化（branch-specific heterogeneity）、调整缩放向量在线性映射周围的放置位置、以及量级-方向重参数化（magnitude-direction reparameterization）。

Result: 研究表明，缩放向量主要通过“自放大预处理效应”（self-amplifying preconditioning）改善优化过程；权重衰减对不同类型的归一化层有截然不同的影响；所提出的统一改进策略在0.12B至2B参数规模的模型（含MoE）上均实现了比基线更低的训练损失。

Conclusion: 通过对缩放向量的深入分析，本文提出了一套统一的优化策略，能够在几乎不增加参数量和计算开销的前提下，显著改善大模型的训练损失和扩展性能。这证明了缩放向量在深度学习架构中的关键作用及优化潜力。

Abstract: Normalization layers in modern large language models (LLMs) consist of a deterministic normalization operation and a learnable scale vector. While the normalization operation has been extensively studied, the scale vector remains poorly understood despite its ubiquitous use. In this work, we present a systematic study of scale vectors in LLMs from the perspectives of expressivity, optimization, and architectural structure. First, we show empirically that although scale vectors constitute only a negligible fraction of model parameters, removing them substantially degrades LLM pre-training. Our theory further shows that, in Pre-Norm architectures, scale vectors do not increase expressivity; instead, they improve optimization through a self-amplifying preconditioning effect on subsequent linear mappings. Second, we investigate the role of weight decay for scale vectors. By distinguishing Input-Norm and Output-Norm layers, we theoretically show that weight decay is beneficial for the former but harmful for the latter, due to their distinct roles in optimization and expressivity. Third, motivated by this understanding, we propose three lightweight and complementary improvements to scale vectors: branch-specific heterogeneity, improved placement around linear mappings, and magnitude-direction reparameterization. Both theory and experiments show that each improvement yields consistent gains. Finally, we combine these improvements into a unified scale-vector strategy and evaluate it through extensive LLM pre-training experiments on dense and mixture-of-experts models ranging from 0.12B to 2B parameters, across multiple optimizers and learning rate schedules, under industrial-scale token budgets. The unified strategy consistently achieves lower terminal loss than well-tuned baselines and exhibits more favorable scaling behavior, while adding negligible parameter and computational overhead.

</details>


### [222] [Agile Online Model Selection: Resolving Adaptation Lag via Safeguarded Large Learning Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26919)
*Kei Takemura,Ryuta Matsuno,Keita Sakuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对在线学习在非平稳环境下的适应滞后问题，本文提出了一种基于事后惩罚机制的大学习率优化算法，有效平衡了鲁棒性与敏捷性，实现了快速适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无调优在线模型选择算法在非平稳环境中存在鲁棒性与敏捷性的权衡。为了保证动态遗憾界（dynamic regret bounds），传统算法通常限制学习率为常数（如 O(1)），这导致在遭遇突发分布偏移时产生巨大的适应滞后（adaptation lag）。

Method: 提出了一种乐观在线镜像下降算法，核心创新是一个“事后惩罚机制”（post-hoc penalty mechanism）。该机制能够动态监控不稳定的更新，排除产生过高遗憾的学习率，从而允许在算法中使用高达 Θ(T) 的大学习率，而无需预设严苛的约束。

Result: 理论上，该算法的累积惩罚保持在 O(log T)，在最差情况下实现了近乎最优的保证，并在良性情况下表现出更优的收敛速率；实证结果显示，相较于基准算法，该方法将适应滞后从数百轮降低至数轮。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新型乐观在线镜像下降算法（Optimistic Online Mirror Descent），通过事后惩罚机制打破了鲁棒性与敏捷性之间的权衡，实现了在非平稳环境下的快速适应，同时保持了近乎最优的动态遗憾界。

Abstract: Maintaining predictive accuracy in non-stationary environments requires online model selection to adapt autonomously to unknown distribution shifts. However, existing tuning-free algorithms face a fundamental trade-off between robustness and agility. Specifically, to ensure dynamic regret bounds, they must restrict learning rates to small constants (e.g., $O(1)$). This restriction inevitably causes significant adaptation lag during abrupt changes. To resolve this, we propose a novel optimistic online mirror descent that utilizes safeguarded large learning rates up to $Θ(T)$, where $T$ is the number of rounds. Our key technical contribution is a post-hoc penalty mechanism that dynamically monitors unstable updates and excludes learning rates incurring excessive regret, eliminating the need for restrictive a priori constraints. We show that the cumulative penalty remains $O(\log T)$, allowing our algorithm to match near-optimal worst-case guarantees while achieving superior rates in benign cases. Empirical evaluations on synthetic and eleven diverse real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach reduces the adaptation lag from hundreds of rounds to a few rounds, consistently outperforming tuning-free baselines.

</details>


### [223] [The Bridge-Garden Dilemma in LLM Distillation: Why Mixing Hard and Soft Labels Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26246)
*Guanghui Wang,Kaiwen Lv Kacuila,Zhiyong Yang,Zitai Wang,Jin-Wen Wu,Longtao Huang,Qianqian Xu,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过提出“桥-花园（Bridge-Garden）”理论解释了为何混合硬/软标签蒸馏优于单一类型，并据此设计了一种高效的知识蒸馏策略，在大幅降低训练成本的同时显著提升了模型的推理与编码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出尽管软标签包含更丰富的信息，但在语言模型知识蒸馏中混合硬/软标签往往效果更好；研究旨在揭示这种现象背后的机理，并解决训练与推理分布不匹配导致的“暴露偏差”问题。

Method: 引入“桥-花园分解（Bridge-Garden Decomposition）”理论，将生成步骤分类为需要精确匹配的 Bridge 和允许灵活性的 Garden；据此开发了一系列自适应平衡硬标签与软标签的混合监督方法。

Result: 在七组主流大模型（如 Qwen, Llama, Gemma, DeepSeek）及推理与代码基准测试中，该方法表现优于现有的基于发散度（divergence-based）和策略上（on-policy）的蒸馏基线，且训练成本降低了 9.7 倍。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 Bridge-Garden 混合监督方法通过自适应平衡硬标签（利于处理需精确匹配的 Bridge 步骤）和软标签（利于保持多样性的 Garden 步骤），有效缓解了语言模型训练中的暴露偏差（exposure bias），在提升性能的同时显著降低了训练成本。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers knowledge from a large teacher model to a smaller student. In language modeling, the student is trained either on tokens sampled from the teacher (hard labels) or the teacher's full next-token distribution (soft labels). Despite soft labels appear strictly richer, we find that mixing hard and soft labels consistently yields better results. Crucially, we show that this gain cannot be explained by closer teacher matching during training. Instead, it comes from reduced exposure bias, the mismatch between training and inference distributions. To explain this phenomenon, we introduce the Bridge-Garden Decomposition theory, which categorizes generation steps into two types: Bridges, where the next token must be exact, and Gardens, where it can be flexible. We show that hard-only KD excels in Bridges by avoiding risky deviations, while soft-only KD preserves diversity in Gardens. A hybrid strategy handles both cases and, as a result, reduces exposure bias across the sequence. Guided by this theory, we develop a family of Bridge-Garden hybrid supervision methods that adaptively balance hard and soft labels. Across a primary suite of seven teacher-student pairs (including Qwen, Llama, Gemma, and DeepSeek) and benchmarks in reasoning and coding, our approach outperforms divergence-based and on-policy KD baselines while reducing training cost by 9.7x, enabling efficient model compression. Code is available at https://github.com/ghwang-s/bridge_garden_hybrid_kd_release.

</details>


### [224] [Unified Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26248)
*Ethan Caballero,Priyank Jaini,David Krueger,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“统一神经缩放定律（UNSL）”，能同时精准建模并预测多维度（参数、数据、计算量等）变化对各类深度学习任务性能的影响，其外推准确性优于现有研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的缩放定律通常针对单一变量或有限维度，难以准确描述在复杂场景下（多变数同时调整）大规模模型在不同视觉、语言、数学及强化学习任务中的性能缩放行为。

Method: 提出了一种新的函数形式——统一神经缩放定律（UNSL），该公式能够同时对模型参数量、数据规模、训练步数、推理步数、计算量及超参数等多个维度进行建模和外推。

Result: UNSL在涵盖视觉、语言、数学和强化学习等多种上游及下游任务的实验中，展现出显著优于现有缩放定律函数形式的预测精度和外推能力。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的“统一神经缩放定律（UNSL）”在处理多维变量同时变化时，比现有的缩放定律具有更高的外推准确性，为理解大规模模型在不同任务和架构下的性能表现提供了更强的预测工具。

Abstract: We present a functional form (that we refer to as a Unified Neural Scaling Law (UNSL)) that accurately models and extrapolates the scaling behaviors of deep neural networks as multiple dimensions all vary simultaneously (i.e. how the evaluation metric of interest varies as one simultaneously varies the number of model parameters, training dataset size, number of training steps, number of inference steps, amount of compute, and various hyperparameters) for various architectures and for each of various tasks within a varied set of upstream and downstream tasks. This set includes large-scale vision, language, math, and reinforcement learning. When compared to other functional forms for neural scaling, this functional form yields extrapolations of scaling behavior that are considerably more accurate on this set.

</details>


### [225] [Quantized Keys Steal Attention: Bias Correction for KV-Cache Compression in Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26266)
*Tuna Tuncer,Felix Becker,Thomas Pfeil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对视频扩散模型 KV 缓存量化导致的精度损失，揭示并定义了 Jensen 偏差现象，并提出了一种零额外显存开销的数学修正方案，有效提升了低位宽量化下的视频生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块级自回归视频扩散模型中，KV 缓存随视频长度增加而占用大量显存。现有的低位宽量化方案虽然能降低显存需求，但会因 Jensen 偏差导致注意力机制性能严重退化，影响视频生成质量。

Method: 提出了一种针对注意力机制的 Jensen 偏差（Jensen bias）数学修正方法。该方法通过二阶泰勒展开，利用缓存 Key 的量化步长和 Query 的范数，在运行时实时修正注意力分数，从而抵消因量化噪声导致的注意力权重偏差。

Result: 在 MAGI-1、SkyReels-V2 和 HY-WorldPlay 等模型上的评估表明，该方法在 INT2 量化下性能显著提升，质量几乎与 BF16 持平，并优于 INT4 量化，同时进一步节省了 50% 的显存。

Conclusion: 通过引入 Jensen 偏差修正技术，可以在不增加额外显存占用的前提下，显著缓解 KV 缓存量化带来的视频质量下降问题，实现了在极低位宽（INT2）下接近 BF16 的生成效果。
